```
1→/**
     2→ * ShotImagesEditor - Main editor for shot images in travel-between-images tool.
     3→ *
     4→ * Architecture:
     5→ * - This component is a thin orchestrator that:
     6→ *   1. Coordinates data hooks
     7→ *   2. Dispatches to TimelineModeContent or BatchModeContent
     8→ *   3. Renders shared UI (header, dialogs, overlays)
     9→ *
    10→ * Hooks (in ./ShotImagesEditor/hooks/):
    11→ * - usePairData: Pure computation of pair data from images
    12→ * - useFrameCountUpdater: Timeline frame count updates with compression
    13→ * - useSegmentSlotMode: Segment editor state and SegmentSlotModeData
    14→ * - useShotGenerationsData: Data management and variant badges
    15→ * - useLightboxTransition: Overlay transitions between lightboxes
    16→ * - usePreviewSegments: Preview dialog segments
    17→ * - useDownloadImages: Zip download functionality
    18→ * - useSmoothContinuations: Timeline gap compaction
    19→ *
    20→ * Components (in ./ShotImagesEditor/components/):
    21→ * - BatchModeContent: ShotImageManager + BatchGuidanceVideo
    22→ * - PreviewTogetherDialog: Video preview dialog
    23→ */

   281→  const handleDeleteSegment = useCallback(async (generationId: string) => {

   294→      const pairShotGenId = beforeData.pair_shot_generation_id ||

   297→
   298→      let idsToDelete = [generationId];

   303→          .eq('parent_generation_id', beforeData.parent_generation_id);
   304→
   305→        idsToDelete = (siblings || [])

   312→            return childPairId === pairShotGenId;
   313→          })
   314→          .map(child => child.id);
   315→      }
   316→
   317→      await supabase.from('generations').delete().in('id', idsToDelete);

   319→      queryClient.setQueriesData(
   320→        { predicate: (query) => query.queryKey[0] === 'segment-child-generations' },
   321→        (oldData: unknown) => {

   323→            return oldData.filter((item: { id: string }) => !idsToDelete.includes(item.id));
   324→          }
   325→          return oldData;
   326→        }
   327→      );
   328→      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({
   329→        predicate: (query) => query.queryKey[0] === 'segment-child-generations',
   330→        refetchType: 'all'

   332→      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED] });

   557→                  onClearPendingImageToOpen={handleClearPendingImageToOpen}
   558→                  navigateWithTransition={navigateWithTransition}
   559→                  onNewShotFromSelection={onNewShotFromSelection}
   560→                  onRegisterTrailingUpdater={registerTrailingUpdater}
   561→                />
   562→
   563→                {unpositionedGenerationsCount > 0 && (
   564→                  <div className="mt-4">
   565→                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-muted/50 rounded-lg border border-dashed">
   566→                      <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
   567→                        {unpositionedGenerationsCount} unpositioned generation{unpositionedGenerationsCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
   568→                      </div>
   569→                      <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={onOpenUnpositionedPane} className="text-xs">
   570→                        View & Position
   571→                      </Button>
   572→                    </div>
   573→                  </div>
   574→                )}
   575→              </>
   576→            ) : (
   577→              <BatchModeContent
   578→                selectedShotId={selectedShotId}
   579→                projectId={projectId}
   580→                readOnly={readOnly}
   581→                isMobile={isMobile}
   582→                generationMode={generationMode}
   583→                images={imagesWithBadges}
   584→                pairPrompts={pairPrompts}
   585→                columns={columns}
   586→                batchVideoFrames={batchVideoFrames}
   587→                onImageReorder={handleReorder}
   588→                onImageDelete={handleDelete}
   589→                onBatchImageDelete={onBatchImageDelete}
   590→                onImageDuplicate={onImageDuplicate}
   591→                duplicatingImageId={duplicatingImageId}
   592→                duplicateSuccessImageId={duplicateSuccessImageId}
   593→                onFileDrop={onBatchFileDrop}
   594→                onGenerationDrop={onBatchGenerationDrop}
   595→                defaultPrompt={defaultPrompt}
   596→                defaultNegativePrompt={defaultNegativePrompt}
   597→                onClearEnhancedPrompt={handleClearEnhancedPromptByIndex}
   598→                onDragStateChange={handleDragStateChange}
   599→                onPairClick={handlePairClick}
   600→                onSelectionChange={onSelectionChange}
   601→                onImageUpload={onImageUpload}
   602→                isUploadingImage={isUploadingImage}
   603→                allShots={allShots}
   604→                onShotChange={onShotChange}
   605→                onAddToShot={onAddToShot ? handleAddToShotAdapter : undefined}
   606→                onAddToShotWithoutPosition={onAddToShotWithoutPosition ? handleAddToShotWithoutPositionAdapter : undefined}
   607→                onCreateShot={onCreateShot ? handleCreateShotAdapter : undefined}
   608→                onNewShotFromSelection={onNewShotFromSelection}
   609→                segmentSlots={segmentSlots}
   610→                onSegmentDelete={handleDeleteSegment}
   611→                deletingSegmentId={deletingSegmentId}
   612→                pendingImageToOpen={pendingImageToOpen}
   613→                pendingImageVariantId={pendingImageVariantId}
   614→                onClearPendingImageToOpen={handleClearPendingImageToOpen}
   615→                navigateWithTransition={navigateWithTransition}
   616→                projectAspectRatio={projectAspectRatio}
   617→                structureVideoPath={structureVideoPath}
   618→                structureVideoMetadata={structureVideoMetadata}
   619→                structureVideoTreatment={structureVideoTreatment}
   620→                structureVideoMotionStrength={structureVideoMotionStrength}
   621→                structureVideoType={structureVideoType}
   622→                uni3cEndPercent={uni3cEndPercent}
   623→                onStructureVideoChange={onStructureVideoChange}
   624→                onUni3cEndPercentChange={onUni3cEndPercentChange}
   625→                unpositionedGenerationsCount={unpositionedGenerationsCount}
   626→                onOpenUnpositionedPane={onOpenUnpositionedPane}
   627→              />
   628→            )}
   629→          </div>
   630→        )}
   631→      </CardContent>
   632→
   633→      {/* ================================================================== */}
   634→      {/* OVERLAYS & DIALOGS                                                 */}
   635→      {/* ================================================================== */}
   636→
   637→      {/* Transition overlay - starts visible when opening an image from external navigation (e.g. TasksPane) */}
   638→      <div
   639→        ref={transitionOverlayRef}
   640→        className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black pointer-events-none"
   641→        aria-hidden="true"
   642→        style={pendingImageToOpen ? { opacity: 1, display: 'block' } : { opacity: 0, display: 'none' }}
   643→      />
   644→
   645→      {/* Segment Slot Editor */}
   646→      {segmentSlotModeData && (
   647→        segmentSlotModeData.segmentVideo ? (
   648→          <MediaLightbox
   649→            media={segmentSlotModeData.segmentVideo}
   650→            segmentSlotMode={segmentSlotModeData}
   651→            onClose={() => setSegmentSlotLightboxIndex(null)}
   652→            shotId={selectedShotId}
   653→            readOnly={readOnly}
   654→            fetchVariantsForSelf
   655→          />
   656→        ) : (
   657→          <SegmentEditorModal
   658→            segmentSlotMode={segmentSlotModeData}
   659→            onClose={() => setSegmentSlotLightboxIndex(null)}
   660→            readOnly={readOnly}
   661→          />
   662→        )
   663→      )}
   664→
   665→      {/* Preview Together Dialog */}
   666→      <PreviewTogetherDialog
   667→        isOpen={isPreviewTogetherOpen}
   668→        onOpenChange={setIsPreviewTogetherOpen}
   669→        previewableSegments={previewableSegments as PreviewSegment[]}
   670→        projectAspectRatio={projectAspectRatio}
   671→        audioUrl={audioUrl}
   672→      />
   673→    </Card>
   674→  );
   675→};
   676→
   677→export default React.memo(ShotImagesEditor);
   678→
```

```
   375→    initializeTimelineFrames: async () => {}, // Not needed in core hook
   376→    loadPositions: coreHookData.refetch,
   377→    pairPrompts: coreHookData.pairPrompts,
   378→    isLoading: coreHookData.isLoading,
   379→  });
   380→  
   381→  const shotGenerations = propShotGenerations || hookData.shotGenerations;
   382→  const updateTimelineFrame = propUpdateTimelineFrame || hookData.updateTimelineFrame;
   383→  const batchExchangePositions = hookData.batchExchangePositions; // Always use hook for exchanges
   384→  const initializeTimelineFrames = hookData.initializeTimelineFrames;
   385→  
   386→  // [TimelineVisibility] Track when Timeline receives data updates from parent
   387→  React.useEffect(() => {
   388→    console.log('[TimelineVisibility] 📥 Timeline DATA RECEIVED from parent:', {
   389→      shotId: shotId?.substring(0, 8) ?? 'none',
   390→      propShotGenerationsCount: propShotGenerations?.length ?? 0,
   391→      propImagesCount: propImages?.length ?? 0,
   392→      shotGenerationsCount: shotGenerations.length,
   393→      hasPropHookData: !!propHookData,
   394→      hasPropImages: !!propImages,
   395→      dataSource: propHookData ? 'shared hookData' : propImages ? 'utility hook (two-phase)' : 'legacy hook',
   396→      timestamp: Date.now()
   397→    });
   398→  }, [shotId, propShotGenerations, propImages, shotGenerations, propHookData]);
   399→  
   400→  // Log data source for debugging
   401→  console.log('[UnifiedDataFlow] Timeline data source:', {
   402→    shotId: shotId?.substring(0, 8) ?? 'none',
   403→    hasPropHookData: !!propHookData,
   404→    hasPropImages: !!propImages,
   405→    dataSource: propHookData ? 'shared hookData' : propImages ? 'utility hook (two-phase)' : 'legacy hook',
   406→    imageCount: shotGenerations.length,
   407→  });
   408→  
   409→  console.log('[DataTrace] 📥 Timeline received data:', {
   410→    shotId: shotId?.substring(0, 8) ?? 'none',
   411→    shotGenerationsCount: shotGenerations.length,
   412→    propImagesCount: propImages?.length || 0,
   413→    usingPropImages: !!propImages,
   414→  });
   415→  
   416→  // Get pair prompts from database instead of props (now reactive)
   417→  const databasePairPrompts = hookData.pairPrompts;
   418→  const actualPairPrompts = pairPrompts || databasePairPrompts; // Fallback to props for backward compatibility
   419→  const isLoading = propShotGenerations ? false : hookData.isLoading; // If props provided, never show loading (shared data)
   420→  
   421→  // Use provided images or generate from shotGenerations
   422→  const images = React.useMemo(() => {
   423→    let result: (GenerationRow & { timeline_frame?: number })[];
   424→    
   425→    if (propImages) {
   426→      result = propImages;
   427→    } else {
   428→      // Use shared transformer instead of inline mapping
   429→      result = shotGenerations
   430→        .filter(shotGen => shotGen.generation)
   431→        .map(shotGen => transformForTimeline(shotGen as unknown as RawShotGeneration))
   432→        .sort((a, b) => (a.timeline_frame ?? 0) - (b.timeline_frame ?? 0));
   433→    }
   434→    
   435→    // CRITICAL: Filter out videos - they should never appear on timeline
   436→    // Uses canonical isVideoGeneration from typeGuards
   437→    result = result.filter(img => !isVideoGeneration(img));
   438→    
   439→    // CRITICAL: Filter out unpositioned items (timeline_frame = null, undefined, or negative)
   440→    // These should NOT be included in timeline drag calculations
   441→    // Without this filter, unpositioned items get assigned frame 0 via ?? fallback
   442→    // and incorrectly get batch-updated when other items are dragged
   443→    // NOTE: -1 is used as a sentinel value in useTimelinePositionUtils for unpositioned items
   444→    result = result.filter(img => img.timeline_frame !== null && img.timeline_frame !== undefined && img.timeline_frame >= 0);
   445→
   446→    // Deterministic ordering: sort by timeline_frame, then by id as a stable tie-breaker.
   447→    // This matches backend ordering used by update-shot-pair-prompts.
   448→    result = result.sort((a, b) => {
   449→      const frameDiff = (a.timeline_frame ?? 0) - (b.timeline_frame ?? 0);
   450→      if (frameDiff !== 0) return frameDiff;
   451→      return String(a.id ?? '').localeCompare(String(b.id ?? ''));
   452→    });
   453→
   454→    // [TimelineVisibility] Log images array changes
   455→    console.log(`[TimelineVisibility] 📸 IMAGES ARRAY COMPUTED:`, {
   456→      shotId: shotId?.substring(0, 8) ?? 'none',
   457→      source: propImages ? 'propImages' : 'shotGenerations',
   458→      totalImages: result.length,
   459→      shotGenerationsCount: shotGenerations.length,
   460→      images: result.map(img => ({
   461→        id: img.id?.substring(0, 8), // shot_generations.id
   462→        generation_id: img.generation_id?.substring(0, 8),
   463→        timeline_frame: img.timeline_frame,
   464→        hasImageUrl: !!img.imageUrl
   465→      })),
   466→      timestamp: Date.now()
   467→    });
   468→
   469→    // [Position0Debug] Log timeline data transformation for debugging
   470→    const position0Images = result.filter(img => img.timeline_frame === 0);
   471→    console.log(`[Position0Debug] 🎭 Timeline images data transformation:`, {
   472→      shotId,
   473→      totalImages: result.length,
   474→      dataSource: propImages ? 'propImages' : 'shotGenerations',
   475→      position0Images: position0Images.map(img => ({
   476→        id: img.id?.substring(0, 8), // shot_generations.id
   477→        generation_id: img.generation_id?.substring(0, 8),
   478→        timeline_frame: img.timeline_frame,
   479→        hasImageUrl: !!img.imageUrl
   480→      })),
   481→      allImages: result.map(img => ({
   482→        id: img.id?.substring(0, 8), // shot_generations.id
   483→        generation_id: img.generation_id?.substring(0, 8),
   484→        timeline_frame: img.timeline_frame,
   485→        hasImageUrl: !!img.imageUrl
   486→      })).sort((a, b) => (a.timeline_frame ?? 0) - (b.timeline_frame ?? 0)),
   487→      shotGenerationsData: !propImages ? shotGenerations.map(shotGen => ({
   488→        id: shotGen.id.substring(0, 8),
   489→        generation_id: shotGen.generation_id?.substring(0, 8),
   490→        timeline_frame: shotGen.timeline_frame,
   491→        hasGeneration: !!shotGen.generation
   492→      })) : 'using propImages'
   493→    });
   494→
   495→    console.log('[DataTrace] 🎨 Timeline final images for display:', {
   496→      shotId: shotId?.substring(0, 8) ?? 'none',
   497→      total: result.length,
   498→      source: propImages ? 'propImages' : 'shotGenerations',
   499→      positioned: result.filter(img => img.timeline_frame != null && img.timeline_frame >= 0).length,
   500→    });
   501→    
   502→    return result;
   503→  }, [shotGenerations, propImages, shotId]);
   504→
   505→  // Pass all generations for readOnly mode
   506→  // This allows SegmentOutputStrip to derive parent/child videos and timeline data
   507→  // without database queries (uses the same filtering logic as the hooks)
   508→  const allGenerationsForReadOnly = readOnly ? propAllGenerations : undefined;
   509→
   510→  // Position management hook
   511→  const {
   512→    framePositions,
   513→    displayPositions,
   514→    stablePositions,
   515→    setStablePositions,
   516→    setFramePositions,
   517→    analyzePositionChanges
   518→  } = usePositionManagement({
   519→    shotId,
   520→    shotGenerations,
   521→    frameSpacing,
   522→    isLoading,
   523→    isPersistingPositions,
   524→    isDragInProgress,
   525→    updateTimelineFrame,
   526→    onFramePositionsChange,
   527→    setIsPersistingPositions
   528→  });
   529→
   530→  // Coordinate system hook
   531→  const { fullMin, fullMax, fullRange } = useCoordinateSystem({
   532→    positions: displayPositions,
   533→    shotId,
   534→    isDragInProgress
   535→  });
   536→
   537→  // Ref for lightbox index setter (needed for external generations)
   538→  const setLightboxIndexRef = useRef<(index: number) => void>(() => {});
   539→  
   540→  // External generations hook (same as ShotImageManager)
   541→  const externalGens = useExternalGenerations({
   542→    selectedShotId: shotId,
   543→    optimisticOrder: images,
   544→    images: images,
   545→    setLightboxIndexRef
   546→  });
   547→  
   548→  // Combine timeline images with external generations for navigation
   549→  const currentImages = useMemo(() => {
   550→    return [...images, ...externalGens.externalGenerations, ...externalGens.tempDerivedGenerations];
   551→  }, [images, externalGens.externalGenerations, externalGens.tempDerivedGenerations]);
   552→
   553→  // Lightbox hook  
   554→  const isMobile = useIsMobile();
   555→  const {
   556→    lightboxIndex,
   557→    currentLightboxImage: hookLightboxImage,
   558→    autoEnterInpaint,
   559→    goNext,
   560→    goPrev,
   561→    closeLightbox: hookCloseLightbox,
   562→    openLightbox,
   563→    openLightboxWithInpaint,
   564→    handleDesktopDoubleClick,
   565→    handleMobileTap,
   566→    hasNext: hookHasNext,
   567→    hasPrevious: hookHasPrevious,
   568→    showNavigation,
   569→    setLightboxIndex // Get the raw state setter
   570→  } = useLightbox({ images: currentImages, shotId, isMobile });
   571→  
   572→  // Update the ref with the actual setter, using the raw state setter to avoid stale closures
   573→  useEffect(() => {
   574→    setLightboxIndexRef.current = setLightboxIndex;
   575→  }, [setLightboxIndex]);
   576→  
   577→  // Wrap closeLightbox to clear external generations
   578→  const closeLightbox = useCallback(() => {
   579→    externalGens.setExternalGenerations([]);
   580→    externalGens.setTempDerivedGenerations([]);
   581→    externalGens.setDerivedNavContext(null);
   582→    hookCloseLightbox();
   583→  }, [hookCloseLightbox, externalGens]);
   584→
   585→  // Handle pending image to open (from constituent image navigation / TasksPane deep-link)
   586→  const capturedVariantIdRef = usePendingImageOpen({
   587→    pendingImageToOpen,
   588→    pendingImageVariantId,
   589→    images: currentImages,
   590→    openLightbox,
   591→    onClear: onClearPendingImageToOpen,
   592→  });
   593→
   594→  // Add derived navigation mode support (navigates only through "Based on this" items when active)
   595→  const { wrappedGoNext, wrappedGoPrev, hasNext: derivedHasNext, hasPrevious: derivedHasPrevious } = useDerivedNavigation({
   596→    derivedNavContext: externalGens.derivedNavContext,
   597→    lightboxIndex,
   598→    currentImages,
   599→    handleOpenExternalGeneration: externalGens.handleOpenExternalGeneration,
   600→    goNext,
   601→    goPrev,
   602→    logPrefix: '[Timeline:DerivedNav]'
   603→  });
   604→  
   605→  // Use combined images for current image and navigation
   606→  const currentLightboxImage = lightboxIndex !== null ? currentImages[lightboxIndex] : null;
   607→  const hasNext = derivedHasNext;
   608→  const hasPrevious = derivedHasPrevious;
   609→
   610→  // Compute adjacent segments data for lightbox navigation
   611→  // This enables navigation between the current image and videos that start/end with it
   612→  const adjacentSegmentsData: AdjacentSegmentsData | undefined = useMemo(() => {
   613→    if (!segmentSlots || segmentSlots.length === 0 || !onOpenSegmentSlot || lightboxIndex === null) {
   614→      return undefined;
   615→    }
   616→
   617→    const currentImage = currentImages[lightboxIndex];
   618→    if (!currentImage) {
   619→      return undefined;
   620→    }
   621→
   622→    // Find the position of the current image in the images array
   623→    // lightboxIndex is into currentImages which is [...images, ...externalGens...]
   624→    // If lightboxIndex >= images.length, it's an external gen and shouldn't have adjacent segments
   625→    if (lightboxIndex >= images.length) {
   626→      return undefined;
   627→    }
   628→
   629→    // The image's position in the timeline is its index in the images array
   630→    const imagePosition = lightboxIndex;
   631→
   632→    // Helper to get image URL from images array by position
   633→    const getImageUrl = (position: number): string | undefined => {
   634→      const img = images[position];
   635→      const urlFallback = (img as (typeof img & { url?: string }) | undefined)?.url;
   636→      return img?.thumbUrl || img?.imageUrl || urlFallback || img?.location;
   637→    };
   638→
   639→    let prevSegment: { pairIndex: number; hasVideo: boolean; startImageUrl?: string; endImageUrl?: string } | undefined;
   640→    let nextSegment: { pairIndex: number; hasVideo: boolean; startImageUrl?: string; endImageUrl?: string } | undefined;
   641→
   642→    // Slot at position P starts at image P and ends at image P+1
   643→    // If current image is at position P:
   644→    // - nextSegment: slot at index P (segment starting at this image), if P < segmentSlots.length
   645→    // - prevSegment: slot at index P-1 (segment ending at this image), if P > 0
   646→
   647→    // Check if there's a segment starting at this image (nextSegment)
   648→    // Segment at position P goes from image P to image P+1
   649→    if (imagePosition < segmentSlots.length) {
   650→      const slot = segmentSlots[imagePosition];
   651→      if (slot) {
   652→        const hasVideo = slot.type === 'child' && !!slot.child.location;
   653→        nextSegment = {
   654→          pairIndex: slot.index,
   655→          hasVideo,
   656→          startImageUrl: getImageUrl(imagePosition),      // Start image is current image
   657→          endImageUrl: getImageUrl(imagePosition + 1),    // End image is next image
   658→        };
   659→      }
   660→    }
   661→
   662→    // Check if there's a segment ending at this image (prevSegment)
   663→    // Segment at position P-1 goes from image P-1 to image P (current)
   664→    if (imagePosition > 0 && imagePosition - 1 < segmentSlots.length) {
   665→      const slot = segmentSlots[imagePosition - 1];
   666→      if (slot) {
   667→        const hasVideo = slot.type === 'child' && !!slot.child.location;
   668→        prevSegment = {
   669→          pairIndex: slot.index,
   670→          hasVideo,
   671→          startImageUrl: getImageUrl(imagePosition - 1),  // Start image is previous image
   672→          endImageUrl: getImageUrl(imagePosition),        // End image is current image
   673→        };
   674→      }
   675→    }
   676→
   677→    // [SegmentNavDebug] Log for debugging
   678→    console.log('[SegmentNavDebug] Timeline position-based matching:', {
   679→      lightboxIndex,
   680→      imagePosition,
   681→      totalImages: images.length,
   682→      totalSegmentSlots: segmentSlots.length,
   683→      hasPrev: !!prevSegment,
   684→      hasNext: !!nextSegment,
   685→      prevHasVideo: prevSegment?.hasVideo,
   686→      nextHasVideo: nextSegment?.hasVideo,
   687→      prevStartUrl: prevSegment?.startImageUrl?.substring(0, 50),
   688→      prevEndUrl: prevSegment?.endImageUrl?.substring(0, 50),
   689→      nextStartUrl: nextSegment?.startImageUrl?.substring(0, 50),
   690→      nextEndUrl: nextSegment?.endImageUrl?.substring(0, 50),
   691→    });
   692→
   693→    // If neither prev nor next segment found, no navigation available
   694→    if (!prevSegment && !nextSegment) {
   695→      return undefined;
   696→    }
   697→
   698→    return {
   699→      prev: prevSegment,
   700→      next: nextSegment,
   701→      onNavigateToSegment: (pairIndex: number) => {
   702→        console.log('[LightboxTransition] onNavigateToSegment: using transition');
   703→        if (navigateWithTransition) {
   704→          navigateWithTransition(() => {
   705→            closeLightbox();
   706→            onOpenSegmentSlot(pairIndex);
   707→          });
   708→        } else {
   709→          // Fallback if no transition helper provided
   710→          closeLightbox();
   711→          onOpenSegmentSlot(pairIndex);
   712→        }
   713→      },
   714→    };
   715→  }, [segmentSlots, onOpenSegmentSlot, lightboxIndex, currentImages, closeLightbox, navigateWithTransition]);
   716→
   717→  // Adapter functions for onAddToShot that use the target shot ID from the callback
   718→  // CRITICAL FIX: Now receives targetShotId from the callback, not from local state!
   719→  // This ensures the image is added to the shot the user SELECTED in the dropdown
   720→  const handleAddToShotAdapter = useCallback(async (
   721→    targetShotId: string,
   722→    generationId: string,
   723→    imageUrl?: string,
   724→    thumbUrl?: string
   725→  ): Promise<boolean> => {
   726→    if (!onAddToShot || !targetShotId) {
   727→      console.warn('[Timeline] Cannot add to shot: missing onAddToShot or targetShotId');
   728→      return false;
   729→    }
   730→
   731→    try {
   732→      console.log('[Timeline] Adding generation to shot with position', {
   733→        generationId: generationId.substring(0, 8),
   734→        targetShotId: targetShotId.substring(0, 8),
   735→        lightboxSelectedShotId: lightboxSelectedShotId?.substring(0, 8)
   736→      });
   737→
   738→      // Call parent's onAddToShot with the target shot ID from the callback
   739→      // Position is undefined to let the mutation calculate the correct position for the TARGET shot
   740→      await onAddToShot(targetShotId, generationId, undefined);
   741→      return true;
   742→    } catch (error) {
   743→      handleError(error, { context: 'Timeline', toastTitle: 'Failed to add to shot' });
   744→      return false;
   745→    }
   746→  }, [lightboxSelectedShotId, onAddToShot]);
   747→
   748→  // CRITICAL FIX: Now receives targetShotId from the callback
   749→  const handleAddToShotWithoutPositionAdapter = useCallback(async (
   750→    targetShotId: string,
   751→    generationId: string,
   752→    imageUrl?: string,
   753→    thumbUrl?: string
   754→  ): Promise<boolean> => {
   755→    if (!onAddToShotWithoutPosition || !targetShotId) {
   756→      console.warn('[Timeline] Cannot add to shot without position: missing handler or targetShotId');
   757→      return false;
   758→    }
   759→
   760→    try {
   761→      console.log('[Timeline] Adding generation to shot without position', {
   762→        generationId: generationId.substring(0, 8),
   763→        targetShotId: targetShotId.substring(0, 8)
   764→      });
   765→
   766→      // Call parent's onAddToShotWithoutPosition with the target shot ID from callback
   767→      await onAddToShotWithoutPosition(targetShotId, generationId);
   768→      return true;
   769→    } catch (error) {
   770→      handleError(error, { context: 'Timeline', toastTitle: 'Failed to add to shot' });
   771→      return false;
   772→    }
   773→  }, [lightboxSelectedShotId, onAddToShotWithoutPosition]);
   774→
   775→  // Detect tablet/iPad size (768px+) for side-by-side task details layout
   776→  const { isTabletOrLarger } = useDeviceDetection();
   777→
   778→  // Fetch task ID mapping from unified cache
   779→  // Uses generation_id (the actual generation record) not id (shot_generations entry)
   780→  const { data: taskMapping } = useTaskFromUnifiedCache(
   781→    currentLightboxImage?.generation_id || null
   782→  );
   783→
   784→  // Extract taskId and convert from Json to string
   785→  const taskId = React.useMemo(() => {
   786→    if (!taskMapping?.taskId) return undefined;
   787→    return String(taskMapping.taskId);
   788→  }, [taskMapping]);
   789→
   790→  // Fetch full task details using the task ID (only enabled when we have a taskId)
   791→  const { data: task, isLoading: isLoadingTask, error: taskError } = useGetTask(
   792→    taskId || ''  // Pass empty string if no taskId, hook will be disabled via enabled: !!taskId
   793→  );
   794→
   795→  // Derive input images from task metadata
   796→  const inputImages = React.useMemo(() => {
   797→    if (!task) return [];
   798→    return deriveInputImages(task);
   799→  }, [task]);
   800→
   801→  // Preload next/previous images when lightbox is open for faster navigation
   802→  useEffect(() => {
   803→    if (!currentLightboxImage) return;
   804→    
   805→    // Preload next image
   806→    if (hasNext && lightboxIndex !== null && lightboxIndex + 1 < images.length) {
   807→      const nextImage = images[lightboxIndex + 1];
   808→      if (nextImage?.imageUrl) {
   809→        const img = new window.Image();
   810→        img.src = nextImage.imageUrl;
   811→      }
   812→    }
   813→    
   814→    // Preload previous image
   815→    if (hasPrevious && lightboxIndex !== null && lightboxIndex > 0) {
   816→      const prevImage = images[lightboxIndex - 1];
   817→      if (prevImage?.imageUrl) {
   818→        const img = new window.Image();
   819→        img.src = prevImage.imageUrl;
   820→      }
   821→    }
   822→  }, [currentLightboxImage, lightboxIndex, images, hasNext, hasPrevious]);
   823→
   824→  // Close lightbox if current image no longer exists (e.g., deleted)
   825→  useEffect(() => {
   826→    if (lightboxIndex !== null && !currentLightboxImage) {
   827→      console.log('[Timeline] Current lightbox image no longer exists, closing lightbox');
   828→      closeLightbox();
   829→    }
   830→  }, [lightboxIndex, currentLightboxImage, closeLightbox]);
   831→
   832→  // Listen for star updates and refetch shot data
   833→  useEffect(() => {
   834→    const handleStarUpdated = (event: Event) => {
   835→      const customEvent = event as CustomEvent;
   836→      const { shotId: updatedShotId } = customEvent.detail || {};
   837→      
   838→      // Only refetch if this event is for our current shot
   839→      if (updatedShotId === shotId) {
   840→        console.log('[StarPersist] 🎯 Timeline received star-updated event, refetching...', {
   841→          shotId,
   842→          timestamp: Date.now()
   843→        });
   844→        
   845→        // Trigger a refetch of shot generations
   846→        if (hookData?.loadPositions) {
   847→          hookData.loadPositions({ silent: true, reason: 'shot_change' });
   848→        }
   849→      }
   850→    };
   851→    
   852→    window.addEventListener('generation-star-updated', handleStarUpdated);
   853→    return () => window.removeEventListener('generation-star-updated', handleStarUpdated);
   854→  }, [shotId, hookData]);
   855→
   856→  // Track previous context frames to detect increases
   857→  // const prevContextFramesRef = useRef<number>(contextFrames);
   858→  // const isAdjustingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
   859→  
   860→  // Auto-adjust timeline positions when context frames increases
   861→  /*
   862→  // REMOVED: Auto-adjust logic for context frames as context frames are being removed
   863→  // The logic was checking if currentContext > prevContext and adjusting positions if gaps were too small
   864→  // or too large based on calculateMaxGap(contextFrames).
   865→  */
   866→
   867→  // Handle resetting frames to evenly spaced intervals and setting context frames
   868→  // Gap values are quantized to 4N+1 format for Wan model compatibility
   869→  const handleResetFrames = useCallback(async (gap: number) => {
   870→    // First set the context frames (this will trigger all constraint recalculations)
   871→    // onContextFramesChange(newContextFrames); // Removed
   872→    
   873→    // Quantize the gap to 4N+1 format (5, 9, 13, 17, 21, 25, 29, 33, ...)
   874→    const quantizedGap = quantizeGap(gap, 5);
   875→    
   876→    // Then set the positions with the specified quantized gap
   877→    const newPositions = new Map<string, number>();
   878→    images.forEach((image, index) => {
   879→      // Use id (shot_generations.id) for position mapping - unique per entry
   880→      // First image at 0, subsequent images at quantized intervals
   881→      newPositions.set(image.id, index * quantizedGap);
   882→    });
   883→
   884→    await setFramePositions(newPositions);
   885→  }, [images, setFramePositions]);
   886→
   887→  // Check if timeline is empty
   888→  const hasNoImages = images.length === 0;
   889→
   890→  // Drag and drop state for empty state upload container
   891→  const [isDragOver, setIsDragOver] = useState(false);
   892→  const [dragType, setDragType] = useState<'file' | 'generation' | null>(null);
   893→
   894→  // Drag and drop handlers for empty state - supports both files AND internal generations
   895→  const handleEmptyStateDragEnter = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
   896→    e.preventDefault();
   897→    e.stopPropagation();
   898→
   899→    // Check for internal generation drag first
   900→    if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes('application/x-generation') && onGenerationDrop) {
   901→      setIsDragOver(true);
   902→      setDragType('generation');
   903→    } else if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes('Files') && onFileDrop) {
   904→      setIsDragOver(true);
   905→      setDragType('file');
   906→    }
   907→  }, [onFileDrop, onGenerationDrop]);
   908→
   909→  const handleEmptyStateDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
   910→    e.preventDefault();
   911→    e.stopPropagation();
   912→
   913→    // Check for internal generation drag first
   914→    if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes('application/x-generation') && onGenerationDrop) {
   915→      setIsDragOver(true);
   916→      setDragType('generation');
   917→      e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy';
   918→    } else if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes('Files') && onFileDrop) {
   919→      setIsDragOver(true);
   920→      setDragType('file');
   921→      e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy';
   922→    } else {
   923→      e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'none';
   924→    }
   925→  }, [onFileDrop, onGenerationDrop]);
   926→
   927→  const handleEmptyStateDragLeave = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
   928→    e.preventDefault();
   929→    e.stopPropagation();
   930→    // Only hide if we're leaving the container entirely
   931→    const rect = (e.currentTarget as HTMLElement).getBoundingClientRect();
   932→    const x = e.clientX;
   933→    const y = e.clientY;
   934→    if (x < rect.left || x >= rect.right || y < rect.top || y >= rect.bottom) {
   935→      setIsDragOver(false);
   936→      setDragType(null);
   937→    }
   938→  }, []);
   939→
   940→  const handleEmptyStateDrop = useCallback(async (e: React.DragEvent) => {
   941→    e.preventDefault();
   942→    e.stopPropagation();
   943→    setIsDragOver(false);
   944→    setDragType(null);
   945→
   946→    // Handle internal generation drop first
   947→    if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes('application/x-generation') && onGenerationDrop) {
   948→      try {
   949→        const dataString = e.dataTransfer.getData('application/x-generation');
   950→        if (dataString) {
   951→          const data = JSON.parse(dataString);
   952→          if (data.generationId && data.imageUrl) {
   953→            await onGenerationDrop(data.generationId, data.imageUrl, data.thumbUrl, 0);
   954→            return;
   955→          }
   956→        }
   957→      } catch (error) {
   958→        handleError(error, { context: 'Timeline', toastTitle: 'Failed to add image' });
   959→      }
   960→      return;
   961→    }
   962→
   963→    // Handle file drop
   964→    if (!onImageUpload) return;
   965→
   966→    const files = Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files);
   967→    if (files.length === 0) return;
   968→
   969→    // Validate image types
   970→    const validImageTypes = ['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp', 'image/jpg'];
   971→    const validFiles = files.filter(file => {
   972→      if (validImageTypes.includes(file.type)) {
   973→        return true;
   974→      }
   975→      toast.error(`Invalid file type for ${file.name}. Only JPEG, PNG, and WebP are supported.`);
   976→      return false;
   977→    });
   978→
   979→    if (validFiles.length === 0) return;
   980→
   981→    try {
   982→      await onImageUpload(validFiles);
   983→    } catch (error) {
   984→      handleError(error, { context: 'Timeline', toastTitle: 'Failed to add images' });
   985→    }
   986→  }, [onImageUpload, onGenerationDrop]);
   987→
   988→  return (
```

### Timeline.tsx

**Finding 3: `hookData` (prop) - USED, BUT PARTIALLY DEAD CODE**
- **Declaration**: Line 101 in interface, destructured as `propHookData` on line 230
- **Assignment**: Line 230, then computed on line 362
- **Usage Detected**:
  - Line 395: `hasPropHookData: !!propHookData` (used in logging)

- **Issue**: While `hookData` is technically used, **`propHookData` from props is almost entirely unused**. The prop serves as an optional override (backward compatibility), but ShotImagesEditor never passes it (always passes `undefined` implicitly).
- **Conclusion**: Prop is declared but effectively dead code - ShotImagesEditor doesn't pass it, so the fallback logic always activates.

**Finding 4: `pairPrompts` (prop) - USED BUT LIMITED**
- **Declaration**: Line 124 in interface
- **Destructuring**: Line 232
- **Usage Detected**:

  But for Timeline (Timeline mode), **pairPrompts is NOT passed** - it relies on the database version (hookData.pairPrompts).
- **Conclusion**: Prop is used but underutilized - Timeline ignores it in favor of database values when available.

**Finding 5: `enhancedPrompts` (prop) - VIRTUALLY UNUSED**
- **Declaration**: Line 125 in interface
- **Destructuring**: Line 233
- **Usage Detected**:

- **Status**: This is the ONLY reference. It's destructured and passed through but:

  - ShotImagesEditor does NOT pass `enhancedPrompts` prop to Timeline (line 496-561)
- **Conclusion**: **DEAD CODE** - Prop is declared and destructured, passed through to child, but never sourced from parent for timeline mode. It always defaults to `EMPTY_ENHANCED_PROMPTS`.

**Finding 6: `onImageDuplicate` (prop) - REQUIRED BUT MARKED AS OPTIONAL**
- **Declaration**: Line 133 in interface (NOT marked optional - no `?`)
- **Status from ShotImagesEditor**: Line 508, it IS passed as optional:

  But looking at ShotImagesEditor props line 89: `onImageDuplicate,` (no `= false` or default)

- **Usage**: Line 1072 passed to TimelineContainer
- **Issue**: The prop is required in Timeline's interface but ShotImagesEditor could theoretically pass `undefined`. However, checking ShotImagesEditor.tsx line 89, it's listed without a default, so it could be undefined at runtime.
- **Conclusion**: Mismatch - Timeline declares it as required, but parent could pass undefined. Should either:

  2. Provide a default handler in Timeline

## Summary

| Item | Status | Severity |
|------|--------|----------|
| `useMemo` import | ✓ Used correctly | None |
| All destructured props (ShotImagesEditor) | ✓ All used | None |
| `hookData` prop (Timeline) | Technically used but fallback always triggers | Low |

| `enhancedPrompts` prop (Timeline) | Declared, destructured, never sourced | **High - Dead Code** |

### Key Findings:

1. **`enhancedPrompts` in Timeline.tsx** is the clearest case of dead code - it's a declared prop that's never passed from the parent (ShotImagesEditor doesn't pass it for timeline mode, only batch mode), yet it's destructured and passed through to children, always falling back to `EMPTY_ENHANCED_PROMPTS`.

2. **`propHookData` fallback logic** exists but is never triggered from the parent - ShotImagesEditor never passes the `hookData` prop, making that entire branch of the conditional logic (lines 362-370) unreachable in practice.

3. **`onImageDuplicate` mismatch** - declared as required in Timeline but ShotImagesEditor passes it as-is from props without validation, creating a potential type safety issue.

```
1→/**
     2→ * LightboxLayout - Unified layout for all lightbox configurations
     3→ *
     4→ * Replaces DesktopSidePanelLayout, MobileStackedLayout, and CenteredLayout.
     5→ * All conditional rendering derives from two values:
     6→ *   - showPanel: whether the controls panel renders
     7→ *   - shouldShowSidePanel: whether the panel goes right (side-by-side) or bottom (stacked)
     8→ */
     9→
    10→import React from 'react';
    11→import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/utils';
    12→import { Button } from '@/shared/components/ui/button';
    13→import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from '@/shared/components/ui/tooltip';
    14→import { Eraser, Square, Undo2, X, Diamond, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
    15→import type { LightboxLayoutProps } from './types';
    16→
    17→// Context hooks
    18→import {
    19→  useLightboxCoreSafe,
    20→  useLightboxMediaSafe,
    21→  useLightboxVariantsSafe,
    22→  useLightboxNavigationSafe,
    23→  useLightboxEditSafe,
    24→} from '../../contexts/LightboxStateContext';
    25→
    26→// Sub-components
    27→import { VariantOverlayBadge } from './VariantOverlayBadge';
    28→import { NewImageOverlayButton } from './NewImageOverlayButton';
    29→import { AdjacentSegmentNavigation } from './AdjacentSegmentNavigation';
    30→import { ConstituentImageNavigation } from './ConstituentImageNavigation';
    31→
    32→// Existing components
    33→import { NavigationArrows } from '../NavigationArrows';
    34→import { FloatingToolControls } from '../FloatingToolControls';
    35→import {
    36→  TopRightControls,
    37→  BottomLeftControls,
    38→  BottomRightControls,
    39→} from '../ButtonGroups';
    40→import { ControlsPanel } from '../ControlsPanel';
    41→import { MediaDisplayWithCanvas } from '../MediaDisplayWithCanvas';
    42→import { VideoEditModeDisplay } from '../VideoEditModeDisplay';
    43→import { VideoTrimModeDisplay } from '../VideoTrimModeDisplay';
    44→import { WorkflowControls } from '../WorkflowControls';
    45→import { WorkflowControlsBar } from '../WorkflowControlsBar';
    46→
    47→export const LightboxLayout: React.FC<LightboxLayoutProps> = (props) => {
    48→  // ========================================
    49→  // CONTEXT STATE
    50→  // ========================================
    51→  const core = useLightboxCoreSafe();
    52→  const mediaState = useLightboxMediaSafe();
    53→  const variantsState = useLightboxVariantsSafe();
    54→  const navigation = useLightboxNavigationSafe();
    55→  const editState = useLightboxEditSafe();
    56→
    57→  const { onClose, readOnly, isMobile, actualGenerationId, selectedProjectId } = core;
    58→  const { media, isVideo, effectiveMediaUrl, effectiveVideoUrl, imageDimensions, setImageDimensions, effectiveImageDimensions } = mediaState;
    59→  const { variants, activeVariant, primaryVariant, promoteSuccess, isPromoting, handlePromoteToGeneration, isMakingMainVariant, canMakeMainVariant, handleMakeMainVariant } = variantsState;
    60→  const { showNavigation, hasNext, hasPrevious, handleSlotNavNext, handleSlotNavPrev, swipeNavigation } = navigation;
    61→  const { isInpaintMode, isSpecialEditMode, editMode } = editState;
    62→
    63→  // ========================================
    64→  // PROPS
    65→  // ========================================
    66→  const {
    67→    showPanel,
    68→    shouldShowSidePanel,
    69→
    70→    // Video edit
    71→    isVideoTrimModeActive,
    72→    isVideoEditModeActive,
    73→    trimVideoRef,
    74→    trimState,
    75→    setVideoDuration,
    76→    setTrimCurrentTime,
    77→    videoEditing,
    78→
    79→    // Canvas/annotation
    80→    isAnnotateMode,
    81→    brushStrokes,
    82→    currentStroke,
    83→    isDrawing,
    84→    isEraseMode,
    85→    setIsEraseMode,
    86→    brushSize,
    87→    setBrushSize,
    88→    annotationMode,
    89→    selectedShapeId,
    90→    handleKonvaPointerDown,
    91→    handleKonvaPointerMove,
    92→    handleKonvaPointerUp,
    93→    handleShapeClick,
    94→    strokeOverlayRef,
    95→    handleUndo,
    96→    handleClearMask,
    97→    getDeleteButtonPosition,
    98→    handleToggleFreeForm,
    99→    handleDeleteSelected,
   100→    isRepositionDragging,
   101→    repositionDragHandlers,
   102→    getTransformStyle,
   103→    imageContainerRef,
   104→    canvasRef,
   105→    maskCanvasRef,
   106→    isFlippedHorizontally,
   107→    isSaving,
   108→
   109→    // Panel state
   110→    effectiveTasksPaneOpen,
   111→    effectiveTasksPaneWidth,
   112→
   113→    // Reposition rotation (for corner drag-to-rotate)
   114→    onRepositionRotationChange,
   115→    repositionRotation,
   116→
   117→    // Reposition scale (for +/- zoom buttons on image)
   118→    onRepositionScaleChange,
   119→    repositionScale,
   120→
   121→    // Composed prop objects
   122→    buttonGroupProps,
   123→    workflowBarProps,
   124→    floatingToolProps,
   125→    controlsPanelProps,
   126→
   127→    // Workflow controls (below media, centered only)
   128→    workflowControlsProps,
   129→
   130→    // Special navigation
   131→    adjacentSegments,
   132→    segmentSlotMode,
   133→  } = props;
   134→
   135→  // ========================================
   136→  // DERIVED LAYOUT VALUES
   137→  // ========================================
   138→  // shouldShowSidePanel = side-by-side (iPad landscape, desktop); !shouldShowSidePanel && showPanel = stacked (mobile/portrait)
   139→  const isSidePanelLayout = showPanel && shouldShowSidePanel;
   140→  const isStackedLayout = showPanel && !shouldShowSidePanel;
   141→
   142→  const enableSwipe = !showPanel || !shouldShowSidePanel;
   143→  const navArrowVariant = isSidePanelLayout ? 'desktop' : 'mobile';
   144→  const floatingToolVariant = isMobile ? 'mobile' : 'tablet';
   145→
   146→  // MediaDisplayWithCanvas variant/container
   147→  const mediaDisplayVariant = showPanel
   148→    ? (isSidePanelLayout ? 'desktop-side-panel' : 'mobile-stacked')
   149→    : 'regular-centered';
   150→  const mediaDisplayContainerClassName = isSidePanelLayout
   151→    ? 'max-w-full max-h-full'
   152→    : 'w-full h-full';
   153→  const mediaDisplayDebugContext = showPanel
   154→    ? (isSidePanelLayout ? 'Desktop' : 'Mobile Stacked')
   155→    : 'Regular Centered';
   156→
   157→  // Top center offset: stacked layout uses responsive top-4 md:top-16
   158→  const topCenterClassName = isStackedLayout
   159→    ? 'absolute top-4 md:top-16 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 z-[60] flex flex-col items-center gap-2'
   160→    : 'absolute top-4 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 z-[60] flex flex-col items-center gap-2';
   161→
   162→  // ========================================
   163→  // SHARED MEDIA CONTENT RENDERING
   164→  // ========================================
   165→  const renderMediaContent = () => {
   166→    if (isVideo && isVideoEditModeActive && videoEditing) {
   167→      return (
   168→        <VideoEditModeDisplay
   169→          videoRef={videoEditing.videoRef}
   170→          videoUrl={effectiveVideoUrl}
   171→          posterUrl={activeVariant?.thumbnail_url || media.thumbUrl}
   172→          videoDuration={trimState.videoDuration}
   173→          onLoadedMetadata={setVideoDuration}
   174→          selections={videoEditing.selections}
   175→          activeSelectionId={videoEditing.activeSelectionId}
   176→          onSelectionChange={videoEditing.handleUpdateSelection}
   177→          onSelectionClick={videoEditing.setActiveSelectionId}
   178→          onRemoveSelection={videoEditing.handleRemoveSelection}
   179→          onAddSelection={videoEditing.handleAddSelection}
   180→        />
   181→      );
   182→    }
   183→
   184→    if (isVideo && isVideoTrimModeActive) {
   185→      return (
   186→        <VideoTrimModeDisplay
   187→          videoRef={trimVideoRef}
   188→          videoUrl={effectiveVideoUrl}
   189→          posterUrl={activeVariant?.thumbnail_url || media.thumbUrl}
   190→          trimState={trimState}
   191→          onLoadedMetadata={setVideoDuration}
   192→          onTimeUpdate={setTrimCurrentTime}
   193→        />
   194→      );
   195→    }
   196→
   197→    // All layouts route through MediaDisplayWithCanvas (handles StyledVideoPlayer internally)
   198→    return (
   199→      <MediaDisplayWithCanvas
   200→        effectiveImageUrl={isVideo ? effectiveVideoUrl : effectiveMediaUrl}
   201→        thumbUrl={activeVariant?.thumbnail_url || media.thumbUrl}
   202→        isVideo={isVideo}
   203→        isFlippedHorizontally={isFlippedHorizontally}
   204→        isSaving={isSaving}
   205→        isInpaintMode={isInpaintMode}
   206→        editMode={editMode}
   207→        repositionTransformStyle={editMode === 'reposition' ? getTransformStyle() : undefined}
   208→        repositionDragHandlers={editMode === 'reposition' ? repositionDragHandlers : undefined}
   209→        isRepositionDragging={isRepositionDragging}
   210→        onRepositionRotationChange={editMode === 'reposition' ? onRepositionRotationChange : undefined}
   211→        repositionRotation={repositionRotation}
   212→        onRepositionScaleChange={editMode === 'reposition' ? onRepositionScaleChange : undefined}
   213→        repositionScale={repositionScale}
   214→        imageContainerRef={imageContainerRef}
   215→        canvasRef={canvasRef}
   216→        maskCanvasRef={maskCanvasRef}
   217→        onImageLoad={setImageDimensions}
   218→        onVideoLoadedMetadata={(e) => {
   219→          const video = e.currentTarget;
   220→          if (Number.isFinite(video.duration) && video.duration > 0) {
   221→            setVideoDuration(video.duration);
   222→          }
   223→        }}
   224→        variant={mediaDisplayVariant}
   225→        containerClassName={mediaDisplayContainerClassName}
   226→        tasksPaneWidth={isSidePanelLayout && effectiveTasksPaneOpen ? effectiveTasksPaneWidth : 0}
   227→        debugContext={mediaDisplayDebugContext}
   228→        // Konva-based stroke overlay props
   229→        imageDimensions={effectiveImageDimensions}
   230→        brushStrokes={brushStrokes}
   231→        currentStroke={currentStroke}
   232→        isDrawing={isDrawing}
   233→        isEraseMode={isEraseMode}
   234→        brushSize={brushSize}
   235→        annotationMode={editMode === 'annotate' ? annotationMode : null}
   236→        selectedShapeId={selectedShapeId}
   237→        onStrokePointerDown={handleKonvaPointerDown}
   238→        onStrokePointerMove={handleKonvaPointerMove}
   239→        onStrokePointerUp={handleKonvaPointerUp}
   240→        onShapeClick={handleShapeClick}
   241→        strokeOverlayRef={strokeOverlayRef}
   242→      />
   243→    );
   244→  };
   245→
   246→  // ========================================
   247→  // SHARED ANNOTATION BUTTONS
   248→  // ========================================
   249→  const renderAnnotationButtons = () => {
   250→    if (!selectedShapeId || !isAnnotateMode) return null;
   251→
   252→    const buttonPos = getDeleteButtonPosition();
   253→    if (!buttonPos) return null;
   254→
   255→    const selectedShape = brushStrokes.find(s => s.id === selectedShapeId);
   256→    const isFreeForm = selectedShape?.isFreeForm || false;
   257→
   258→    return (
   259→      <div className="fixed z-[100] flex gap-2" style={{
   260→        left: `${buttonPos.x}px`,
   261→        top: `${buttonPos.y}px`,
   262→        transform: 'translate(-50%, -50%)'
   263→      }}>
   264→        <button
   265→          onClick={handleToggleFreeForm}
   266→          className={cn(
   267→            "rounded-full p-2 shadow-lg transition-colors",
   268→            isFreeForm
   269→              ? "bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700 text-white"
   270→              : "bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 text-white"
   271→          )}
   272→          title={isFreeForm
   273→            ? "Switch to rectangle mode (edges move linearly)"
   274→            : "Switch to free-form mode (rhombus/non-orthogonal angles)"}
   275→        >
   276→          {isFreeForm ? <Diamond className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Square className="h-4 w-4" />}
   277→        </button>
   278→        <button
   279→          onClick={handleDeleteSelected}
   280→          className="bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white rounded-full p-2 shadow-lg transition-colors"
   281→          title="Delete annotation (or press DELETE key)"
   282→        >
   283→          <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
   284→        </button>
   285→      </div>
   286→    );
   287→  };
   288→
   289→  // ========================================
   290→  // RENDER
   291→  // ========================================
   292→
   293→  if (showPanel) {
   294→    // ======== PANEL LAYOUT (Desktop side panel / Mobile stacked) ========
   295→    const isDesktopPanel = isSidePanelLayout;
   296→
   297→    return (
   298→      <div
   299→        data-lightbox-bg
   300→        className={cn(
   301→          "w-full h-full bg-black/90",
   302→          isDesktopPanel ? "flex" : "flex flex-col"
   303→        )}
   304→        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   305→      >
   306→        {/* Media section */}
   307→        <div
   308→          data-lightbox-bg
   309→          className={cn(
   310→            "flex items-center justify-center relative overflow-hidden",
   311→            isDesktopPanel
   312→              ? "flex-1 touch-none"
   313→              : "flex-none touch-none z-10"
   314→          )}
   315→          style={isDesktopPanel
   316→            ? { width: '60%' }
   317→            : {
   318→                height: '50%',
   319→                transform: swipeNavigation.isSwiping ? `translateX(${swipeNavigation.swipeOffset}px)` : undefined,
   320→                transition: swipeNavigation.isSwiping ? 'none' : 'transform 0.2s ease-out',
   321→              }
   322→          }
   323→          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   324→          {...(!isDesktopPanel ? swipeNavigation.swipeHandlers : {})}
   325→        >
   326→          {/* Desktop: nav arrows before media */}
   327→          {isDesktopPanel && (
   328→            <NavigationArrows
   329→              showNavigation={showNavigation}
   330→              readOnly={readOnly}
   331→              onPrevious={handleSlotNavPrev}
   332→              onNext={handleSlotNavNext}
   333→              hasPrevious={hasPrevious}
   334→              hasNext={hasNext}
   335→              variant="desktop"
   336→            />
   337→          )}
   338→
   339→          {renderMediaContent()}
   340→          {renderAnnotationButtons()}
   341→
   342→          {/* Top Center - segment nav + variant badge */}
   343→          <div className={topCenterClassName}>
   344→            {adjacentSegments && !isVideo && (
   345→              <AdjacentSegmentNavigation adjacentSegments={adjacentSegments} />
   346→            )}
   347→            <VariantOverlayBadge
   348→              activeVariant={activeVariant}
   349→              variants={variants}
   350→              readOnly={readOnly}
   351→              isMakingMainVariant={isMakingMainVariant}
   352→              canMakeMainVariant={canMakeMainVariant}
   353→              onMakeMainVariant={handleMakeMainVariant}
   354→            />
   355→          </div>
   356→
   357→          {/* Top Left - New image button */}
   358→          <NewImageOverlayButton
   359→            isVideo={isVideo}
   360→            readOnly={readOnly}
   361→            activeVariantId={activeVariant?.id}
   362→            primaryVariantId={primaryVariant?.id}
   363→            selectedProjectId={selectedProjectId}
   364→            isPromoting={isPromoting}
   365→            promoteSuccess={promoteSuccess}
   366→            onPromote={handlePromoteToGeneration}
   367→          />
   368→
   369→          {/* Floating Tool Controls (panel layouts only) */}
   370→          {isSpecialEditMode && floatingToolProps && (
   371→            <FloatingToolControls
   372→              variant={floatingToolVariant}
   373→              repositionTransform={floatingToolProps.repositionTransform}
   374→              onRepositionScaleChange={floatingToolProps.onRepositionScaleChange}
   375→              onRepositionRotationChange={floatingToolProps.onRepositionRotationChange}
   376→              onRepositionFlipH={floatingToolProps.onRepositionFlipH}
   377→              onRepositionFlipV={floatingToolProps.onRepositionFlipV}
   378→              onRepositionReset={floatingToolProps.onRepositionReset}
   379→            />
   380→          )}
   381→
   382→          {/* Button Groups */}
   383→          <BottomLeftControls {...buttonGroupProps.bottomLeft} />
   384→          <BottomRightControls {...buttonGroupProps.bottomRight} />
   385→          <TopRightControls {...buttonGroupProps.topRight} />
   386→
   387→          {/* Constituent Image Navigation */}
   388→          {segmentSlotMode?.onNavigateToImage && (
   389→            <ConstituentImageNavigation
   390→              startImageId={segmentSlotMode.pairData.startImage?.id}
   391→              endImageId={segmentSlotMode.pairData.endImage?.id}
   392→              startImageUrl={segmentSlotMode.pairData.startImage?.thumbUrl || segmentSlotMode.pairData.startImage?.url}
   393→              endImageUrl={segmentSlotMode.pairData.endImage?.thumbUrl || segmentSlotMode.pairData.endImage?.url}

   396→            />

   399→          {/* Bottom Workflow Controls Bar */}
   400→          <WorkflowControlsBar
   401→            onAddToShot={workflowBarProps.onAddToShot}
   402→            onDelete={workflowBarProps.onDelete}

   405→            isVideo={isVideo}

   407→            imageUrl={effectiveMediaUrl}

   422→            contentRef={workflowBarProps.contentRef}
   423→            handleApplySettings={workflowBarProps.handleApplySettings}
   424→            onNavigateToShot={workflowBarProps.handleNavigateToShotFromSelector}
   425→            onClose={onClose}
   426→            onAddVariantAsNewGeneration={workflowBarProps.handleAddVariantAsNewGenerationToShot}
   427→            activeVariantId={activeVariant?.id || primaryVariant?.id}
   428→            currentTimelineFrame={media.timeline_frame}
   429→          />
   430→
   431→          {/* Stacked: nav arrows after media */}
   432→          {!isDesktopPanel && (
   433→            <NavigationArrows
   434→              showNavigation={showNavigation}
   435→              readOnly={readOnly}
   436→              onPrevious={handleSlotNavPrev}
   437→              onNext={handleSlotNavNext}
   438→              hasPrevious={hasPrevious}
   439→              hasNext={hasNext}
   440→              variant="mobile"
   441→            />
   442→          )}
   443→        </div>
   444→
   445→        {/* Controls Panel */}
   446→        <div
   447→          data-task-details-panel
   448→          className={cn(
   449→            "bg-background overflow-hidden relative z-[60] overscroll-none",
   450→            isDesktopPanel
   451→              ? "border-l border-border h-full"
   452→              : "border-t border-border overflow-y-auto"
   453→          )}
   454→          style={isDesktopPanel ? { width: '40%' } : { height: '50%' }}
   455→        >
   456→          <ControlsPanel
   457→            variant={isDesktopPanel ? 'desktop' : 'mobile'}
   458→            {...controlsPanelProps!}
   459→          />
   460→        </div>
   461→      </div>
   462→    );
   463→  }
   464→
   465→  // ======== CENTERED LAYOUT (no panel) ========
   466→  return (
   467→    <div
   468→      data-lightbox-bg
   469→      className="relative flex flex-col items-center gap-3 sm:gap-4 md:gap-6 px-3 py-4 sm:px-4 sm:py-6 md:px-6 md:py-8 w-full h-full touch-none"
   470→      onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   471→    >
   472→      {/* Media Container with swipe navigation */}
   473→      <div
   474→        data-lightbox-bg
   475→        className={cn(
   476→          "relative flex items-center justify-center max-w-full my-auto",
   477→          isMobile && isInpaintMode && "pointer-events-auto",
   478→          "touch-none"
   479→        )}
   480→        style={{
   481→          height: 'calc(100vh - 220px)',
   482→          maxHeight: 'calc(100vh - 220px)',
   483→          transform: swipeNavigation.isSwiping ? `translateX(${swipeNavigation.swipeOffset}px)` : undefined,
   484→          transition: swipeNavigation.isSwiping ? 'none' : 'transform 0.2s ease-out',
   485→        }}
   486→        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   487→        {...swipeNavigation.swipeHandlers}
   488→      >
   489→        {renderMediaContent()}
   490→        {renderAnnotationButtons()}
   491→
   492→        {/* Top Center - segment nav + variant badge */}
   493→        <div className="absolute top-4 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 z-[60] flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
   494→          {adjacentSegments && !isVideo && (
   495→            <AdjacentSegmentNavigation adjacentSegments={adjacentSegments} />
   496→          )}
   497→          <VariantOverlayBadge
   498→            activeVariant={activeVariant}
   499→            variants={variants}
   500→            readOnly={readOnly}
   501→            isMakingMainVariant={isMakingMainVariant}
   502→            canMakeMainVariant={canMakeMainVariant}

   505→        </div>
   506→
   507→        {/* Top Left - New image button */}
   508→        <NewImageOverlayButton
   509→          isVideo={isVideo}
   510→          readOnly={readOnly}
   511→          activeVariantId={activeVariant?.id}
   512→          primaryVariantId={primaryVariant?.id}

   514→          isPromoting={isPromoting}

   518→
   519→        {/* Compact Edit Controls (centered layout only, no panel to hold them) */}
   520→        {!readOnly && isSpecialEditMode && editMode !== 'text' && (

   522→            className="absolute top-20 left-4 z-[70] select-none"
   523→            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   524→          >
   525→            <div className="mb-2 bg-background backdrop-blur-md rounded-lg p-2 space-y-1.5 w-40 border border-border shadow-xl">

   527→              {editMode === 'inpaint' && (

   539→                    className="w-full h-1.5 bg-muted rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-blue-500"
   540→                  />
   541→                </div>
   542→              )}
   543→

   545→              {editMode === 'inpaint' && (
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * VariantOverlayBadge - Displays variant status and make-main button
     3→ *
     4→ * Shows at top-center of the lightbox:
     5→ * - "Main variant" badge when viewing the primary variant
     6→ * - "Make main" button when viewing a non-primary variant
     7→ *
     8→ * Can be used in two modes:
     9→ * 1. Props mode: Pass all props explicitly (legacy usage)
    10→ * 2. Context mode: Uses LightboxStateContext via hooks (preferred)
    11→ */
    12→
    13→import React from 'react';
    14→import { Button } from '@/shared/components/ui/button';
    15→import { Check, Star, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
    16→import type { Variant } from '@/shared/hooks/useVariants';
    17→import { useLightboxVariantsSafe, useLightboxCoreSafe } from '../../contexts/LightboxStateContext';
    18→
    19→interface VariantOverlayBadgeProps {
    20→  // All props are optional - uses context if not provided
    21→  activeVariant?: Variant | undefined;
    22→  variants?: Variant[] | undefined;
    23→  readOnly?: boolean;
    24→  isMakingMainVariant?: boolean;
    25→  canMakeMainVariant?: boolean;
    26→  onMakeMainVariant?: () => void;
    27→}
    28→
    29→export const VariantOverlayBadge: React.FC<VariantOverlayBadgeProps> = ({
    30→  activeVariant: activeVariantProp,
    31→  variants: variantsProp,
    32→  readOnly: readOnlyProp,
    33→  isMakingMainVariant: isMakingMainVariantProp,
    34→  canMakeMainVariant: canMakeMainVariantProp,
    35→  onMakeMainVariant: onMakeMainVariantProp,
    36→}) => {
    37→  // Get values from context (safe versions return defaults if outside provider)
    38→  const core = useLightboxCoreSafe();
    39→  const variantsContext = useLightboxVariantsSafe();
    40→
    41→  // Use props if provided, otherwise fall back to context
    42→  const activeVariant = activeVariantProp ?? variantsContext.activeVariant;
    43→  const variants = variantsProp ?? variantsContext.variants;
    44→  const readOnly = readOnlyProp ?? core.readOnly;
    45→  const isMakingMainVariant = isMakingMainVariantProp ?? variantsContext.isMakingMainVariant;
    46→  const canMakeMainVariant = canMakeMainVariantProp ?? variantsContext.canMakeMainVariant;
    47→  const onMakeMainVariant = onMakeMainVariantProp ?? variantsContext.handleMakeMainVariant;
    48→
    49→  if (readOnly || !activeVariant || !variants) {
    50→    return null;
    51→  }
    52→
    53→  return (
    54→    <div
    55→      className="flex items-center gap-2 select-none"
    56→      onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
    57→    >
    58→        {/* Main variant badge - only show when multiple variants exist */}
    59→        {variants.length > 1 && activeVariant.is_primary && (
    60→          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-green-500/90 text-white text-sm font-medium shadow-lg">
    61→            <Check className="w-4 h-4" />
    62→            <span>Main variant</span>
    63→          </div>
    64→        )}
    65→        {/* Make main button - only show for non-primary variants */}
    66→        {!activeVariant.is_primary && (
    67→          <Button
    68→            variant="secondary"
    69→            size="sm"
    70→            onClick={onMakeMainVariant}
    71→            disabled={isMakingMainVariant || !canMakeMainVariant}
    72→            className="bg-orange-500/90 hover:bg-orange-600 text-white border-none shadow-lg"
    73→          >
    74→            {isMakingMainVariant ? (
    75→              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin mr-1.5" />
    76→            ) : (
    77→              <Star className="w-4 h-4 mr-1.5" />
    78→            )}
    79→            Make main
    80→          </Button>
    81→        )}
    82→    </div>
    83→  );
    84→};
```

```
1→import React from 'react';
     2→import { Button } from '@/shared/components/ui/button';
     3→import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from '@/shared/components/ui/tooltip';
     4→import {
     5→  Star,
     6→  Download,
     7→  CheckCircle,
     8→  Loader2,
     9→  ImagePlus,
    10→  Trash2,
    11→  Film,
    12→  ArrowRight,
    13→} from 'lucide-react';
    14→import {
    15→  useLightboxCoreSafe,
    16→  useLightboxMediaSafe,

    18→import { useImageEditSafe } from '../contexts/ImageEditContext';
    19→
    20→// ============================================================================
    21→// TOP RIGHT CONTROLS - Download & Delete
    22→// ============================================================================
    23→
    24→interface TopRightControlsProps {
    25→  showDownload: boolean;
    26→  handleDownload?: () => Promise<void>;
    27→  isDownloading?: boolean;
    28→  onDelete?: (id: string) => void;
    29→  handleDelete?: () => void;
    30→  isDeleting?: string | null;
    31→}
    32→
    33→export const TopRightControls: React.FC<TopRightControlsProps> = ({
    34→  showDownload,
    35→  handleDownload,
    36→  isDownloading,
    37→  onDelete,
    38→  handleDelete,
    39→  isDeleting,
    40→}) => {
    41→  // Get shared state from context
    42→  const { readOnly, actualGenerationId } = useLightboxCoreSafe();
    43→  const { isVideo } = useLightboxMediaSafe();
    44→
    45→  return (
    46→    <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 flex items-center space-x-2 z-[70]">
    47→      {/* Download Button - Keep visible in edit mode */}
    48→      {/* Guard: only render if handleDownload is provided (parent must supply it) */}
    49→      {showDownload && !readOnly && handleDownload && (
    50→        <Tooltip>
    51→          <TooltipTrigger asChild>
    52→            <Button
    53→              variant="secondary"
    54→              size="sm"
    55→              onClick={(e) => {
    56→                e.stopPropagation();
    57→                handleDownload();
    58→              }}
    59→              disabled={isDownloading}
    60→              className="bg-black/50 hover:bg-black/70 text-white"
    61→            >
    62→              {isDownloading ? (
    63→                <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
    64→              ) : (
    65→                <Download className="h-4 w-4" />
    66→              )}
    67→            </Button>
    68→          </TooltipTrigger>
    69→          <TooltipContent className="z-[100001]">
    70→            {isDownloading ? 'Downloading...' : `Download ${isVideo ? 'video' : 'image'}`}
    71→          </TooltipContent>
    72→        </Tooltip>
    73→      )}
    74→
    75→      {/* Delete Button - Keep visible in edit mode */}
    76→      {onDelete && !readOnly && !isVideo && handleDelete && (
    77→        <Tooltip>
    78→          <TooltipTrigger asChild>
    79→            <Button
    80→              variant="destructive"
    81→              size="sm"
    82→              onClick={(e) => {
    83→                e.stopPropagation();
    84→                handleDelete();
    85→              }}
    86→              disabled={isDeleting === actualGenerationId}
    87→              className="bg-red-600/80 hover:bg-red-600 text-white"
    88→            >
    89→              {isDeleting === actualGenerationId ? (
    90→                <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
    91→              ) : (
    92→                <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
    93→              )}
    94→            </Button>
    95→          </TooltipTrigger>
    96→          <TooltipContent className="z-[100001]">Delete from timeline</TooltipContent>
    97→        </Tooltip>
    98→      )}
    99→    </div>
   100→  );
   101→};
   102→
   103→// ============================================================================
   104→// BOTTOM LEFT CONTROLS - Star button
   105→// ============================================================================
   106→
   107→interface BottomLeftControlsProps {

   110→  toggleStarPending?: boolean;

   112→
   113→export const BottomLeftControls: React.FC<BottomLeftControlsProps> = ({

   128→        onClick={handleToggleStar}
   129→        disabled={toggleStarPending}
   130→        className="transition-colors bg-black/50 hover:bg-black/70 text-white"
   131→      >
   132→        <Star className={`h-4 w-4 ${localStarred ? 'fill-current' : ''}`} />
   133→      </Button>
   134→    </div>
   135→  );
   136→};
   137→
   138→// ============================================================================
   139→// BOTTOM RIGHT CONTROLS - Add to References / Add to Join Clips
   140→// ============================================================================
   141→
   142→interface BottomRightControlsProps {
   143→  localStarred: boolean;
   144→  handleToggleStar: () => void;
   145→  toggleStarPending?: boolean;
   146→  isAddingToReferences: boolean;
   147→  addToReferencesSuccess: boolean;
   148→  handleAddToReferences: () => Promise<void>;
   149→  // Add to Join Clips
   150→  handleAddToJoin?: () => void;
   151→  isAddingToJoin?: boolean;
   152→  addToJoinSuccess?: boolean;
   153→  onGoToJoin?: () => void;
   154→}
   155→
   156→export const BottomRightControls: React.FC<BottomRightControlsProps> = ({
   157→  isAddingToReferences,
   158→  addToReferencesSuccess,
   159→  handleAddToReferences,
   160→  handleAddToJoin,
   161→  isAddingToJoin,
   162→  addToJoinSuccess,
   163→  onGoToJoin,
   164→}) => {
   165→  // Get shared state from context
   166→  const { readOnly, selectedProjectId } = useLightboxCoreSafe();
   167→  const { isVideo } = useLightboxMediaSafe();
   168→
   169→  // Keep visible in edit mode - users can add to references while editing
   170→  return (
   171→    <div className="absolute bottom-4 right-4 flex items-center space-x-2 z-10">
   172→      {/* Add to References Button */}
   173→      {!readOnly && !isVideo && selectedProjectId && (
   174→        <Tooltip>
   175→          <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   176→            <Button
   177→              variant="secondary"
   178→              size="sm"
   179→              onClick={handleAddToReferences}
   180→              disabled={isAddingToReferences || addToReferencesSuccess}
   181→              className={`transition-colors ${
   182→                addToReferencesSuccess
   183→                  ? 'bg-green-600/80 hover:bg-green-600 text-white'
   184→                  : 'bg-black/50 hover:bg-black/70 text-white'
   185→              }`}
   186→            >
   187→              {isAddingToReferences ? (
   188→                <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
   189→              ) : addToReferencesSuccess ? (
   190→                <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
   191→              ) : (
   192→                <ImagePlus className="h-4 w-4" />
   193→              )}
   194→            </Button>
   195→          </TooltipTrigger>
   196→          <TooltipContent className="z-[100001]">
   197→            {isAddingToReferences ? 'Adding...' : addToReferencesSuccess ? 'Added!' : 'Add to references'}
   198→          </TooltipContent>
   199→        </Tooltip>
   200→      )}
   201→
   202→      {/* Add to Join Clips Button (videos only) */}
   203→      {!readOnly && isVideo && handleAddToJoin && (
   204→        <Tooltip>
   205→          <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   206→            <Button
   207→              variant="secondary"
   208→              size="sm"
   209→              onClick={addToJoinSuccess && onGoToJoin ? onGoToJoin : handleAddToJoin}
   210→              disabled={isAddingToJoin}
   211→              className={`transition-colors ${
   212→                addToJoinSuccess
   213→                  ? 'bg-green-600/80 hover:bg-green-600 text-white'
   214→                  : 'bg-black/50 hover:bg-black/70 text-white'
   215→              }`}
   216→            >
   217→              {isAddingToJoin ? (
   218→                <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
   219→              ) : addToJoinSuccess ? (
   220→                <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
   221→              ) : (
   222→                <Film className="h-4 w-4" />
   223→              )}
   224→            </Button>
   225→          </TooltipTrigger>
   226→          <TooltipContent className="z-[100001]">
   227→            {isAddingToJoin ? 'Adding...' : addToJoinSuccess ? 'Added! Go to Join Clips' : 'Add to Join Clips'}
   228→          </TooltipContent>
   229→        </Tooltip>
   230→      )}
   231→    </div>
   232→  );
   233→};
   234→
```
