1→/**
     2→ * InfoPanel Component
     3→ *
     4→ * Unified info panel for both desktop and mobile layouts.
     5→ * Shows task details, variants, and Info/Edit toggle controls.
     6→ *
     7→ * Uses context hooks for shared state (core, media, edit, variants).
     8→ * Receives only layout-specific and deeply-nested props.
     9→ */
    10→
    11→import React, { useState } from 'react';
    12→import { Button } from '@/shared/components/ui/button';
    13→import { SegmentedControl, SegmentedControlItem } from '@/shared/components/ui/segmented-control';
    14→import { X, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
    15→import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/utils';
    16→
    17→import { TaskDetailsPanelWrapper } from './TaskDetailsPanelWrapper';
    18→import { VariantSelector } from '@/shared/components/VariantSelector';
    19→import { VariantBadge } from '@/shared/components/VariantBadge';
    20→import {
    21→  useLightboxCoreSafe,
    22→  useLightboxMediaSafe,
    23→  useLightboxVariantsSafe,
    24→} from '../contexts/LightboxStateContext';
    25→import { useImageEditSafe } from '../contexts/ImageEditContext';
    26→import { useVideoEditSafe } from '../contexts/VideoEditContext';
    27→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/types/shots';
    28→import type { TaskDetailsData } from '../types';
    29→
    30→export interface InfoPanelProps {
    31→  /** Layout variant */
    32→  variant: 'desktop' | 'mobile';
    33→
    34→  // Header toggle - only specialized props now
    35→  showImageEditTools: boolean;
    36→
    37→  // TaskDetailsPanelWrapper props (deeply nested data)
    38→  taskDetailsData: TaskDetailsData | undefined;
    39→  derivedItems: GenerationRow[];
    40→  derivedGenerations: GenerationRow[] | null;
    41→  paginatedDerived: GenerationRow[];
    42→  derivedPage: number;
    43→  derivedTotalPages: number;
    44→  onSetDerivedPage: (page: number | ((prev: number) => number)) => void;
    45→  onNavigateToGeneration?: (generationId: string, derivedContext?: string[]) => Promise<void>;
    46→  currentMediaId: string;
    47→  currentShotId?: string;
    48→  replaceImages: boolean;
    49→  onReplaceImagesChange: (value: boolean) => void;
    50→  onSwitchToPrimary?: () => void;
    51→
    52→  /** Task ID for copy functionality (fallback when not in taskDetailsData) */
    53→  taskId?: string | null;
    54→}
    55→
    56→export const InfoPanel: React.FC<InfoPanelProps> = ({
    57→  variant,
    58→  // Header props
    59→  showImageEditTools,
    60→  // TaskDetails props
    61→  taskDetailsData,
    62→  derivedItems,
    63→  derivedGenerations,
    64→  paginatedDerived,
    65→  derivedPage,
    66→  derivedTotalPages,
    67→  onSetDerivedPage,
    68→  onNavigateToGeneration,
    69→  currentMediaId,
    70→  currentShotId,
    71→  replaceImages,
    72→  onReplaceImagesChange,
    73→  onSwitchToPrimary,
    74→  // Task ID fallback
    75→  taskId: taskIdProp,
    76→}) => {
    77→  const isMobile = variant === 'mobile';
    78→
    79→  // ========================================
    80→  // CONTEXT STATE (no longer from props)
    81→  // ========================================
    82→  const { onClose, readOnly } = useLightboxCoreSafe();
    83→  const { isVideo } = useLightboxMediaSafe();
    84→  const { isInpaintMode, handleEnterInpaintMode, handleExitInpaintMode } = useImageEditSafe();
    85→  const { isInVideoEditMode, handleEnterVideoEditMode, handleExitVideoEditMode } = useVideoEditSafe();
    86→  const {
    87→    variants,
    88→    activeVariant,
    89→    primaryVariant,
    90→    handleVariantSelect: onVariantSelect,
    91→    handleMakePrimary: onMakePrimary,
    92→    isLoadingVariants,
    93→    variantsSectionRef,
    94→    pendingTaskCount,
    95→    unviewedVariantCount,
    96→    onMarkAllViewed,
    97→    handlePromoteToGeneration: onPromoteToGeneration,
    98→    isPromoting,
    99→    handleDeleteVariant: onDeleteVariant,
   100→    onLoadVariantSettings,
   101→    onLoadVariantImages,
   102→    currentSegmentImages,
   103→  } = useLightboxVariantsSafe();
   104→
   105→  const hasVariants = variants && variants.length >= 1;
   106→  const [idCopied, setIdCopied] = useState(false);
   107→
   108→  // Get task ID for copy functionality - use prop as fallback for videos
   109→  const taskId = taskDetailsData?.taskId || taskIdProp;
   110→
   111→  // Responsive styles
   112→  const variantsMaxHeight = isMobile ? 'max-h-[120px]' : 'max-h-[200px]';
   113→
   114→  // Render the Info/Edit toggle for images
   115→  const renderImageToggle = () => {
   116→    if (!showImageEditTools || readOnly || isVideo) return null;
   117→
   118→    return (
   119→      <SegmentedControl
   120→        value={isInpaintMode ? 'edit' : 'info'}
   121→        onValueChange={(value) => {
   122→          if (value === 'info' && isInpaintMode) {
   123→            handleExitInpaintMode();
   124→          } else if (value === 'edit' && !isInpaintMode) {
   125→            handleEnterInpaintMode();
   126→          }
   127→        }}
   128→      >
   129→        <SegmentedControlItem value="info">Info</SegmentedControlItem>
   130→        <SegmentedControlItem value="edit">Edit</SegmentedControlItem>
   131→      </SegmentedControl>
   132→    );
   133→  };
   134→
   135→  // Render the Info/Edit toggle for videos
   136→  const renderVideoToggle = () => {
   137→    if (!isVideo || readOnly) return null;
   138→
   139→    return (
   140→      <SegmentedControl
   141→        value={isInVideoEditMode ? 'edit' : 'info'}
   142→        onValueChange={(value) => {
   143→          if (value === 'info' && isInVideoEditMode) {
   144→            handleExitVideoEditMode();
   145→          } else if (value === 'edit' && !isInVideoEditMode) {
   146→            handleEnterVideoEditMode();
   147→          }
   148→        }}
   149→      >
   150→        <SegmentedControlItem value="info">Info</SegmentedControlItem>
   151→        <SegmentedControlItem value="edit">Edit</SegmentedControlItem>
   152→      </SegmentedControl>
   153→    );
   154→  };
   155→
   156→  // Handle ID copy with simple approach that works on all devices
   157→  // Set state immediately (synchronously) for instant feedback, don't wait for clipboard promise
   158→  const handleCopyId = () => {
   159→    if (!taskId) return;
   160→    setIdCopied(true);
   161→    setTimeout(() => setIdCopied(false), 2000);
   162→    // Copy to clipboard async - don't block on it
   163→    navigator.clipboard.writeText(taskId).catch(() => {
   164→      // Silently fail - we already showed feedback
   165→    });
   166→  };
   167→
   168→  // Render the header - consistent single row layout for both mobile and desktop
   169→  const renderHeader = () => (
   170→    <div className={cn(
   171→      "flex-shrink-0 border-b border-border bg-background",
   172→      isMobile ? "sticky top-0 z-[80] px-3 py-2" : "p-4"
   173→    )}>
   174→      {/* Single row: ID + variants on left, toggle + close on right */}
   175→      <div className={cn(
   176→        "flex items-center justify-between",
   177→        isMobile ? "gap-2" : "gap-3"
   178→      )}>
   179→        {/* Left side - copy id + variants link */}
   180→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   181→          {taskId && (
   182→            <button
   183→              onClick={handleCopyId}
   184→              className={cn(
   185→                "px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded transition-colors touch-manipulation",
   186→                idCopied
   187→                  ? "text-green-400 bg-green-400/10"
   188→                  : "text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-700"
   189→              )}
   190→            >
   191→              {idCopied ? 'copied' : 'id'}
   192→            </button>
   193→          )}
   194→          {hasVariants && (
   195→            <button
   196→              onClick={() => variantsSectionRef?.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' })}
   197→              className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded bg-muted/50 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors touch-manipulation"
   198→            >
   199→              <span>{variants.length} variants</span>
   200→              <svg className="w-2.5 h-2.5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
   201→                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M19 14l-7 7m0 0l-7-7m7 7V3" />
   202→              </svg>
   203→            </button>
   204→          )}
   205→          {pendingTaskCount > 0 ? (
   206→            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded bg-primary/10 text-primary">
   207→              <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" />
   208→              <span>{pendingTaskCount} pending</span>
   209→            </div>
   210→          ) : variants && variants.length > 1 && unviewedVariantCount > 0 ? (
   211→            <VariantBadge
   212→              variant="inline"
   213→              derivedCount={variants.length}
   214→              unviewedVariantCount={unviewedVariantCount}
   215→              hasUnviewedVariants={true}
   216→              tooltipSide="bottom"
   217→              onMarkAllViewed={onMarkAllViewed}
   218→              onClick={() => variantsSectionRef?.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' })}
   219→            />
   220→          ) : null}
   221→        </div>
   222→
   223→        {/* Right side - toggles and close button */}
   224→        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
   225→          {renderImageToggle()}
   226→          {renderVideoToggle()}
   227→          <Button
   228→            variant="ghost"
   229→            size="sm"
   230→            onClick={onClose}
   231→            className={cn("p-0 hover:bg-muted", isMobile ? "h-7 w-7" : "h-8 w-8")}
   232→          >
   233→            <X className={cn(isMobile ? "h-3.5 w-3.5" : "h-4 w-4")} />
   234→          </Button>
   235→        </div>
   236→      </div>
   237→    </div>
   238→  );
   239→
   240→  // Render task details wrapper
   241→  const renderTaskDetails = () => (
   242→    <TaskDetailsPanelWrapper
   243→      taskDetailsData={taskDetailsData}
   244→      derivedItems={derivedItems}
   245→      derivedGenerations={derivedGenerations}
   246→      paginatedDerived={paginatedDerived}
   247→      derivedPage={derivedPage}
   248→      derivedTotalPages={derivedTotalPages}
   249→      onSetDerivedPage={onSetDerivedPage}
   250→      onNavigateToGeneration={onNavigateToGeneration}
   251→      onVariantSelect={onVariantSelect}
   252→      currentMediaId={currentMediaId}
   253→      currentShotId={currentShotId}
   254→      replaceImages={replaceImages}
   255→      onReplaceImagesChange={onReplaceImagesChange}
   256→      onClose={onClose}
   257→      variant={variant}
   258→      activeVariant={activeVariant}
   259→      primaryVariant={primaryVariant}
   260→      onSwitchToPrimary={onSwitchToPrimary}
   261→    />
   262→  );
   263→
   264→  // Render variants section - matches EditPanelLayout styling
   265→  const renderVariants = () => {
   266→    if (!hasVariants) return null;
   267→
   268→    // Match EditPanelLayout: border-t, consistent padding
   269→    const variantPadding = isMobile ? 'pt-2 mt-2 px-3 pb-2' : 'pt-4 mt-4 p-6';
   270→
   271→    return (
   272→      <div
   273→        ref={variantsSectionRef}
   274→        className={cn("border-t border-border", variantPadding)}
   275→      >
   276→        <VariantSelector
   277→          variants={variants}
   278→          activeVariantId={activeVariant?.id || null}
   279→          onVariantSelect={onVariantSelect}
   280→          onMakePrimary={onMakePrimary}
   281→          isLoading={isLoadingVariants}
   282→          onPromoteToGeneration={onPromoteToGeneration}
   283→          isPromoting={isPromoting}
   284→          onDeleteVariant={onDeleteVariant}
   285→          onLoadVariantSettings={onLoadVariantSettings}
   286→          onLoadVariantImages={onLoadVariantImages}
   287→          currentSegmentImages={currentSegmentImages}
   288→          readOnly={readOnly}
   289→        />
   290→      </div>
   291→    );
   292→  };
   293→
   294→  // Both desktop and mobile: variants inside scroll area (matches EditPanelLayout)
   295→  return (
   296→    <div className={cn("w-full flex flex-col", !isMobile && "h-full")}>
   297→      {renderHeader()}
   298→
   299→      {/* Scrollable content area - contains both task details and variants */}
   300→      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto min-h-0 overscroll-none">
   301→        {renderTaskDetails()}
   302→        {renderVariants()}
   303→      </div>
   304→    </div>
   305→  );
   306→};

1→/**
     2→ * VariantSelector Component
     3→ *
     4→ * Displays a grid of clickable variant thumbnails to switch between variants.
     5→ * Shows which variant is primary and which is currently active.
     6→ * Shows variant relationships (what it's based on / what's based on it).
     7→ * Allows filtering by relationship and making the current variant primary.
     8→ */
     9→
    10→import React, { useState, useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';
    11→import { Check, Loader2, ArrowDown, ArrowUp, X, ImagePlus, Star } from 'lucide-react';
    12→import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/utils';

    18→import { usePrefetchTaskData, usePrefetchTaskById } from '@/shared/hooks/useTaskPrefetch';
    19→import { usePublicLoras } from '@/shared/hooks/useResources';
    20→import { ChunkLoadErrorBoundary } from '@/shared/components/ChunkLoadErrorBoundary';
    21→// Lazy load LineageGifModal since it's only opened on demand
    22→const LazyLineageGifModal = React.lazy(() =>
    23→  import('@/shared/components/LineageGifModal').then(module => ({
    24→    default: module.LineageGifModal
    25→  }))
    26→);
    27→import { getLineageDepth } from '@/shared/hooks/useLineageChain';
    28→import { getSourceTaskId } from '@/shared/lib/taskIdHelpers';
    29→import { useToggleVariantStar } from '@/shared/hooks/useToggleVariantStar';
    30→import type { GenerationVariant } from '@/shared/hooks/useVariants';
    31→import type { RelationshipFilter, CurrentSegmentImagesData } from './utils';
    32→import { VariantGrid } from './components/VariantGrid';
    33→import { MobileInfoModal } from './components/MobileInfoModal';
    34→
    35→/**
    36→ * Props for VariantSelector component
    37→ */
    38→interface VariantSelectorProps {
    39→  /** List of variants */
    40→  variants: GenerationVariant[];
    41→  /** Currently active variant ID */
    42→  activeVariantId: string | null;
    43→  /** Handler for variant selection */
    44→  onVariantSelect: (variantId: string) => void;
    45→  /** Handler to make a variant primary */
    46→  onMakePrimary?: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
    47→  /** Whether component is loading */

    49→  /** Handler to promote variant to a standalone generation */
    50→  onPromoteToGeneration?: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
    51→  /** Whether a promotion is currently in progress */
    52→  isPromoting?: boolean;
    53→  /** Handler to load a variant's settings into the regenerate form */
    54→  onLoadVariantSettings?: (variantParams: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
    55→  /** Handler to delete a variant (not available for primary variant) */
    56→  onDeleteVariant?: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
    57→  /** Read-only mode - hides action buttons (Make Primary, Promote, Delete) */
    58→  readOnly?: boolean;
    59→  /** Handler to load a variant's source images onto the timeline */
    60→  onLoadVariantImages?: (variant: GenerationVariant) => void;
    61→  /** Current segment images data for comparison */
    62→  currentSegmentImages?: CurrentSegmentImagesData;
    63→}
    64→
    65→export const VariantSelector: React.FC<VariantSelectorProps> = ({
    66→  variants,
    67→  activeVariantId,
    68→  onVariantSelect,
    69→  onMakePrimary,
    70→  isLoading = false,
    71→  onPromoteToGeneration,
    72→  isPromoting = false,
    73→  onLoadVariantSettings,
    74→  onDeleteVariant,
    75→  readOnly = false,
    76→  onLoadVariantImages,
    77→  currentSegmentImages,
    78→}) => {
    79→  const [isMakingPrimary, setIsMakingPrimary] = useState(false);
    80→  const [localIsPromoting, setLocalIsPromoting] = useState(false);
    81→  const [promoteSuccess, setPromoteSuccess] = useState(false);
    82→  const [relationshipFilter, setRelationshipFilter] = useState<RelationshipFilter>('all');
    83→  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(0);
    84→  const [loadedSettingsVariantId, setLoadedSettingsVariantId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    85→  const [copiedVariantId, setCopiedVariantId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    86→  const [loadedImagesVariantId, setLoadedImagesVariantId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    87→  const deleteOperation = useAsyncOperationMap();
    88→  const [lineageGifVariantId, setLineageGifVariantId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    89→  const [mobileInfoVariantId, setMobileInfoVariantId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    90→  const [variantLineageDepth, setVariantLineageDepth] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
    91→  const isMobile = useIsMobile();
    92→  const { data: availableLoras } = usePublicLoras();
    93→  const { toggleStar } = useToggleVariantStar();

    95→  const starredCount = useMemo(() => variants.filter(v => v.starred).length, [variants]);
    96→
    97→  const checkedLineageIdsRef = React.useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
    98→  const prefetchTaskData = usePrefetchTaskData();
    99→  const prefetchTaskById = usePrefetchTaskById();
   100→
   101→  // Check lineage depth lazily on hover
   102→  const checkLineageDepthOnHover = useCallback(async (variantId: string) => {
   103→    if (checkedLineageIdsRef.current.has(variantId)) return;
   104→    checkedLineageIdsRef.current.add(variantId);
   105→    try {
   106→      const depth = await getLineageDepth(variantId);
   107→      setVariantLineageDepth(prev => ({ ...prev, [variantId]: depth }));
   108→    } catch {
   109→      setVariantLineageDepth(prev => ({ ...prev, [variantId]: 0 }));
   110→    }
   111→  }, []);
   112→
   113→  const handleVariantMouseEnter = useCallback((variant: GenerationVariant) => {
   114→    if (isMobile) return;
   115→    checkLineageDepthOnHover(variant.id);
   116→
   117→    const variantParams = variant.params;
   118→    const validSourceTaskId = getSourceTaskId(variantParams);
   119→
   120→    if (validSourceTaskId) {
   121→      console.log('[VariantPrefetch] Prefetching source task:', validSourceTaskId.substring(0, 8), 'for variant:', variant.id.substring(0, 8));
   122→      prefetchTaskById(validSourceTaskId);
   123→    } else {
   124→      console.log('[VariantPrefetch] No source_task_id, prefetching via generation:', variant.generation_id.substring(0, 8));
   125→      prefetchTaskData(variant.generation_id);
   126→    }
   127→  }, [isMobile, prefetchTaskData, prefetchTaskById, checkLineageDepthOnHover]);
   128→
   129→  // Calculate variant relationships
   130→  const { parentVariants, childVariants, relationshipMap } = useMemo(() => {
   131→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] Computing relationships:');
   132→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] variantsCount:', variants.length);
   133→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] activeVariantId:', activeVariantId);
   134→
   135→    const parents = new Set<string>();
   136→    const children = new Set<string>();
   137→    const relMap: Record<string, { isParent: boolean; isChild: boolean }> = {};
   138→
   139→    variants.forEach(variant => {
   140→      relMap[variant.id] = { isParent: false, isChild: false };
   141→    });
   142→
   143→    const activeVar = variants.find(variant => variant.id === activeVariantId);
   144→    if (!activeVar) {
   145→      console.log('[VariantRelationship] No active variant found');
   146→      return { parentVariants: parents, childVariants: children, relationshipMap: relMap };
   147→    }
   148→
   149→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] Active variant:');
   150→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] - id:', activeVar.id);
   151→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] - variant_type:', activeVar.variant_type);
   152→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] - params:', JSON.stringify(activeVar.params));
   153→
   154→    const activeSourceVariantId = activeVar.params?.source_variant_id as string | undefined;
   155→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] Active variant source_variant_id:', activeSourceVariantId);
   156→
   157→    if (activeSourceVariantId) {
   158→      const parentVariant = variants.find(variant => variant.id === activeSourceVariantId);
   159→      if (parentVariant) {
   160→        console.log('[VariantRelationship] Found parent variant:', parentVariant.id);
   161→        parents.add(parentVariant.id);
   162→        relMap[parentVariant.id].isParent = true;
   163→      } else {
   164→        console.log('[VariantRelationship] Parent variant not in variants list');
   165→      }
   166→    }
   167→
   168→    variants.forEach(variant => {
   169→      const sourceId = variant.params?.source_variant_id as string | undefined;
   170→      if (sourceId) {
   171→        console.log('[VariantRelationship] Variant', variant.id.substring(0, 8), 'has source_variant_id:', sourceId);
   172→      }
   173→      if (sourceId === activeVariantId) {
   174→        console.log('[VariantRelationship] Found child variant:', variant.id);
   175→        children.add(variant.id);
   176→        relMap[variant.id].isChild = true;
   177→      }
   178→    });
   179→
   180→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] Result:');
   181→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] - parentCount:', parents.size);
   182→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] - childCount:', children.size);
   183→
   184→    return { parentVariants: parents, childVariants: children, relationshipMap: relMap };
   185→  }, [variants, activeVariantId]);
   186→
   187→  // Sort variants with primary first
   188→  const sortedVariants = useMemo(() => {
   189→    return [...variants].sort((a, b) => {
   190→      if (a.is_primary && !b.is_primary) return -1;
   191→      if (!a.is_primary && b.is_primary) return 1;
   192→      return 0;
   193→    });
   194→  }, [variants]);
   195→
   196→  // Filter variants based on relationship filter
   197→  const filteredVariants = useMemo(() => {
   198→    if (relationshipFilter === 'all') return sortedVariants;
   199→    if (relationshipFilter === 'parents') {
   200→      return sortedVariants.filter(variant => parentVariants.has(variant.id) || variant.id === activeVariantId);
   201→    }
   202→    if (relationshipFilter === 'children') {
   203→      return sortedVariants.filter(variant => childVariants.has(variant.id) || variant.id === activeVariantId);
   204→    }
   205→    if (relationshipFilter === 'starred') {
   206→      return sortedVariants.filter(variant => variant.starred || variant.id === activeVariantId);
   207→    }
   208→    return sortedVariants;
   209→  }, [sortedVariants, relationshipFilter, parentVariants, childVariants, activeVariantId]);
   210→
   211→  // Reset page when filter changes
   212→  React.useEffect(() => {
   213→    setCurrentPage(0);
   214→  }, [relationshipFilter]);
   215→
   216→  const hasRelationships = parentVariants.size > 0 || childVariants.size > 0;
   217→  const showFilterRow = hasRelationships || starredCount > 0;
   218→
   219→  // Don't show if no variants at all
   220→  if (!isLoading && variants.length === 0) {
   221→    return null;
   222→  }
   223→
   224→  const activeVariant = variants.find(variant => variant.id === activeVariantId);
   225→  const isViewingNonPrimary = activeVariant && !activeVariant.is_primary;
   226→
   227→  const handleMakePrimary = async () => {
   228→    if (!activeVariantId || !onMakePrimary) return;
   229→    setIsMakingPrimary(true);
   230→    try {
   231→      await onMakePrimary(activeVariantId);
   232→    } finally {
   233→      setIsMakingPrimary(false);
   234→    }
   235→  };
   236→
   237→  const handlePromoteToGeneration = async () => {
   238→    if (!activeVariantId || !onPromoteToGeneration) return;
   239→    setLocalIsPromoting(true);
   240→    setPromoteSuccess(false);
   241→    try {
   242→      await onPromoteToGeneration(activeVariantId);
   243→      setPromoteSuccess(true);
   244→      setTimeout(() => setPromoteSuccess(false), 2000);
   245→    } finally {
   246→      setLocalIsPromoting(false);
   247→    }
   248→  };
   249→
   250→  const handleCopyId = (variantId: string) => {

   252→    setCopiedVariantId(variantId);
   253→    setTimeout(() => setCopiedVariantId(null), 2000);
   254→  };
   255→
   256→  const handleLoadSettings = (variant: GenerationVariant) => {
   257→    if (!onLoadVariantSettings) return;
   258→    onLoadVariantSettings(variant.params as Record<string, unknown>);
   259→    setLoadedSettingsVariantId(variant.id);
   260→    setTimeout(() => setLoadedSettingsVariantId(null), 2000);

   262→
   263→  const handleLoadImages = (variant: GenerationVariant) => {
   264→    if (!onLoadVariantImages) return;
   265→    onLoadVariantImages(variant);
   266→    setLoadedImagesVariantId(variant.id);

   269→

   295→    );
   296→  }
   297→
   298→  return (

   302→        {/* Header section */}
   303→        <div className={cn("flex gap-2", isMobile ? "flex-col" : "items-center justify-between")}>

   307→            {!readOnly && (

   314→                      variant="outline"

   317→                      className={cn(

   330→                    </Button>

   337→              {isViewingNonPrimary && onMakePrimary ? (

   346→                    >

   352→                      Make main

   400→                        className={cn(
   401→                          'flex items-center gap-0.5 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] transition-colors',
   402→                          relationshipFilter === 'children'
   403→                            ? 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400 border border-purple-500/50'
   404→                            : 'bg-muted/50 text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted',
   405→                          childVariants.size === 0 && 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed'
   406→                        )}
   407→                        disabled={childVariants.size === 0}
   408→                      >
   409→                        <ArrowDown className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />
   410→                        <span>Based on this ({childVariants.size})</span>
   411→                      </button>
   412→                    </TooltipTrigger>
   413→                    <TooltipContent side="top" className="z-[100001]">
   414→                      <p>Show variants based on this one</p>
   415→                    </TooltipContent>
   416→                  </Tooltip>
   417→                </>
   418→              )}
   419→
   420→              {starredCount > 0 && (
   421→                <Tooltip>
   422→                  <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   423→                    <button
   424→                      onClick={() => setRelationshipFilter(relationshipFilter === 'starred' ? 'all' : 'starred')}
   425→                      className={cn(
   426→                        'flex items-center gap-0.5 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] transition-colors',
   427→                        relationshipFilter === 'starred'
   428→                          ? 'bg-yellow-500/20 text-yellow-400 border border-yellow-500/50'
   429→                          : 'bg-muted/50 text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted'
   430→                      )}
   431→                    >
   432→                      <Star className={cn('w-2.5 h-2.5', relationshipFilter === 'starred' && 'fill-current')} />
   433→                      <span>Starred ({starredCount})</span>
   434→                    </button>
   435→                  </TooltipTrigger>
   436→                  <TooltipContent side="top" className="z-[100001]">
   437→                    <p>Show starred variants</p>
   438→                  </TooltipContent>
   439→                </Tooltip>
   440→              )}
   441→
   442→              {relationshipFilter !== 'all' && (
   443→                <button
   444→                  onClick={() => setRelationshipFilter('all')}
   445→                  className="p-0.5 rounded hover:bg-muted text-muted-foreground"
   446→                >
   447→                  <X className="w-3 h-3" />
   448→                </button>
   449→              )}
   450→            </div>
   451→          )}
   452→        </div>
   453→
   454→        {/* Grid with pagination */}
   455→        <VariantGrid
   456→          filteredVariants={filteredVariants}
   457→          allVariants={variants}
   458→          activeVariantId={activeVariantId}
   459→          currentPage={currentPage}
   460→          onPageChange={setCurrentPage}
   461→          isMobile={isMobile}
   462→          readOnly={readOnly}
   463→          availableLoras={availableLoras}
   464→          relationshipMap={relationshipMap}
   465→          variantLineageDepth={variantLineageDepth}
   466→          copiedVariantId={copiedVariantId}
   467→          loadedSettingsVariantId={loadedSettingsVariantId}
   468→          onVariantSelect={onVariantSelect}
   469→          onMakePrimary={onMakePrimary}
   470→          onDeleteVariant={onDeleteVariant ? handleDeleteVariant : undefined}
   471→          onLoadVariantSettings={onLoadVariantSettings}
   472→          onToggleStar={handleToggleStar}
   473→          onMouseEnter={handleVariantMouseEnter}
   474→          onShowMobileInfo={setMobileInfoVariantId}
   475→          onShowLineageGif={setLineageGifVariantId}
   476→          onCopyId={handleCopyId}
   477→          onLoadSettings={handleLoadSettings}
   478→          onLoadImages={onLoadVariantImages ? handleLoadImages : undefined}
   479→          currentSegmentImages={currentSegmentImages}
   480→          loadedImagesVariantId={loadedImagesVariantId}
   481→          isDeleteLoading={(id) => deleteOperation.isLoading(id)}
   482→        />
   483→      </div>
   484→    </TooltipProvider>
   485→
   486→    {/* Lineage GIF Modal - lazy loaded since only opened on demand */}
   487→    <ChunkLoadErrorBoundary>
   488→      <React.Suspense fallback={null}>
   489→        <LazyLineageGifModal
   490→          open={!!lineageGifVariantId}
   491→          onClose={() => setLineageGifVariantId(null)}
   492→          variantId={lineageGifVariantId}
   493→        />
   494→      </React.Suspense>
   495→    </ChunkLoadErrorBoundary>
   496→
   497→    {/* Mobile variant info modal */}
   498→    {isMobile && mobileInfoVariant && (
   499→      <MobileInfoModal
   500→        variant={mobileInfoVariant}
   501→        activeVariantId={activeVariantId}
   502→        availableLoras={availableLoras}
   503→        readOnly={readOnly}
   504→        onClose={() => setMobileInfoVariantId(null)}
   505→        onMakePrimary={onMakePrimary}
   506→        onLoadVariantSettings={onLoadVariantSettings}
   507→        onLoadImages={onLoadVariantImages ? handleLoadImages : undefined}
   508→        currentSegmentImages={currentSegmentImages}
   509→        loadedImagesVariantId={loadedImagesVariantId}
   510→      />
   511→    )}
   512→    </>
   513→  );
   514→};
   515→

1→/**
     2→ * VariantGrid Component
     3→ *
     4→ * Renders the variant grid with pagination controls.
     5→ * Handles layout (3 cols on mobile, 4 on desktop) and page navigation.
     6→ */
     7→
     8→import React from 'react';
     9→import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
    10→import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/utils';
    11→import type { GenerationVariant } from '@/shared/hooks/useVariants';
    12→import type { LoraModel } from '@/shared/components/LoraSelectorModal';
    13→import type { CurrentSegmentImagesData } from '../utils';
    14→import { VariantCard } from './VariantCard';
    15→
    16→const ITEMS_PER_PAGE = 20;
    17→
    18→interface VariantGridProps {
    19→  filteredVariants: GenerationVariant[];
    20→  allVariants: GenerationVariant[];
    21→  activeVariantId: string | null;
    22→  currentPage: number;
    23→  onPageChange: (page: number) => void;
    24→  isMobile: boolean;
    25→  readOnly: boolean;
    26→  availableLoras?: LoraModel[];
    27→  relationshipMap: Record<string, { isParent: boolean; isChild: boolean }>;
    28→  variantLineageDepth: Record<string, number>;
    29→  copiedVariantId: string | null;
    30→  loadedSettingsVariantId: string | null;
    31→  // Callbacks
    32→  onVariantSelect: (variantId: string) => void;
    33→  onMakePrimary?: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
    34→  onDeleteVariant?: (variantId: string) => void;
    35→  onLoadVariantSettings?: (variantParams: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
    36→  onToggleStar?: (variantId: string, starred: boolean) => void;
    37→  onMouseEnter: (variant: GenerationVariant) => void;
    38→  onShowMobileInfo: (variantId: string) => void;
    39→  onShowLineageGif: (variantId: string) => void;
    40→  onCopyId: (variantId: string) => void;
    41→  onLoadSettings: (variant: GenerationVariant) => void;
    42→  onLoadImages?: (variant: GenerationVariant) => void;
    43→  currentSegmentImages?: CurrentSegmentImagesData;
    44→  loadedImagesVariantId: string | null;
    45→  isDeleteLoading: (variantId: string) => boolean;
    46→}
    47→
    48→export const VariantGrid: React.FC<VariantGridProps> = ({
    49→  filteredVariants,
    50→  allVariants,
    51→  activeVariantId,
    52→  currentPage,
    53→  onPageChange,
    54→  isMobile,
    55→  readOnly,
    56→  availableLoras,
    57→  relationshipMap,
    58→  variantLineageDepth,
    59→  copiedVariantId,
    60→  loadedSettingsVariantId,
    61→  onVariantSelect,
    62→  onMakePrimary,
    63→  onDeleteVariant,
    64→  onLoadVariantSettings,
    65→  onToggleStar,
    66→  onMouseEnter,
    67→  onShowMobileInfo,
    68→  onShowLineageGif,
    69→  onCopyId,
    70→  onLoadSettings,
    71→  onLoadImages,
    72→  currentSegmentImages,
    73→  loadedImagesVariantId,
    74→  isDeleteLoading,
    75→}) => {
    76→  const totalPages = Math.ceil(filteredVariants.length / ITEMS_PER_PAGE);
    77→  const start = currentPage * ITEMS_PER_PAGE;
    78→  const paginatedVariants = filteredVariants.slice(start, start + ITEMS_PER_PAGE);
    79→
    80→  return (
    81→    <>
    82→      {/* Pagination info */}
    83→      {totalPages > 1 && (
    84→        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-1 border-b border-border/30">
    85→          <span className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
    86→            {currentPage * ITEMS_PER_PAGE + 1}-{Math.min((currentPage + 1) * ITEMS_PER_PAGE, filteredVariants.length)} of {filteredVariants.length}
    87→          </span>
    88→          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
    89→            <button
    90→              onClick={() => onPageChange(Math.max(0, currentPage - 1))}
    91→              disabled={currentPage === 0}
    92→              className={cn(
    93→                'p-0.5 rounded hover:bg-muted transition-colors',
    94→                currentPage === 0 && 'opacity-30 cursor-not-allowed'
    95→              )}
    96→            >
    97→              <ChevronLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
    98→            </button>
    99→            <span className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground min-w-[3ch] text-center">
   100→              {currentPage + 1}/{totalPages}
   101→            </span>
   102→            <button
   103→              onClick={() => onPageChange(Math.min(totalPages - 1, currentPage + 1))}
   104→              disabled={currentPage >= totalPages - 1}
   105→              className={cn(
   106→                'p-0.5 rounded hover:bg-muted transition-colors',
   107→                currentPage >= totalPages - 1 && 'opacity-30 cursor-not-allowed'
   108→              )}
   109→            >
   110→              <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   111→            </button>
   112→          </div>
   113→        </div>
   114→      )}
   115→
   116→      {/* Variants grid */}
   117→      <div className={cn(
   118→        "grid gap-1 w-full p-0.5 items-start",
   119→        isMobile ? "grid-cols-3" : "grid-cols-4"
   120→      )}>
   121→        {paginatedVariants.map((variant) => {
   122→          const isActive = variant.id === activeVariantId;
   123→          const isPrimary = variant.is_primary;
   124→          const isParent = relationshipMap[variant.id]?.isParent || false;
   125→          const isChild = relationshipMap[variant.id]?.isChild || false;
   126→
   127→          return (
   128→            <VariantCard
   129→              key={variant.id}
   130→              variant={variant}
   131→              isActive={isActive}
   132→              isPrimary={isPrimary}
   133→              isParent={isParent}
   134→              isChild={isChild}
   135→              activeVariantId={activeVariantId}
   136→              isMobile={isMobile}
   137→              readOnly={readOnly}
   138→              variants={allVariants}
   139→              availableLoras={availableLoras}
   140→              lineageDepth={variantLineageDepth[variant.id] || 0}
   141→              isDeleteLoading={isDeleteLoading(variant.id)}
   142→              copiedVariantId={copiedVariantId}
   143→              loadedSettingsVariantId={loadedSettingsVariantId}
   144→              onVariantSelect={onVariantSelect}
   145→              onMakePrimary={onMakePrimary}
   146→              onDeleteVariant={onDeleteVariant}
   147→              onLoadVariantSettings={onLoadVariantSettings}
   148→              onToggleStar={onToggleStar}
   149→              onMouseEnter={onMouseEnter}
   150→              onShowMobileInfo={onShowMobileInfo}
   151→              onShowLineageGif={onShowLineageGif}
   152→              onCopyId={onCopyId}
   153→              onLoadSettings={onLoadSettings}
   154→              onLoadImages={onLoadImages}
   155→              currentSegmentImages={currentSegmentImages}
   156→              loadedImagesVariantId={loadedImagesVariantId}
   157→            />
   158→          );
   159→        })}
   160→      </div>
   161→
   162→      {/* Empty state when filtering */}
   163→      {filteredVariants.length === 0 && (
   164→        <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground text-center py-2">
   165→          No variants match this filter
   166→        </div>
   167→      )}
   168→    </>
   169→  );
   170→};

1→/**
     2→ * VariantCard Component
     3→ *
     4→ * Renders an individual variant thumbnail with:
     5→ * - Clickable thumbnail with status rings (active, primary, parent, child)
     6→ * - NEW badge or time-ago indicator
     7→ * - Desktop: Info button in bottom-right corner (appears on card hover) with HoverCard showing
     8→ *   full details and actions (Make Primary, Load Settings, Delete, Copy ID, Lineage GIF)
     9→ * - Mobile: plain button (info shown via MobileInfoModal on re-tap)
    10→ */
    11→
    12→import React from 'react';
    13→import { Check, ArrowDown, ArrowUp, Info, Loader2, Trash2, GitBranch, Star, Download, Image } from 'lucide-react';
    14→import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/utils';
    15→import { Button } from '@/shared/components/ui/button';
    16→import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from '@/shared/components/ui/tooltip';
    17→import { HoverCard, HoverCardTrigger, HoverCardContent } from '@/shared/components/ui/hover-card';
    18→import { GenerationDetails } from '@/shared/components/GenerationDetails';
    19→import type { LoraModel } from '@/shared/components/LoraSelectorModal';
    20→import { getSourceTaskId } from '@/shared/lib/taskIdHelpers';
    21→import { useGetTask } from '@/shared/hooks/useTasks';
    22→import type { GenerationVariant } from '@/shared/hooks/useVariants';
    23→import { getVariantIcon, getVariantLabel, isNewVariant, getTimeAgo, hasLoadableSettings, hasDifferentSourceImages, type CurrentSegmentImagesData } from '../utils';
    24→
    25→// --- VariantHoverDetails (fetches real task data for hover tooltip) ---
    26→
    27→interface VariantHoverDetailsProps {
    28→  variant: GenerationVariant;
    29→  availableLoras?: LoraModel[];
    30→}
    31→
    32→const VariantHoverDetails: React.FC<VariantHoverDetailsProps> = ({ variant, availableLoras }) => {
    33→  const variantParams = variant.params;
    34→  const sourceTaskId = getSourceTaskId(variantParams);
    35→  const { data: task, isLoading } = useGetTask(sourceTaskId || '');
    36→
    37→  if (task && !isLoading) {
    38→    return (
    39→      <GenerationDetails
    40→        task={task}
    41→        inputImages={[]}
    42→        variant="hover"
    43→        isMobile={false}
    44→        availableLoras={availableLoras}
    45→        showCopyButtons={true}
    46→      />
    47→    );
    48→  }
    49→
    50→  return (
    51→    <GenerationDetails
    52→      task={{
    53→        taskType: variantParams?.task_type || variantParams?.created_from || 'video_generation',
    54→        params: variantParams,
    55→      }}
    56→      inputImages={[]}
    57→      variant="hover"
    58→      isMobile={false}
    59→      availableLoras={availableLoras}
    60→      showCopyButtons={true}
    61→    />
    62→  );
    63→};
    64→
    65→// --- VariantCard ---
    66→
    67→interface VariantCardProps {
    68→  variant: GenerationVariant;
    69→  isActive: boolean;
    70→  isPrimary: boolean;
    71→  isParent: boolean;
    72→  isChild: boolean;
    73→  activeVariantId: string | null;
    74→  isMobile: boolean;
    75→  readOnly: boolean;
    76→  /** All variants in the list (for finding parent variant by source_variant_id) */
    77→  variants: GenerationVariant[];
    78→  availableLoras?: LoraModel[];
    79→  /** Lineage depth for this variant (show GIF button when >= 5) */
    80→  lineageDepth: number;
    81→  /** Whether a delete operation is loading for this variant */
    82→  isDeleteLoading: boolean;
    83→  /** Currently copied variant ID (for copy-id feedback) */
    84→  copiedVariantId: string | null;
    85→  /** Loaded settings variant ID (for load-settings feedback) */
    86→  loadedSettingsVariantId: string | null;
    87→  // Callbacks
    88→  onVariantSelect: (variantId: string) => void;
    89→  onMakePrimary?: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
    90→  onDeleteVariant?: (variantId: string) => void;
    91→  onLoadVariantSettings?: (variantParams: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
    92→  onToggleStar?: (variantId: string, starred: boolean) => void;
    93→  onMouseEnter: (variant: GenerationVariant) => void;
    94→  onShowMobileInfo: (variantId: string) => void;
    95→  onShowLineageGif: (variantId: string) => void;
    96→  onCopyId: (variantId: string) => void;
    97→  onLoadSettings: (variant: GenerationVariant) => void;
    98→  onLoadImages?: (variant: GenerationVariant) => void;
    99→  currentSegmentImages?: CurrentSegmentImagesData;
   100→  loadedImagesVariantId: string | null;
   101→}
   102→
   103→export const VariantCard: React.FC<VariantCardProps> = ({
   104→  variant,
   105→  isActive,
   106→  isPrimary,
   107→  isParent,
   108→  isChild,
   109→  activeVariantId,
   110→  isMobile,
   111→  readOnly,
   112→  availableLoras,
   113→  lineageDepth,
   114→  isDeleteLoading,
   115→  copiedVariantId,
   116→  loadedSettingsVariantId,
   117→  onVariantSelect,
   118→  onMakePrimary,
   119→  onDeleteVariant,
   120→  onLoadVariantSettings,
   121→  onToggleStar,
   122→  onMouseEnter,
   123→  onShowMobileInfo,
   124→  onShowLineageGif,
   125→  onCopyId,
   126→  onLoadSettings,
   127→  onLoadImages,
   128→  currentSegmentImages,
   129→  loadedImagesVariantId,
   130→}) => {
   131→  const Icon = getVariantIcon(variant.variant_type);
   132→  const label = getVariantLabel(variant);
   133→
   134→  const buttonContent = (
   135→    <div
   136→      role="button"
   137→      tabIndex={0}
   138→      onClick={(e) => {
   139→        e.stopPropagation();
   140→        if (isMobile && isActive) {
   141→          onShowMobileInfo(variant.id);
   142→          return;
   143→        }
   144→        onVariantSelect(variant.id);
   145→      }}
   146→      onKeyDown={(e) => {
   147→        if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
   148→          e.preventDefault();
   149→          onVariantSelect(variant.id);
   150→        }
   151→      }}
   152→      onTouchEnd={(e) => {
   153→        if (isMobile) {
   154→          e.stopPropagation();
   155→          if (isActive) {
   156→            onShowMobileInfo(variant.id);
   157→            return;
   158→          }
   159→          onVariantSelect(variant.id);
   160→        }
   161→      }}
   162→      onMouseEnter={() => onMouseEnter(variant)}
   163→      className={cn(
   164→        'relative p-0.5 rounded transition-all w-full touch-manipulation group/variant overflow-hidden cursor-pointer',
   165→        'hover:bg-muted/80',
   166→        isPrimary && !isActive && 'ring-2 ring-green-500 bg-green-500/10',
   167→        isActive
   168→          ? 'ring-2 ring-orange-500 bg-orange-500/10'
   169→          : 'opacity-70 hover:opacity-100',
   170→        isParent && !isActive && !isPrimary && 'ring-1 ring-blue-500/50',
   171→        isChild && !isActive && !isPrimary && 'ring-1 ring-purple-500/50'
   172→      )}
   173→    >
   174→      {/* Thumbnail */}
   175→      <div className="relative w-full rounded overflow-hidden bg-muted" style={{ paddingBottom: '56.25%' }}>
   176→        {(variant.thumbnail_url || variant.location) ? (
   177→          <img
   178→            src={variant.thumbnail_url || variant.location}
   179→            alt={label}
   180→            className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover pointer-events-none"
   181→          />
   182→        ) : (
   183→          <div className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center">
   184→            <Icon className="w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
   185→          </div>
   186→        )}
   187→
   188→        {/* Top-right: primary badge + relationship badges */}
   189→        <div className="absolute top-0.5 right-0.5 flex items-center gap-0.5 pointer-events-auto">
   190→          {/* Parent relationship badge */}
   191→          {isParent && !isActive && (
   192→            <Tooltip>
   193→              <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   194→                <div className="bg-blue-500 rounded-full p-0.5 cursor-default">
   195→                  <ArrowUp className="w-2 h-2 text-white" />
   196→                </div>
   197→              </TooltipTrigger>
   198→              <TooltipContent side="bottom" className="text-xs z-[100001]">
   199→                Current variant is based on this one
   200→              </TooltipContent>
   201→            </Tooltip>
   202→          )}
   203→
   204→          {/* Child relationship badge */}
   205→          {isChild && !isActive && (
   206→            <Tooltip>
   207→              <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   208→                <div className="bg-purple-500 rounded-full p-0.5 cursor-default">
   209→                  <ArrowDown className="w-2 h-2 text-white" />
   210→                </div>
   211→              </TooltipTrigger>
   212→              <TooltipContent side="bottom" className="text-xs z-[100001]">
   213→                Based on the current variant
   214→              </TooltipContent>
   215→            </Tooltip>
   216→          )}
   217→
   218→          {/* Primary badge */}
   219→          {isPrimary && (
   220→            <div className="bg-green-500 rounded-full p-0.5 pointer-events-none">
   221→              <Check className="w-2 h-2 text-white" />
   222→            </div>
   223→          )}
   224→        </div>
   225→
   226→        {/* Top-left: star button */}
   227→        {onToggleStar && (
   228→          <div
   229→            className={cn(
   230→              'absolute top-0.5 left-0.5 rounded-full p-0.5 transition-opacity cursor-pointer pointer-events-auto',
   231→              variant.starred
   232→                ? 'opacity-100 bg-yellow-500/80'
   233→                : 'opacity-0 group-hover/variant:opacity-100 bg-black/50 hover:bg-black/70'
   234→            )}
   235→            onClick={(e) => {
   236→              e.stopPropagation();
   237→              onToggleStar(variant.id, !variant.starred);
   238→            }}
   239→          >
   240→            <Star className={cn('w-2 h-2', variant.starred ? 'text-white fill-white' : 'text-white')} />
   241→          </div>
   242→        )}
   243→
   244→        {/* Bottom-left: NEW badge or time ago */}
   245→        {isNewVariant(variant, activeVariantId) ? (

   247→            NEW
   248→          </div>

   255→        {/* Info button - bottom right, shows on hover (desktop only) */}
   256→        {!isMobile && (
   257→          <div className="absolute bottom-0.5 right-0.5 opacity-0 group-hover/variant:opacity-100 transition-opacity pointer-events-auto">
   258→            <HoverCard openDelay={0} closeDelay={0}>

   293→                      )}
   294→                      {isChild && (

   297→                        </span>
   298→                      )}
   299→                    </div>

   302→                      <Tooltip>

   307→                              onCopyId(variant.id);

   313→                                : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted/80 active:scale-95"
   314→                            )}
   315→                          >
   316→                            {copiedVariantId === variant.id ? 'copied' : 'id'}
   317→                          </button>
   318→                        </TooltipTrigger>
   319→                        <TooltipContent side="bottom" className="text-xs">
   320→                          Copy ID
   321→                        </TooltipContent>
   322→                      </Tooltip>
   323→                      {/* Lineage GIF button */}
   324→                      {lineageDepth >= 5 && (

   326→                          <TooltipTrigger asChild>

   329→                                e.stopPropagation();
   330→                                onShowLineageGif(variant.id);
   331→                              }}
   332→                              className="p-1 rounded text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted/80 transition-all duration-150 active:scale-95 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-left-1 duration-300"
   333→                            >
   334→                              <GitBranch className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   335→                            </button>
   336→                          </TooltipTrigger>
   337→                          <TooltipContent side="bottom" className="text-xs">
   338→                            View evolution ({lineageDepth} generations)

   340→                        </Tooltip>
   341→                      )}
   342→                      {/* Delete button */}
   343→                      {!readOnly && onDeleteVariant && !isPrimary && (

   349→                                onDeleteVariant(variant.id);

   351→                              disabled={isDeleteLoading}

   353→                            >
   354→                              {isDeleteLoading ? (

   356→                              ) : (
   357→                                <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   358→                              )}
   359→                            </button>

   362→                            Delete
   363→                          </TooltipContent>
   364→                        </Tooltip>
   365→                      )}
   366→                    </div>
   367→                  </div>
   368→
   369→                  {/* Scrollable area: task details only */}
   370→                  {variant.params && (
   371→                    <div className="relative min-h-0 flex-1">
   372→                      <div
   373→                        className="px-2 pb-2 overflow-y-auto max-h-[calc(85vh-5rem)]"
   374→                        onScroll={(e) => {
   375→                          const el = e.currentTarget;
   376→                          const fade = el.nextElementSibling as HTMLElement | null;
   377→                          if (fade) {
   378→                            const isAtBottom = el.scrollHeight - el.scrollTop - el.clientHeight < 8;
   379→                            fade.style.opacity = isAtBottom ? '0' : '1';
   380→                          }
   381→                        }}
   382→                        ref={(el) => {
   383→                          if (!el) return;
   384→                          const fade = el.nextElementSibling as HTMLElement | null;
   385→                          if (fade) {
   386→                            const isScrollable = el.scrollHeight > el.clientHeight;
   387→                            fade.style.opacity = isScrollable ? '1' : '0';
   388→                          }
   389→                        }}
   390→                      >
   391→                        <div className="border-t border-border/50 pt-2">
   392→                          <VariantHoverDetails
   393→                            variant={variant}
   394→                            availableLoras={availableLoras}
   395→                          />
   396→                        </div>
   397→                      </div>
   398→                      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-8 bg-gradient-to-t from-popover to-transparent transition-opacity duration-150" />
   399→                    </div>
   400→                  )}
   401→
   402→                  {/* Action buttons row - pinned at bottom */}
   403→                  {!readOnly && ((!isPrimary && onMakePrimary) || (onLoadVariantSettings && hasLoadableSettings(variant)) || (onLoadImages && hasDifferentSourceImages(variant, currentSegmentImages))) && (
   404→                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 px-2 py-1.5 border-t border-border/50 shrink-0">
   405→                      {!isPrimary && onMakePrimary && (
   406→                        <Button
   407→                          size="sm"
   408→                          variant="outline"
   409→                          onClick={(e) => {
   410→                            e.stopPropagation();
   411→                            onVariantSelect(variant.id);
   412→                            setTimeout(() => onMakePrimary(variant.id), 50);
   413→                          }}
   414→                          className="h-6 text-xs gap-1 flex-1"
   415→                        >
   416→                          <Star className="w-3 h-3" />
   417→                          Make Primary
   418→                        </Button>
   419→                      )}
   420→                      {onLoadVariantSettings && hasLoadableSettings(variant) && (
   421→                        <Button
   422→                          size="sm"
   423→                          variant="outline"
   424→                          onClick={(e) => {

   427→                          }}
   428→                          className={cn(
   429→                            "h-6 text-xs gap-1 flex-1",
   430→                            loadedSettingsVariantId === variant.id && "bg-green-500/20 border-green-500/50 text-green-400"
   431→                          )}
   432→                        >
   433→                          {loadedSettingsVariantId === variant.id ? (
   434→                            <>
   435→                              <Check className="w-3 h-3" />
   436→                              Loaded!
   437→                            </>
   438→                          ) : (
   439→                            <>
   440→                              <Download className="w-3 h-3" />
   441→                              Load Settings
   442→                            </>
   443→                          )}
   444→                        </Button>
   445→                      )}
   446→                      {onLoadImages && hasDifferentSourceImages(variant, currentSegmentImages) && (
   447→                        <Button
   448→                          size="sm"
   449→                          variant="outline"
   450→                          onClick={(e) => {
   451→                            e.stopPropagation();
   452→                            onLoadImages(variant);
   453→                          }}
   454→                          className={cn(
   455→                            "h-6 text-xs gap-1 flex-1",
   456→                            loadedImagesVariantId === variant.id && "bg-green-500/20 border-green-500/50 text-green-400"
   457→                          )}
   458→                        >
   459→                          {loadedImagesVariantId === variant.id ? (
   460→                            <>
   461→                              <Check className="w-3 h-3" />
   462→                              Loaded!
   463→                            </>
   464→                          ) : (
   465→                            <>
   466→                              <Image className="w-3 h-3" />
   467→                              Load Images
   468→                            </>
   469→                          )}
   470→                        </Button>
   471→                      )}
   472→                    </div>
   473→                  )}

   477→          </div>
   478→        )}
   479→      </div>
   480→    </div>
   481→  );

   486→};

## Summary

I found the variant selector component and the "info thing" you mentioned. Here's the complete breakdown:

### File Locations

1. **Main VariantSelector component**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/VariantSelector/index.tsx`
2. **VariantGrid (grid layout)**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/VariantSelector/components/VariantGrid.tsx`
3. **VariantCard (individual variant item)**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/VariantSelector/components/VariantCard.tsx`
4. **MobileInfoModal (mobile variant)**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/VariantSelector/components/MobileInfoModal.tsx`

### The "Info Thing" - Bottom Right Info Button

In **VariantCard.tsx** (lines 255-478), the info button is positioned at **bottom-right** of each variant thumbnail and appears on hover (desktop only):

```tsx
{/* Info button - bottom right, shows on hover (desktop only) */}
{!isMobile && (
  <div className="absolute bottom-0.5 right-0.5 opacity-0 group-hover/variant:opacity-100 transition-opacity pointer-events-auto">
    <HoverCard openDelay={0} closeDelay={0}>
      <HoverCardTrigger asChild>
        <div
          className="h-5 w-5 rounded-full bg-black/50 flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer hover:bg-black/70"
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        >
          <Info className="h-3 w-3 text-white" strokeWidth={2.5} />
        </div>
      </HoverCardTrigger>
```

### What Happens When You Click the Info Button

When you click the info icon, a **HoverCard** (Radix UI popover) appears with:

1. **Header section** (pinned at top):
   - Variant label and badges (Primary, Viewing, Parent of current, Child of current)
   - Action buttons:
     - **Copy ID** button (top right)
     - **Lineage GIF** button (shows when lineage depth >= 5)
     - **Delete** button (if not primary and not in read-only mode)

2. **Scrollable content area**:
   - **VariantHoverDetails** component which renders `GenerationDetails`
   - Shows full task parameters/metadata for that variant
   - Fetches real task data if a valid source_task_id exists

3. **Action buttons row** (pinned at bottom):
   - **Make Primary** button (if not already primary)
   - **Load Settings** button (if variant has loadable settings)
   - **Load Images** button (if variant has different source images than current)

### Mobile Alternative

On mobile, the info button doesn't appear on hover. Instead, tapping the active variant card triggers the **MobileInfoModal** (line 141-142 in VariantCard.tsx):

```tsx
if (isMobile && isActive) {
  onShowMobileInfo(variant.id);
  return;
}
```

### Integration Context

The VariantSelector is used in:
- **InfoPanel.tsx** (lines 276-291): Renders within the side panel of the MediaLightbox when viewing generation details
- The variant selector appears at the bottom of the info panel with other variant management controls

   671→                    className="space-y-0 pb-8"

   694→      <ImageGenerationModal

   696→        onClose={() => setIsGenerationModalOpen(false)}

   698→      />
   699→    </>
   700→  );
   701→};
   702→
   703→// Memoize GenerationsPane - it has no props so a simple memo is sufficient
   704→export const GenerationsPane = React.memo(GenerationsPaneComponent);

   244→      toast({

   246→        description: `${count} generation${count === 1 ? '' : 's'} in past hour`,

   249→    } else if (type === 'Failed') {

   257→
   258→  const handleCancelAllPending = () => {
   259→    if (!selectedProjectId) {
   260→      toast({ title: 'Error', description: 'No project selected.', variant: 'destructive' });
   261→      return;
   262→    }
   263→
   264→    const queryKey = [...queryKeys.tasks.paginated(selectedProjectId!), STATUS_GROUPS[selectedFilter], ITEMS_PER_PAGE, (currentPage - 1) * ITEMS_PER_PAGE];
   265→    const previousData = queryClient.getQueryData(queryKey);
   266→    
   267→    queryClient.setQueryData<PaginatedTasksResponse | undefined>(queryKey, (oldData) => {
   268→      if (!oldData?.tasks) return oldData;
   269→
   270→      return {
   271→        ...oldData,
   272→        tasks: oldData.tasks.map((task) => {
   273→          if (task.status === 'Queued') {
   274→            return { ...task, status: 'Cancelled' as const };
   275→          }
   276→          return task;
   277→        }),

   279→    });

   286→          variant: 'default',
   287→        });
   288→        
   289→        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](selectedProjectId) });
   290→        queryClient.refetchQueries({ [REDACTED](selectedProjectId) });
   291→      },

   293→        queryClient.setQueryData(queryKey, previousData);

1→/**
     2→ * MediaLightbox - Unified Lightbox Dispatcher
     3→ *
     4→ * This is a thin dispatcher that routes to specialized lightbox components
     5→ * based on media type:
     6→ * - ImageLightbox: For images with edit/inpaint/reposition capabilities
     7→ * - VideoLightbox: For videos with trim/regenerate/enhance capabilities
     8→ *
     9→ * The original implementation is preserved in MediaLightbox.legacy.tsx for reference.
    10→ *
    11→ * Architecture notes:
    12→ * - MediaLightbox preserves the original props interface for backwards compatibility
    13→ * - Media type detection uses isVideoAny from typeGuards
    14→ * - Segment slot mode (form-only) goes through VideoLightbox
    15→ */
    16→
    17→import React from 'react';
    18→import type { GenerationRow, Shot } from '@/types/shots';
    19→import { isVideoAny } from '@/shared/lib/typeGuards';
    20→import type { SegmentSlotModeData, AdjacentSegmentsData } from './types';
    21→import { ImageLightbox } from './ImageLightbox';
    22→import { VideoLightbox } from './VideoLightbox';
    23→
    24→// ============================================================================
    25→// Props Interface (unchanged from original for backwards compatibility)
    26→// ============================================================================
    27→
    28→interface MediaLightboxProps {
    29→  /** Media to display. Optional when segmentSlotMode is provided with no video. */
    30→  media?: GenerationRow;
    31→  onClose: () => void;
    32→  onNext?: () => void;
    33→  onPrevious?: () => void;
    34→  /**
    35→   * Segment slot mode - when provided, MediaLightbox acts as a unified segment editor.
    36→   * If segmentVideo is null, shows only the generate form (no media display).
    37→   * Navigation uses onNavigateToPair for seamless slot-based navigation.
    38→   */
    39→  segmentSlotMode?: SegmentSlotModeData;
    40→  // Configuration props to control features
    41→  readOnly?: boolean;
    42→  showNavigation?: boolean;
    43→  showImageEditTools?: boolean;
    44→  showDownload?: boolean;
    45→  showMagicEdit?: boolean;
    46→  autoEnterInpaint?: boolean;
    47→  // Navigation availability
    48→  hasNext?: boolean;
    49→  hasPrevious?: boolean;
    50→  // Workflow-specific props
    51→  allShots?: ShotOption[];
    52→  selectedShotId?: string;
    53→  onShotChange?: (shotId: string) => void;
    54→  onAddToShot?: (targetShotId: string, generationId: string, imageUrl?: string, thumbUrl?: string) => Promise<boolean>;
    55→  onAddToShotWithoutPosition?: (targetShotId: string, generationId: string, imageUrl?: string, thumbUrl?: string) => Promise<boolean>;
    56→  onDelete?: (id: string) => void;
    57→  isDeleting?: string | null;
    58→  onApplySettings?: (metadata: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
    59→  showTickForImageId?: string | null;
    60→  onShowTick?: (imageId: string) => void;
    61→  showTickForSecondaryImageId?: string | null;
    62→  onShowSecondaryTick?: (imageId: string) => void;
    63→  onMagicEdit?: (imageUrl: string, prompt: string, numImages: number) => void;
    64→  // Star functionality
    65→  starred?: boolean;
    66→  onToggleStar?: (id: string, starred: boolean) => void;
    67→  // Task details functionality
    68→  showTaskDetails?: boolean;
    69→  taskDetailsData?: {
    70→    task: Record<string, unknown> | null;
    71→    isLoading: boolean;
    72→    error: Error | null;
    73→    inputImages: string[];
    74→    taskId: string | null;
    75→    onApplySettingsFromTask?: (taskId: string, replaceImages: boolean, inputImages: string[]) => void;
    76→    onClose?: () => void;
    77→  };
    78→  onShowTaskDetails?: () => void;
    79→  // Shot creation functionality
    80→  onCreateShot?: (shotName: string, files: File[]) => Promise<{shotId?: string; shotName?: string} | void>;
    81→  // Shot navigation functionality
    82→  onNavigateToShot?: (shot: Shot, options?: { isNewlyCreated?: boolean }) => void;
    83→  // Tool type override for magic edit
    84→  toolTypeOverride?: string;
    85→  // Optimistic updates
    86→  optimisticPositionedIds?: Set<string>;
    87→  optimisticUnpositionedIds?: Set<string>;
    88→  onOptimisticPositioned?: (mediaId: string, shotId: string) => void;
    89→  onOptimisticUnpositioned?: (mediaId: string, shotId: string) => void;
    90→  // Precomputed overrides from gallery source record
    91→  positionedInSelectedShot?: boolean;
    92→  associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot?: boolean;
    93→  // Navigation to specific generation
    94→  onNavigateToGeneration?: (generationId: string) => void;
    95→  // Open external generation
    96→  onOpenExternalGeneration?: (generationId: string, derivedContext?: string[]) => Promise<void>;
    97→  // Shot ID for star persistence
    98→  shotId?: string;
    99→  // Tasks pane integration (desktop only)
   100→  tasksPaneOpen?: boolean;
   101→  tasksPaneWidth?: number;
   102→  // Video trim functionality
   103→  showVideoTrimEditor?: boolean;
   104→  onTrimModeChange?: (isTrimMode: boolean) => void;
   105→  // Initial video trim mode
   106→  initialVideoTrimMode?: boolean;
   107→  // Initial variant to display
   108→  initialVariantId?: string;
   109→  // Fetch variants for self vs parent
   110→  fetchVariantsForSelf?: boolean;
   111→  // Current segment images from timeline
   112→  currentSegmentImages?: {
   113→    startUrl?: string;
   114→    endUrl?: string;
   115→    startGenerationId?: string;
   116→    endGenerationId?: string;
   117→    startShotGenerationId?: string;
   118→    endShotGenerationId?: string;
   119→    activeChildGenerationId?: string;
   120→    startVariantId?: string;
   121→    endVariantId?: string;
   122→  };
   123→  // Segment frame count callback
   124→  onSegmentFrameCountChange?: (pairShotGenerationId: string, frameCount: number) => void;
   125→  // Current frame count
   126→  currentFrameCount?: number;
   127→  // Adjacent segments for image-to-video navigation
   128→  adjacentSegments?: AdjacentSegmentsData;
   129→}
   130→
   131→// ============================================================================
   132→// Component
   133→// ============================================================================
   134→
   135→const MediaLightbox: React.FC<MediaLightboxProps> = (props) => {
   136→  const {
   137→    media,
   138→    segmentSlotMode,
   139→    ...restProps
   140→  } = props;
   141→
   142→  // Determine if this is a video or segment slot mode (both go to VideoLightbox)
   143→  const isSegmentSlotMode = !!segmentSlotMode;
   144→  const isVideo = media ? isVideoAny(media) : false;
   145→
   146→  // Segment slot mode always uses VideoLightbox (handles form-only case)
   147→  // media may be undefined in form-only mode - VideoLightbox handles this
   148→  if (isSegmentSlotMode) {
   149→    return (
   150→      <VideoLightbox
   151→        media={media}
   152→        segmentSlotMode={segmentSlotMode}
   153→        {...restProps}
   154→      />
   155→    );
   156→  }
   157→
   158→  // Safety check - media is required outside segment slot mode
   159→  if (!media) {
   160→    console.error('[MediaLightbox] ❌ No media prop provided!');
   161→    return null;
   162→  }
   163→
   164→  // Dispatch based on media type
   165→  if (isVideo) {
   166→    return (
   167→      <VideoLightbox
   168→        media={media}
   169→        {...restProps}
   170→      />
   171→    );
   172→  }
   173→
   174→  // Image media
   175→  return (
   176→    <ImageLightbox
   177→      media={media}
   178→      {...restProps}
   179→    />
   180→  );
   181→};
   182→
   183→export default MediaLightbox;
   184→
   185→// Re-export ShotOption for external use
   186→export type { ShotOption } from './types';

    26→
