    30→
    31→  // Programmatic tasks pane control (desktop only)
    32→  isTasksPaneOpen: boolean;
    33→  setIsTasksPaneOpen: (isOpen: boolean) => void;
    34→
    35→  // Reset all pane locks (used by ProductTour)
    36→  resetAllPaneLocks: () => void;
    37→}
    38→
    39→const PanesContext = createContext<PanesContextType | undefined>(undefined);
    40→
    41→export const PanesProvider: React.FC<{ children: ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
    42→  const isMobile = useIsMobile();
    43→  const isTablet = useIsTablet();
    44→  
    45→  // On tablets, allow locking but only one pane at a time
    46→  // On phones (mobile but not tablet), no locking allowed
    47→  const isSmallMobile = isMobile && !isTablet;
    48→  
    49→  // Load pane locks from user settings (desktop and tablet)
    50→  const { value: paneLocks, update: savePaneLocks, isLoading } = useUserUIState('paneLocks', {
    51→    shots: false,
    52→    tasks: false,
    53→    gens: false,
    54→  });
    55→
    56→  // Local state for lock status (source of truth for UI)
    57→  const [locks, setLocks] = useState(paneLocks);
    58→
    59→  // Pane open states (not persisted, runtime only)
    60→  const [isGenerationsPaneOpenState, setIsGenerationsPaneOpenState] = useState(false);
    61→  const [isTasksPaneOpenState, setIsTasksPaneOpenState] = useState(false);
    62→
    63→  // Pane dimensions (not persisted)
    64→  const [generationsPaneHeight, setGenerationsPaneHeightState] = useState<number>(PANE_CONFIG.dimensions.DEFAULT_HEIGHT);
    65→  const [shotsPaneWidth, setShotsPaneWidthState] = useState<number>(PANE_CONFIG.dimensions.DEFAULT_WIDTH);
    66→  const [tasksPaneWidth, setTasksPaneWidthState] = useState<number>(PANE_CONFIG.dimensions.DEFAULT_WIDTH);
    67→  
    68→  // Active task tracking (not persisted)
    69→  const [activeTaskId, setActiveTaskId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    70→
    71→  // Hydrate local state once when settings load (desktop and tablet)
    72→  useEffect(() => {
    73→    if (isSmallMobile) {
    74→      // On small mobile (phones), always start with unlocked state
    75→      setLocks({
    76→        shots: false,
    77→        tasks: false,
    78→        gens: false,
    79→      });
    80→      return;
    81→    }
    82→
    83→    if (!isLoading) {
    84→      // Hydrating pane locks from server (desktop and tablet)
    85→      // On tablets, only keep one lock if multiple are saved
    86→      let newLocks = paneLocks;
    87→      if (isTablet) {
    88→        const activeLocks = Object.entries(paneLocks).filter(([_, locked]) => locked);
    89→        if (activeLocks.length > 1) {
    90→          // Keep only the first locked pane on tablets
    91→          const firstLocked = activeLocks[0][0] as 'shots' | 'tasks' | 'gens';
    92→          newLocks = {
    93→            shots: firstLocked === 'shots',
    94→            tasks: firstLocked === 'tasks',
    95→            gens: firstLocked === 'gens',
    96→          };
    97→        }
    98→      }
    99→      setLocks(newLocks);
   100→
   101→      // IMPORTANT: Sync isTasksPaneOpen with isTasksPaneLocked on hydration
   102→      // This ensures lightboxes (which read isTasksPaneOpen from context) correctly
   103→      // account for a locked pane even after page refresh
   104→      if (newLocks.tasks) {
   105→        setIsTasksPaneOpenState(true);
   106→      }
   107→    }
   108→  }, [isLoading, paneLocks, isSmallMobile, isTablet]);
   109→
   110→  // Lock toggle functions
   111→  const toggleLock = useCallback((pane: 'shots' | 'tasks' | 'gens') => {
   112→    setLocks(prev => {
   113→      const newValue = !prev[pane];
   114→      const newLocks = { ...prev, [pane]: newValue };
   115→      
   116→      // Toggling pane lock
   117→      
   118→      // Save to database only on desktop
   119→      if (!isMobile) {
   120→        savePaneLocks({ [pane]: newValue });
   121→      }
   122→      
   123→      return newLocks;
   124→    });
   125→  }, [savePaneLocks, isMobile]);
   126→
   127→  // Individual setters for backward compatibility
   128→  // On tablets, locking one pane unlocks all others (only one lock allowed at a time)
   129→  const setIsGenerationsPaneLocked = useCallback((isLocked: boolean) => {
   130→    setLocks(prev => {
   131→      if (prev.gens === isLocked) return prev;
   132→      
   133→      // On mobile/tablets, unlock other panes when locking this one (only one pane can be locked at a time)
   134→      const newLocks = (isMobile || isTablet) && isLocked
   135→        ? { shots: false, tasks: false, gens: isLocked }
   136→        : { ...prev, gens: isLocked };
   137→      
   138→      // Save to database (desktop and tablet only, not small phones)
   139→      if (!isSmallMobile) {
   140→        savePaneLocks((isMobile || isTablet) && isLocked ? newLocks : { gens: isLocked });
   141→      }
   142→      
   143→      return newLocks;
   144→    });
   145→  }, [savePaneLocks, isSmallMobile, isMobile, isTablet]);
   146→
   147→  const setIsShotsPaneLocked = useCallback((isLocked: boolean) => {
   148→    setLocks(prev => {
   149→      if (prev.shots === isLocked) return prev;
   150→      
   151→      // On mobile/tablets, unlock other panes when locking this one (only one pane can be locked at a time)
   152→      const newLocks = (isMobile || isTablet) && isLocked
   153→        ? { shots: isLocked, tasks: false, gens: false }
   154→        : { ...prev, shots: isLocked };
   155→      
   156→      // Save to database (desktop and tablet only, not small phones)
   157→      if (!isSmallMobile) {
   158→        savePaneLocks((isMobile || isTablet) && isLocked ? newLocks : { shots: isLocked });
   159→      }
   160→      
   161→      return newLocks;
   162→    });
   163→  }, [savePaneLocks, isSmallMobile, isMobile, isTablet]);
   164→
   165→  const setIsTasksPaneLocked = useCallback((isLocked: boolean) => {
   166→    setLocks(prev => {
   167→      if (prev.tasks === isLocked) return prev;
   168→
   169→      // On mobile/tablets, unlock other panes when locking this one (only one pane can be locked at a time)
   170→      const newLocks = (isMobile || isTablet) && isLocked
   171→        ? { shots: false, tasks: isLocked, gens: false }
   172→        : { ...prev, tasks: isLocked };
   173→
   174→      // Save to database (desktop and tablet only, not small phones)
   175→      if (!isSmallMobile) {
   176→        savePaneLocks((isMobile || isTablet) && isLocked ? newLocks : { tasks: isLocked });
   177→      }
   178→
   179→      return newLocks;
   180→    });
   181→
   182→    // IMPORTANT: Sync isTasksPaneOpen with isTasksPaneLocked
   183→    // This ensures lightboxes correctly account for locked pane state
   184→    if (isLocked) {
   185→      setIsTasksPaneOpenState(true);
   186→    }
   187→  }, [savePaneLocks, isSmallMobile, isMobile, isTablet]);
   188→
   189→  // Open state setters
   190→  const setIsGenerationsPaneOpen = useCallback((isOpen: boolean) => {
   191→    setIsGenerationsPaneOpenState(isOpen);
   192→  }, []);
   193→  
   194→  const setIsTasksPaneOpen = useCallback((isOpen: boolean) => {
   195→    // Works on desktop and tablets - only small phones use hover/tap behavior exclusively
   196→    if (!isSmallMobile) {
   197→      setIsTasksPaneOpenState(isOpen);
   198→    }
   199→  }, [isSmallMobile]);
   200→
   201→  // Reset all pane locks immediately (used by ProductTour)
   202→  // This updates local state, useUserUIState value, and database without debounce
   203→  const resetAllPaneLocks = useCallback(async () => {
   204→    const unlockedState = { shots: false, tasks: false, gens: false };
   205→
   206→    // Update local locks state immediately
   207→    setLocks(unlockedState);
   208→
   209→    // Update the useUserUIState local value (prevents sync effect from restoring old value)
   210→    savePaneLocks(unlockedState);
   211→
   212→    // Also save to database immediately (no debounce) for reliability
   213→    try {
   214→      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
   215→      if (user) {
   216→        await updateToolSettingsSupabase({
   217→          scope: 'user',
   218→          id: user.id,
   219→          toolId: 'ui',
   220→          patch: { paneLocks: unlockedState },
   221→        }, undefined, 'immediate');
   222→        console.log('[PanesContext] All pane locks reset in database');
   223→      }
   224→    } catch (error) {
   225→      handleError(error, { context: 'PanesContext', showToast: false });
   226→    }
   227→  }, [savePaneLocks]);
   228→
   229→  // Dimension setters
   230→  const setGenerationsPaneHeight = useCallback((height: number) => {
   231→    setGenerationsPaneHeightState(height);
   232→  }, []);
   233→
   234→  const setShotsPaneWidth = useCallback((width: number) => {
   235→    setShotsPaneWidthState(width);
   236→  }, []);
   237→
   238→  const setTasksPaneWidth = useCallback((width: number) => {
   239→    setTasksPaneWidthState(width);
   240→  }, []);
   241→
   242→  const value = useMemo(
   243→    () => ({
   244→      // Return actual lock state for all device types (mobile locking is now supported)
   245→      isGenerationsPaneLocked: locks.gens,
   246→      setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
   247→      isGenerationsPaneOpen: isGenerationsPaneOpenState,
   248→      setIsGenerationsPaneOpen,
   249→      generationsPaneHeight,
   250→      setGenerationsPaneHeight,
   251→      isShotsPaneLocked: locks.shots,

   254→      setShotsPaneWidth,
   255→      isTasksPaneLocked: locks.tasks,
   256→      setIsTasksPaneLocked,
   257→      tasksPaneWidth,
   258→      setTasksPaneWidth,
   259→      activeTaskId,

   261→      isTasksPaneOpen: isTasksPaneOpenState,

   265→    [
   266→      locks.gens,

   268→      locks.tasks,
   269→      setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
   270→      setIsShotsPaneLocked,
   271→      setIsTasksPaneLocked,
   272→      isGenerationsPaneOpenState,

   275→      setGenerationsPaneHeight,

   283→      setIsTasksPaneOpen,
   284→      resetAllPaneLocks,
   285→    ]

   287→
   288→  return (

   291→    </PanesContext.Provider>

   293→};

   122→    const handler = (e: CustomEvent<boolean>) => setSelectionActive(!!e.detail);
   123→    window.addEventListener('mobileSelectionActive', handler as EventListener);
   124→    return () => window.removeEventListener('mobileSelectionActive', handler as EventListener);
   125→  }, []);
   126→
   127→  // Position calculation
   128→  const isVisible = isLocked || (isOpen && !isLocked);
   129→  const position: PanePosition = {
   130→    side,
   131→    dimension: paneDimension,
   132→    offsets: { bottom: bottomOffset, horizontal: horizontalOffset },
   133→    isVisible,
   134→  };
   135→  const dynamicStyle = usePositionStrategy(position);
   136→
   137→  // Icon helpers
   138→  const iconSize = isMobile ? "h-5 w-5" : "h-4 w-4";
   139→  const buttonSize = isMobile ? "h-9 w-9" : "h-8 w-8";
   140→
   141→  const getIcon = () => {
   142→    if (customIcon) return customIcon;
   143→    if (paneIcon !== 'chevron') {
   144→      switch (paneIcon) {
   145→        case 'tools': return <LayoutGrid className={iconSize} />;
   146→        case 'gallery': return <Images className={iconSize} />;
   147→        case 'tasks': return <ListTodo className={iconSize} />;
   148→      }
   149→    }
   150→    switch (side) {
   151→      case 'left': return <ChevronRight className={iconSize} />;
   152→      case 'right': return <ChevronLeft className={iconSize} />;
   153→      case 'bottom': return <ChevronUp className={iconSize} />;
   154→      default: return null;
   155→    }
   156→  };
   157→
   158→  // ==========================================================================
   159→  // BUTTON CONFIGURATION - Determines which buttons show based on state
   160→  // ==========================================================================
   161→  const getButtonsToShow = (): ButtonType[] => {
   162→    // Mobile behavior
   163→    if (!useDesktopBehavior) {
   164→      // Left/Right panes on mobile: Only show thirdButton
   165→      if (!isBottom) {
   166→        return thirdButton ? ['third'] : [];
   167→      }
   168→      // Bottom pane on mobile with allowMobileLock
   169→      if (allowMobileLock) {
   170→        if (isLocked) return ['third', 'unlock', 'fourth'];
   171→        if (isOpen) return ['third', 'lock', 'fourth'];
   172→        return ['third', 'lock', 'open'];
   173→      }
   174→      // Bottom pane on mobile without allowMobileLock
   175→      if (isOpen) return []; // Hide when open
   176→      return ['third', 'open', 'fourth'];
   177→    }
   178→
   179→    // Desktop behavior
   180→    if (isLocked) {
   181→      return isBottom ? ['third', 'unlock', 'fourth'] : ['third', 'unlock'];
   182→    }
   183→    if (isOpen) {
   184→      return isBottom ? ['third', 'lock', 'fourth'] : ['third', 'lock'];
   185→    }
   186→    // Closed
   187→    return isBottom ? ['third', 'lock', 'open'] : ['third', 'lock'];
   188→  };
   189→
   190→  // ==========================================================================
   191→  // RENDER HELPERS
   192→  // ==========================================================================
   193→  const renderButton = (type: ButtonType) => {
   194→    const baseClass = `${buttonSize} text-zinc-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-zinc-700`;
   195→
   196→    switch (type) {
   197→      case 'third':
   198→        if (!thirdButton) return null;
   199→        return (
   200→          <TooltipButton key="third" tooltip={thirdButton.tooltip} showTooltip={showTooltips} side={tooltipSide}>
   201→            <Button
   202→              variant="ghost"
   203→              size="icon"
   204→              onPointerUp={handleButtonClick(thirdButton.onClick)}
   205→              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   206→              className={baseClass}
   207→              aria-label={thirdButton.ariaLabel}
   208→            >
   209→              {thirdButton.content || <Square className={iconSize} />}
   210→            </Button>
   211→          </TooltipButton>
   212→        );
   213→
   214→      case 'fourth':
   215→        if (!fourthButton) return null;
   216→        return (
   217→          <TooltipButton key="fourth" tooltip={fourthButton.tooltip} showTooltip={showTooltips} side={tooltipSide}>
   218→            <Button
   219→              variant="ghost"
   220→              size="icon"
   221→              onPointerUp={handleButtonClick(fourthButton.onClick)}
   222→              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   223→              className={baseClass}
   224→              aria-label={fourthButton.ariaLabel}
   225→              data-tour={dataTourFourthButton}
   226→            >
   227→              {fourthButton.content || <Square className={iconSize} />}
   228→            </Button>
   229→          </TooltipButton>
   230→        );
   231→
   232→      case 'lock':
   233→        return (
   234→          <TooltipButton key="lock" tooltip="Lock pane open" showTooltip={showTooltips} side={tooltipSide}>
   235→            <Button
   236→              variant="ghost"
   237→              size="icon"
   238→              onPointerUp={handleButtonClick(() => toggleLock(true))}
   239→              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   240→              className={baseClass}
   241→              aria-label="Lock pane"
   242→              data-tour={dataTourLock}
   243→            >
   244→              <LockIcon className={iconSize} />
   245→            </Button>
   246→          </TooltipButton>
   247→        );
   248→
   249→      case 'unlock':
   250→        return (
   251→          <TooltipButton key="unlock" tooltip="Unlock pane" showTooltip={showTooltips} side={tooltipSide}>
   252→            <Button
   253→              variant="ghost"
   254→              size="icon"
   255→              onPointerUp={handleButtonClick(() => toggleLock(false))}
   256→              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   257→              className={baseClass}
   258→              aria-label="Unlock pane"
   259→            >
   260→              <UnlockIcon className={iconSize} />
   261→            </Button>
   262→          </TooltipButton>
   263→        );
   264→
   265→      case 'open':
   266→        return (
   267→          <TooltipButton key="open" tooltip={paneTooltip} showTooltip={showTooltips} side={tooltipSide}>
   268→            <Button
   269→              variant="ghost"
   270→              size="icon"
   271→              onPointerUp={handleButtonClick(handleOpen)}
   272→              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   273→              className={baseClass}
   274→              aria-label={paneTooltip || "Open pane"}
   275→            >
   276→              {getIcon()}
   277→            </Button>
   278→          </TooltipButton>
   279→        );
   280→
   281→      default:
   282→        return null;
   283→    }
   284→  };
   285→
   286→  // ==========================================================================
   287→  // RENDER
   288→  // ==========================================================================
   289→
   290→  // Hide on mobile when selection is active
   291→  if (!useDesktopBehavior && selectionActive) return null;
   292→
   293→  const buttons = getButtonsToShow();
   294→
   295→  // Nothing to show
   296→  if (buttons.length === 0) return null;
   297→
   298→  // Container styling
   299→  // TasksPane (right side) should appear above MediaLightbox (z-[100000]) on iPad/desktop
   300→  // but BEHIND the lightbox on mobile phones for cleaner full-screen lightbox experience
   301→  const isTasksPane = side === 'right';
   302→  const isMobilePhone = isMobile && !isTablet;
   303→  const aboveLightboxZIndex = 'z-[100001]';
   304→  const belowLightboxZIndex = 'z-[99]'; // Below lightbox (z-[100000])
   305→  const normalLockedZIndex = PANE_CONFIG.zIndex.CONTROL_LOCKED;
   306→  const normalUnlockedZIndex = PANE_CONFIG.zIndex.CONTROL_UNLOCKED;
   307→
   308→  const zIndex = isTasksPane
   309→    ? (isMobilePhone ? belowLightboxZIndex : aboveLightboxZIndex)  // Behind lightbox on mobile phones
   310→    : (isLocked || (isOpen && !isLocked) ? normalLockedZIndex : normalUnlockedZIndex);
   311→
   312→  const bgOpacity = isLocked || (isOpen && !isLocked) ? 'bg-zinc-800/90' : 'bg-zinc-800/80';
   313→  const transition = isLocked || (isOpen && !isLocked)
   314→    ? PANE_CONFIG.transition.PROPERTIES.TRANSFORM_ONLY
   315→    : PANE_CONFIG.transition.PROPERTIES.TRANSFORM_OPACITY;
   316→
   317→  const positionClasses = (() => {
   318→    switch (side) {
   319→      case 'left': return 'left-0 flex-col';
   320→      case 'right': return 'right-0 flex-col';
   321→      case 'bottom': return 'left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bottom-0 flex-row';
   322→      default: return '';
   323→    }
   324→  })();
   325→
   326→  // On mobile, left/right panes only show one button - make them taller for easier tapping
   327→  const isMobileSidePane = !useDesktopBehavior && !isBottom && buttons.length === 1;
   328→
   329→  // Add safe area margin for iOS home indicator on touch devices
   330→  // Only when the pane is NOT visible - when visible, the control sits on the pane, not at screen edge
   331→  const needsSafeAreaMargin = isMobile && isBottom && !isVisible;
   332→  const mobileStyle = needsSafeAreaMargin ? {
   333→    ...dynamicStyle,
   334→    marginBottom: safeAreaCalc.marginBottom(0.5),
   335→  } : dynamicStyle;
   336→
   337→  const content = (
   338→    <div
   339→      data-pane-control
   340→      data-tour={dataTour}
   341→      style={mobileStyle}
   342→      className={cn(
   343→        `fixed ${zIndex} flex items-center p-1 backdrop-blur-sm border border-zinc-700 rounded-md gap-1 duration-${PANE_CONFIG.timing.ANIMATION_DURATION} ${PANE_CONFIG.transition.EASING}`,
   344→        bgOpacity,
   345→        transition,
   346→        positionClasses,
   347→        'opacity-100 touch-none',
   348→        // Taller touch target for mobile side panes with single button
   349→        isMobileSidePane && 'min-h-[72px] justify-center'
   350→      )}
   351→      onMouseEnter={useDesktopBehavior && isOpen && !isLocked ? handlePaneEnter : undefined}
   352→      onMouseLeave={useDesktopBehavior && isOpen && !isLocked ? handlePaneLeave : undefined}
   353→      {...containerEventHandlers}
   354→    >
   355→      {buttons.map(renderButton)}
   356→    </div>
   357→  );
   358→
   359→  // Wrap in TooltipProvider for desktop
   360→  if (useDesktopBehavior) {
   361→    return <TooltipProvider delayDuration={300}>{content}</TooltipProvider>;
   362→  }
   363→
   364→  return content;
   365→};
   366→
   367→export default PaneControlTab;
   368→

1→/**
     2→ * LightboxShell - Dialog/Overlay container for MediaLightbox
     3→ *
     4→ * Encapsulates all the complex event handling for the lightbox modal:
     5→ * - Dialog root/portal/overlay
     6→ * - Pointer/touch/click event handling with z-index awareness
     7→ * - Body scroll locking
     8→ * - Accessibility elements
     9→ *
    10→ * Note: Tasks pane controls are handled by the existing PaneControlTab from TasksPane,
    11→ * which is visible above the lightbox at z-[100001]. The overlay adjusts its size
    12→ * to account for the pane when it's open or locked.
    13→ *
    14→ * This allows the main MediaLightbox to focus on content orchestration.
    15→ */
    16→
    17→import React, { useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
    18→import { Dialog as DialogPrimitive } from "@base-ui-components/react/dialog";
    19→import { TooltipProvider } from '@/shared/components/ui/tooltip';
    20→import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/utils';
    21→
    22→interface LightboxShellProps {
    23→  children: React.ReactNode;
    24→  onClose: () => void;
    25→
    26→  // Edit mode
    27→  /** Canvas/brush edit overlay is active (inpaint, annotate, reposition, text).
    28→   *  Used to allow canvas touch events to pass through. */
    29→  hasCanvasOverlay: boolean;
    30→  /** Reposition (move) mode — the only edit mode that blocks background click-to-close
    31→   *  (because dragging could cause accidental closes). */
    32→  isRepositionMode: boolean;
    33→
    34→  // Responsive
    35→  isMobile: boolean;
    36→  isTabletOrLarger: boolean;
    37→
    38→  // Tasks pane (for overlay/content sizing)
    39→  effectiveTasksPaneOpen: boolean;
    40→  effectiveTasksPaneWidth: number;
    41→  isTasksPaneLocked: boolean;
    42→
    43→  // Layout
    44→  needsFullscreenLayout: boolean;
    45→  needsTasksPaneOffset: boolean;
    46→
    47→  // Ref for content
    48→  contentRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
    49→
    50→  // Accessibility
    51→  accessibilityTitle: string;
    52→  accessibilityDescription: string;
    53→}
    54→
    55→/**
    56→ * Helper to check if a higher z-index dialog is open
    57→ */
    58→function hasHigherZIndexDialog(): boolean {
    59→  // Query for our custom compat attribute on dialog overlays/backdrops
    60→  const dialogOverlays = document.querySelectorAll('[data-dialog-backdrop]');
    61→  return Array.from(dialogOverlays).some((overlay) => {
    62→    const zIndex = parseInt(window.getComputedStyle(overlay as Element).zIndex || '0', 10);
    63→    return zIndex > 100000;
    64→  });
    65→}
    66→
    67→/**
    68→ * Helper to check if target or any ancestor has higher z-index
    69→ */
    70→function targetHasHigherZIndex(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
    71→  let element = target as HTMLElement | null;
    72→  while (element && element !== document.body) {
    73→    const zIndex = parseInt(window.getComputedStyle(element).zIndex || '0', 10);
    74→    if (zIndex > 100000) {
    75→      return true;
    76→    }
    77→    element = element.parentElement;
    78→  }
    79→  return false;
    80→}
    81→
    82→/**
    83→ * Helper to check if element is interactive (buttons, inputs, etc.)
    84→ */
    85→function isInteractiveElement(target: HTMLElement): boolean {
    86→  return (
    87→    target.tagName === 'BUTTON' ||
    88→    target.tagName === 'INPUT' ||
    89→    target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' ||
    90→    target.tagName === 'SELECT' ||
    91→    target.tagName === 'A' ||
    92→    target.closest('button') !== null ||
    93→    target.closest('a') !== null
    94→  );
    95→}
    96→
    97→/**
    98→ * Check if a touch event should pass through to the element
    99→ * (canvas in edit mode, or interactive elements like buttons/inputs).
   100→ */
   101→function shouldAllowTouchThrough(target: HTMLElement, hasCanvasOverlay: boolean): boolean {
   102→  if (hasCanvasOverlay && target.tagName === 'CANVAS') return true;
   103→  if (isInteractiveElement(target)) return true;
   104→  return false;
   105→}
   106→
   107→export const LightboxShell: React.FC<LightboxShellProps> = ({
   108→  children,
   109→  onClose,
   110→  hasCanvasOverlay,
   111→  isRepositionMode,
   112→  isMobile,
   113→  isTabletOrLarger,
   114→  effectiveTasksPaneOpen,
   115→  effectiveTasksPaneWidth,
   116→  isTasksPaneLocked,
   117→  needsFullscreenLayout,
   118→  needsTasksPaneOffset,
   119→  contentRef,
   120→  accessibilityTitle,
   121→  accessibilityDescription,
   122→}) => {
   123→  // Track where pointer/click started to prevent accidental modal closure on drag
   124→  const pointerDownTargetRef = useRef<EventTarget | null>(null);
   125→
   126→  // Track pointerdown target for capture-phase background click detection
   127→  const bgPointerDownTargetRef = useRef<EventTarget | null>(null);
   128→
   129→  // Lock body scroll when lightbox is open.
   130→  // On phones, modal={true} handles scroll locking. On desktop and iPad,
   131→  // modal={false} (to allow TasksPane interaction), so we lock manually.
   132→  //
   133→  // Layers of scroll prevention:
   134→  //   1. touch-action:none on non-scrollable regions (backdrop, media area)
   135→  //   2. overscroll-behavior:none on scrollable regions (panels)
   136→  //   3. overflow:hidden on <body> via CSS class (NOT on <html> — that
   137→  //      blocks iOS Safari's visual viewport restoration after keyboard dismiss)
   138→  //
   139→  // IMPORTANT: No scroll event listener! Safari's keyboard dismiss self-repair
   140→  // works by scrolling the page to recalculate the visual viewport. A scroll
   141→  // listener that forces scrollTo() prevents this, causing permanent viewport
   142→  // offset (the "dead space at bottom" bug). Instead we use a focusout handler
   143→  // with a micro-scroll to nudge Safari into recalculating when it gets stuck.
   144→  const isActuallyModal = isMobile && !isTabletOrLarger;
   145→  useEffect(() => {
   146→    if (isActuallyModal) return; // modal={true} handles scroll locking
   147→
   148→    const savedScrollY = window.scrollY;
   149→    const html = document.documentElement;
   150→    html.classList.add('lightbox-open');
   151→
   152→    // iOS Safari bug: visualViewport.offsetTop can get stuck after keyboard
   153→    // dismiss. A micro-scroll on focusout forces Safari to recalculate.
   154→    const handleFocusOut = (e: FocusEvent) => {
   155→      const tag = (e.target as HTMLElement)?.tagName;
   156→      if (tag !== 'INPUT' && tag !== 'TEXTAREA') return;
   157→      setTimeout(() => {
   158→        const vv = window.visualViewport;
   159→        if (vv && vv.offsetTop !== 0) {
   160→          window.scrollBy(0, -1);
   161→          window.scrollBy(0, 1);
   162→        }
   163→      }, 100);
   164→    };
   165→    document.addEventListener('focusout', handleFocusOut);
   166→
   167→    return () => {
   168→      document.removeEventListener('focusout', handleFocusOut);
   169→      // Dismiss keyboard to reset visual viewport offset before removing lock.
   170→      if (document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement) {
   171→        document.activeElement.blur();
   172→      }
   173→      html.classList.remove('lightbox-open');
   174→      // Restore scroll position in case iOS moved it.
   175→      window.scrollTo(0, savedScrollY);
   176→    };
   177→  }, [isActuallyModal]);
   178→
   179→  // ========================================
   180→  // OVERLAY EVENT HANDLERS (Backdrop layer)
   181→  // ========================================
   182→  // Active when needsFullscreenLayout=false (non-fullscreen video layouts).
   183→  // In fullscreen layouts the Popup covers the Backdrop, so these don't fire —
   184→  // close-on-click is handled by the capture-phase handlers below instead.
   185→
   186→  const handleOverlayPointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
   187→    pointerDownTargetRef.current = e.target;
   188→
   189→    if (hasHigherZIndexDialog() || targetHasHigherZIndex(e.target)) {
   190→      return;
   191→    }
   192→
   193→    e.preventDefault();
   194→    e.stopPropagation();
   195→    if (e.nativeEvent && typeof e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation === 'function') {
   196→      e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation();
   197→    }
   198→  };
   199→
   200→  const handleOverlayPointerUp = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
   201→    if (hasHigherZIndexDialog() || targetHasHigherZIndex(e.target)) {
   202→      return;
   203→    }
   204→
   205→    // Close logic lives here (not in onClick) because preventDefault() on
   206→    // pointerdown suppresses compatibility mouse events including click.
   207→    // Pointer events still fire, so we detect a "click" as pointerdown +
   208→    // pointerup on the same overlay element.
   209→    if (!isRepositionMode) {
   210→      const clickStartedOnOverlay = pointerDownTargetRef.current === e.currentTarget;
   211→      const clickEndedOnOverlay = e.target === e.currentTarget;
   212→
   213→      if (clickStartedOnOverlay && clickEndedOnOverlay) {
   214→        onClose();
   215→      }
   216→    }
   217→
   218→    pointerDownTargetRef.current = null;
   219→
   220→    e.preventDefault();
   221→    e.stopPropagation();
   222→    if (e.nativeEvent && typeof e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation === 'function') {
   223→      e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation();
   224→    }
   225→  };
   226→
   227→  const handleOverlayClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
   228→    if (hasHigherZIndexDialog() || targetHasHigherZIndex(e.target)) {
   229→      return;
   230→    }
   231→
   232→    // Primary close logic is in handleOverlayPointerUp (see comment there).
   233→    // This handler is a fallback for any edge cases where click still fires.
   234→    if (isRepositionMode) {
   235→      pointerDownTargetRef.current = null;
   236→      return;
   237→    }
   238→
   239→    const clickStartedOnOverlay = pointerDownTargetRef.current === e.currentTarget;
   240→    const clickEndedOnOverlay = e.target === e.currentTarget;
   241→
   242→    if (clickStartedOnOverlay && clickEndedOnOverlay) {
   243→      onClose();
   244→    }
   245→
   246→    pointerDownTargetRef.current = null;
   247→  };
   248→
   249→  // ========================================
   250→  // STANDARDIZED BACKGROUND CLICK-TO-CLOSE
   251→  // ========================================
   252→  // Uses capture phase on the Popup so it fires before any child handler.
   253→  // Layouts mark their background elements with data-lightbox-bg.
   254→  // This is the ONE place that handles click-to-close for all layouts.
   255→
   256→  const handleBgPointerDownCapture = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
   257→    bgPointerDownTargetRef.current = e.target;
   258→  };
   259→
   260→  const handleBgClickCapture = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
   261→    const downTarget = bgPointerDownTargetRef.current as HTMLElement | null;
   262→    const upTarget = e.target as HTMLElement;
   263→    bgPointerDownTargetRef.current = null;
   264→
   265→    if (hasHigherZIndexDialog() || targetHasHigherZIndex(upTarget)) return;
   266→    // Only reposition mode blocks background close (dragging could cause accidental closes).
   267→    // All other edit modes (inpaint, annotate, magic edit, etc.) allow background close.
   268→    if (isRepositionMode) return;
   269→
   270→    // Both pointerdown and click must land directly on a background element
   271→    // (not on a child of one — the target itself must have the attribute)
   272→    if (
   273→      downTarget?.hasAttribute?.('data-lightbox-bg') &&
   274→      upTarget.hasAttribute?.('data-lightbox-bg')
   275→    ) {
   276→      e.stopPropagation();
   277→      onClose();
   278→    }
   279→  };
   280→
   281→  // ========================================
   282→  // CONTENT EVENT HANDLERS
   283→  // ========================================
   284→
   285→  const handleContentPointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
   286→    const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
   287→    // Don't stop propagation for floating UI content (selects, popovers, menus)
   288→    const isFloatingContent = target.closest('[role="listbox"], [role="menu"], [data-side], [data-popup]') !== null;
   289→
   290→    if (isFloatingContent) {
   291→      return;
   292→    }
   293→
   294→    e.stopPropagation();
   295→  };
   296→
   297→  const handleContentClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
   298→    e.stopPropagation();
   299→  };
   300→
   301→  // ========================================
   302→  // SHARED TOUCH HANDLERS (Backdrop + Popup)
   303→  // ========================================
   304→  // Used on both layers. Allow canvas/interactive element touches through;
   305→  // otherwise stop propagation on mobile to prevent Base UI interference.
   306→
   307→  const handleTouchEvent = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
   308→    if (shouldAllowTouchThrough(e.target as HTMLElement, hasCanvasOverlay)) return;
   309→    if (isMobile) e.stopPropagation();
   310→  };
   311→
   312→  const handleTouchCancel = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
   313→    if (hasHigherZIndexDialog()) return;
   314→    e.preventDefault();
   315→    e.stopPropagation();
   316→    if (e.nativeEvent && typeof e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation === 'function') {
   317→      e.nativeEvent.stopImmediatePropagation();
   318→    }
   319→  };
   320→
   321→  // Focus the content element on mount
   322→  useEffect(() => {
   323→    contentRef.current?.focus();
   324→  }, [contentRef]);
   325→
   326→  // ========================================
   327→  // OVERLAY STYLES
   328→  // ========================================
   329→
   330→  // Check both effectiveTasksPaneOpen AND isTasksPaneLocked to handle edge cases
   331→  // where they might be temporarily out of sync (e.g., during hydration)
   332→  const shouldAccountForTasksPane = (effectiveTasksPaneOpen || isTasksPaneLocked) && isTabletOrLarger;
   333→
   334→  const overlayStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
   335→    pointerEvents: 'all',
   336→    touchAction: 'none',
   337→    cursor: 'pointer', // Required for iOS touch events
   338→    zIndex: 10000,
   339→    position: 'fixed',
   340→    top: 0,
   341→    left: 0,
   342→    right: shouldAccountForTasksPane ? `${effectiveTasksPaneWidth}px` : 0,
   343→    bottom: 0,
   344→    transition: 'right 300ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1), width 300ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1)',
   345→    ...(shouldAccountForTasksPane
   346→      ? { width: `calc(100vw - ${effectiveTasksPaneWidth}px)` }
   347→      : {}),
   348→  };
   349→
   350→  // ========================================
   351→  // CONTENT STYLES
   352→  // ========================================
   353→
   354→  const contentStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
   355→    transition: 'width 300ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1)',
   356→    ...(needsTasksPaneOffset
   357→      ? { width: `calc(100vw - ${effectiveTasksPaneWidth}px)` }
   358→      : needsFullscreenLayout
   359→      ? { width: '100vw' }
   360→      : {}),
   361→  };
   362→
   363→  return (
   364→    <TooltipProvider delayDuration={500}>
   365→      <DialogPrimitive.Root
   366→        open={true}
   367→        modal={isActuallyModal}
   368→        disablePointerDismissal
   369→        onOpenChange={(_open, eventDetails) => {
   370→          // Prevent Base UI from updating internal state (which corrupts controlled open={true})
   371→          // and from calling stopPropagation on the native event (which blocks our custom handlers).
   372→          // Our own handlers in useLightboxNavigation (Escape) and handleOverlayClick (outside press)
   373→          // manage all closing logic.
   374→          eventDetails.cancel();
   375→          eventDetails.allowPropagation();
   376→        }}
   377→      >
   378→        <DialogPrimitive.Portal>
   379→          <DialogPrimitive.Backdrop
   380→            data-dialog-backdrop
   381→            className={cn(
   382→              "fixed z-[100000] bg-black/80 p-0 border-none shadow-none",
   383→              "data-[open]:animate-in data-[ending-style]:animate-out data-[ending-style]:fade-out-0 data-[open]:fade-in-0",
   384→              !isMobile && "duration-200"
   385→            )}
   386→            onPointerDown={handleOverlayPointerDown}
   387→            onPointerUp={handleOverlayPointerUp}
   388→            onClick={handleOverlayClick}
   389→            onTouchStart={handleTouchEvent}
   390→            onTouchMove={handleTouchEvent}
   391→            onTouchEnd={handleTouchEvent}
   392→            onTouchCancel={handleTouchCancel}
   393→            style={overlayStyle}
   394→          />
   395→
   396→          {/* Task pane handle removed - the existing PaneControlTab from TasksPane
   397→              is now visible above the lightbox at z-[100001] and handles all pane controls.
   398→              The overlay correctly accounts for the pane via shouldAccountForTasksPane. */}
   399→
   400→          <DialogPrimitive.Popup

   402→            tabIndex={-1}
   403→            onPointerDownCapture={handleBgPointerDownCapture}
   404→            onClickCapture={handleBgClickCapture}
   405→            onPointerDown={handleContentPointerDown}

    overscroll-behavior: none;
  }
  html.lightbox-open body {
    overscroll-behavior: none;
  }

  /* Fix scroll lock interfering with Select scroll on mobile */
  /* When body scroll is locked, it can prevent scrolling within Select dropdowns */
  body[data-scroll-locked] [role="listbox"] {
    overflow-y: auto !important;
    -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch !important;
    touch-action: pan-y !important;
  }

     3→
     4→export class BottomPanePositionStrategy extends BasePanePositionStrategy {
     5→  getStyle(position: PanePosition): React.CSSProperties {
     6→    const { dimension, offsets, isVisible } = position;
     7→    const horizontalOffset = offsets.horizontal || 0;
     8→    
     9→    return {
    10→      ...this.getBaseStyle(),
    11→      left: '50%',
    12→      bottom: '0px',
    13→      // Centre within visible width by shifting half the horizontalOffset.
    14→      // translateX(-50%) centres on viewport; additional translateX accounts for
    15→      // asymmetrical side panes (e.g. shots/tasks) so the control remains centred
    16→      // within the bottom pane itself.
    17→      transform: `translateX(-50%) translateX(${horizontalOffset / 2}px) translateY(${isVisible ? -dimension : 0}px)`,
    18→    };
    19→  }
    20→}

## Analysis Report: Generation Pane Handler Shift on MediaLightbox Open

Based on my investigation of the codebase, I've identified the root cause of the task pane handler shifting when the MediaLightbox opens while the generation pane is locked.

### Key Components Identified

**1. Generation Pane (GenerationsPane.tsx)**
- **Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/GenerationsPane/GenerationsPane.tsx`
- **What it is:** A bottom pane that displays generation gallery items, filters, and pagination controls
- **Locked state:** The `isGenerationsPaneLocked` state (lines 54-55) determines if the pane stays open when clicking outside
- **Positioning:** Fixed bottom pane with CSS styling (line 361-365):
  ```tsx
  style={{
    height: `${generationsPaneHeight}px`,
    left: isShotsPaneLocked ? `${shotsPaneWidth}px` : 0,
    right: isTasksPaneLocked ? `${tasksPaneWidth}px` : 0,
  }}
  ```

**2. Task Pane Handler (PaneControlTab.tsx)**
- **Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/PaneControlTab.tsx`
- **What it is:** The draggable/clickable control tab that shows at the edge of each pane (lock/unlock buttons, task count, etc.)
- **Key positioning:** Uses `usePositionStrategy` hook which accounts for `horizontalOffset` (lines 127-135)
- **The Problem:** For the bottom pane (generations pane), the PaneControlTab receives a `horizontalOffset` calculated at line 329-331:
  ```tsx
  horizontalOffset={
    (isShotsPaneLocked ? shotsPaneWidth : 0) - (isTasksPaneLocked ? tasksPaneWidth : 0)
  }
  ```
  This is used in BottomPaneStrategy (line 17) to shift the control's position via transform.

**3. MediaLightbox & Body Scroll Lock**
- **Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/components/LightboxShell.tsx`
- **Scroll locking mechanism (lines 145-177):**
  - Adds `lightbox-open` class to `<html>` element
  - In `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/index.css`, this applies:
    ```css
    html.lightbox-open {
      overscroll-behavior: none;
    }
    html.lightbox-open body {
      overscroll-behavior: none;
    }
    ```
- **No overflow:hidden on body** - only `overscroll-behavior` is applied
- **Note:** The code explicitly avoids setting `overflow:hidden` on `<body>` to prevent iOS Safari issues (lines 136-143)

### Root Cause Analysis

The shift occurs due to **browser scrollbar width reflow**:

1. **Before lightbox opens:** Body may have a scrollbar visible (16px on most browsers)
2. **Lightbox adds `lightbox-open` class:** The `overscroll-behavior: none` prevents scroll, but doesn't hide the scrollbar or prevent layout shift
3. **Missing scrollbar compensation:** There's **no `padding-right`** or **`scrollbar-gutter`** compensation applied to body/html when the lightbox opens
4. **Generation pane is positioned from screen edges:** The pane uses `left` and `right` CSS values positioned from the viewport edges
5. **PaneControlTab centers using `left: 50%` + `translateX`:** The transform-based positioning is calculated at render time based on `horizontalOffset`
6. **Viewport width changes:** When the browser hides the scrollbar (due to scroll lock), the viewport effective width changes slightly, causing:
   - The `left: 50%` calculation shifts by ~8px (half of scrollbar width)
   - The transform calculation becomes misaligned
   - The control tab appears to move

### Technical Details

**Affected files:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/components/LightboxShell.tsx` (lines 145-177) - where scroll lock is applied
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/PaneControlTab.tsx` (lines 317-324) - bottom positioning uses `left: 50%` + transform
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/hooks/pane-positioning/strategies/BottomPaneStrategy.ts` (line 17) - transform calculation
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/index.css` (lines for `lightbox-open`) - no scrollbar-gutter or padding compensation

### Why This Happens

The current implementation uses:
- `overscroll-behavior: none` to prevent scrolling (not `overflow: hidden`)
- Browser defaults to showing/hiding scrollbar based on scroll capability
- When scroll is prevented, browser may optimize scrollbar visibility
- No CSS compensation for scrollbar-width changes
- PaneControlTab uses viewport-relative positioning (`left: 50%`) which is affected by scrollbar width changes

### Solution Strategy (for your reference)

To fix this, you'd need to either:
1. **Add `scrollbar-gutter: stable` to body/html** when lightbox opens - reserves scrollbar space
2. **Use `overflow: hidden` on body** (but address iOS Safari issues mentioned in code comments)
3. **Use `position: fixed` with `padding-right` compensation** for the body
4. **Use CSS `calc()` with safe viewport width units** instead of `left: 50%` for the control tab

The code comments (lines 136-143 of LightboxShell.tsx) explicitly explain why they avoid `overflow: hidden` - it breaks iOS Safari's visual viewport restoration after keyboard dismiss.

    76→  onShotChange?: (shotId: string) => void;
    77→  onAddToShot?: (targetShotId: string, generationId: string, imageUrl?: string, thumbUrl?: string) => Promise<boolean>;
    78→  onAddToShotWithoutPosition?: (targetShotId: string, generationId: string, imageUrl?: string, thumbUrl?: string) => Promise<boolean>;
    79→  onDelete?: (id: string) => void;
    80→  isDeleting?: string | null;
    81→  onApplySettings?: (metadata: GenerationMetadata | undefined) => void;
    82→  showTickForImageId?: string | null;
    83→  onShowTick?: (imageId: string) => void;
    84→  showTickForSecondaryImageId?: string | null;
    85→  onShowSecondaryTick?: (imageId: string) => void;
    86→  starred?: boolean;
    87→  onToggleStar?: (id: string, starred: boolean) => void;
    88→  showTaskDetails?: boolean;
    89→  taskDetailsData?: TaskDetailsData;
    90→  onShowTaskDetails?: () => void;
    91→  onCreateShot?: (shotName: string, files: File[]) => Promise<{shotId?: string; shotName?: string} | void>;
    92→  onNavigateToShot?: (shot: Shot, options?: { isNewlyCreated?: boolean }) => void;
    93→  toolTypeOverride?: string;
    94→  optimisticPositionedIds?: Set<string>;
    95→  optimisticUnpositionedIds?: Set<string>;
    96→  onOptimisticPositioned?: (mediaId: string, shotId: string) => void;
    97→  onOptimisticUnpositioned?: (mediaId: string, shotId: string) => void;
    98→  positionedInSelectedShot?: boolean;
    99→  associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot?: boolean;
   100→  onNavigateToGeneration?: (generationId: string) => void;
   101→  onOpenExternalGeneration?: (generationId: string, derivedContext?: string[]) => Promise<void>;
   102→  shotId?: string;
   103→  tasksPaneOpen?: boolean;
   104→  tasksPaneWidth?: number;
   105→  initialVariantId?: string;
   106→  adjacentSegments?: AdjacentSegmentsData;
   107→}
   108→
   109→// ============================================================================
   110→// Component
   111→// ============================================================================
   112→
   113→export const ImageLightbox: React.FC<ImageLightboxProps> = (props) => {
   114→  const {
   115→    media,
   116→    onClose,
   117→    onNext,
   118→    onPrevious,
   119→    readOnly = false,
   120→    showNavigation = true,
   121→    showImageEditTools = true,
   122→    showDownload = true,
   123→    showMagicEdit = true,
   124→    autoEnterInpaint = false,
   125→    hasNext = false,
   126→    hasPrevious = false,
   127→    allShots,
   128→    selectedShotId,
   129→    onShotChange,
   130→    onAddToShot,
   131→    onAddToShotWithoutPosition,
   132→    onDelete,
   133→    isDeleting,
   134→    onApplySettings,
   135→    showTickForImageId,
   136→    onShowTick,
   137→    showTickForSecondaryImageId,
   138→    onShowSecondaryTick,
   139→    starred,
   140→    showTaskDetails = false,
   141→    taskDetailsData,
   142→    onShowTaskDetails,
   143→    onCreateShot,
   144→    onNavigateToShot,
   145→    toolTypeOverride,
   146→    optimisticPositionedIds,
   147→    optimisticUnpositionedIds,
   148→    onOptimisticPositioned,
   149→    onOptimisticUnpositioned,
   150→    positionedInSelectedShot,
   151→    associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot,
   152→    onOpenExternalGeneration,
   153→    shotId,
   154→    tasksPaneOpen,
   155→    tasksPaneWidth,
   156→    initialVariantId,
   157→    adjacentSegments,
   158→  } = props;
   159→
   160→  // ========================================
   161→  // CORE SETUP
   162→  // ========================================
   163→
   164→  const isMobile = useIsMobile();
   165→  const { project, selectedProjectId } = useProject();
   166→  const projectAspectRatio = project?.aspect_ratio;
   167→  const { value: generationMethods } = useUserUIState('generationMethods', { onComputer: true, inCloud: true });
   168→  const isCloudMode = generationMethods.inCloud;
   169→  const isLocalGeneration = generationMethods.onComputer && !generationMethods.inCloud;
   170→
   171→  // Panes context for tasks pane
   172→  const {
   173→    isTasksPaneOpen: tasksPaneOpenContext,
   174→    setIsTasksPaneOpen: setTasksPaneOpenContext,
   175→    tasksPaneWidth: tasksPaneWidthContext,
   176→    isTasksPaneLocked,
   177→    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
   178→  } = usePanes();
   179→
   180→  // Use prop values if provided, else fall back to context
   181→  const effectiveTasksPaneOpen = tasksPaneOpen ?? tasksPaneOpenContext;
   182→  const effectiveTasksPaneWidth = tasksPaneWidth ?? tasksPaneWidthContext;
   183→
   184→  // Count cancellable tasks
   185→  const { data: statusCounts } = useTaskStatusCounts(selectedProjectId);
   186→  const cancellableTaskCount = statusCounts?.processing || 0;
   187→
   188→  // Refs
   189→  const contentRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   190→  const displayCanvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
   191→  const maskCanvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
   192→  const imageContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   193→  const variantsSectionRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   194→
   195→  // State
   196→  const [isDownloading, setIsDownloading] = useState(false);
   197→  const [isSelectOpen, setIsSelectOpen] = useState(false);
   198→  const [replaceImages, setReplaceImages] = useState(true);
   199→  const [variantParamsToLoad, setVariantParamsToLoad] = useState<Record<string, unknown> | null>(null);
   200→
   201→  // Compute actual generation ID
   202→  // For timeline images, media.id is shot_generations.id, not the generation ID
   203→  // So we need to prefer media.generation_id for variant fetching
   204→  const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
   205→  const variantFetchGenerationId = media.parent_generation_id || actualGenerationId;
   206→
   207→  // ========================================
   208→  // IMAGE DIMENSIONS
   209→  // ========================================
   210→
   211→  const resolutionToDimensions = (resolution: string): { width: number; height: number } | null => {
   212→    if (!resolution || typeof resolution !== 'string' || !resolution.includes('x')) return null;
   213→    const [w, h] = resolution.split('x').map(Number);
   214→    if (!isNaN(w) && !isNaN(h) && w > 0 && h > 0) {
   215→      return { width: w, height: h };
   216→    }
   217→    return null;
   218→  };
   219→
   220→  const aspectRatioToDimensions = (aspectRatio: string): { width: number; height: number } | null => {
   221→    if (!aspectRatio) return null;
   222→    const directResolution = ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION[aspectRatio];
   223→    if (directResolution) return resolutionToDimensions(directResolution);
   224→    const ratio = parseRatio(aspectRatio);
   225→    if (!isNaN(ratio)) {
   226→      const closestAspectRatio = findClosestAspectRatio(ratio);
   227→      const closestResolution = ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION[closestAspectRatio];
   228→      if (closestResolution) return resolutionToDimensions(closestResolution);
   229→    }
   230→    return null;
   231→  };
   232→
   233→  const extractDimensionsFromMedia = useCallback((mediaObj: typeof media): { width: number; height: number } | null => {
   234→    if (!mediaObj) return null;
   235→    const params = mediaObj.params as GenerationParams | undefined;
   236→    const metadata = mediaObj.metadata as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   237→
   238→    // Check metadata for width/height (these may exist as extended metadata fields)
   239→    if (metadata?.width && metadata?.height && typeof metadata.width === 'number' && typeof metadata.height === 'number') {
   240→      return { width: metadata.width, height: metadata.height };
   241→    }
   242→
   243→    const resolutionSources = [
   244→      params?.resolution,
   245→      (params as Record<string, unknown>)?.originalParams,
   246→      (params as Record<string, unknown>)?.orchestrator_details,
   247→      metadata?.resolution,
   248→      (metadata?.originalParams as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.resolution,
   249→      ((metadata?.originalParams as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.orchestrator_details as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.resolution,
   250→    ];
   251→    for (const res of resolutionSources) {
   252→      if (typeof res === 'string') {
   253→        const dims = resolutionToDimensions(res);
   254→        if (dims) return dims;
   255→      }
   256→    }
   257→
   258→    const aspectRatioSources = [
   259→      params?.aspect_ratio,
   260→      (params as Record<string, unknown>)?.custom_aspect_ratio,
   261→      (params as Record<string, unknown>)?.originalParams,
   262→      (params as Record<string, unknown>)?.orchestrator_details,
   263→      metadata?.aspect_ratio,
   264→      (metadata?.originalParams as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.aspect_ratio,
   265→      ((metadata?.originalParams as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.orchestrator_details as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.aspect_ratio,
   266→    ];
   267→    for (const ar of aspectRatioSources) {
   268→      if (ar && typeof ar === 'string') {
   269→        const dims = aspectRatioToDimensions(ar);
   270→        if (dims) return dims;
   271→      }
   272→    }
   273→
   274→    return null;
   275→  }, []);
   276→
   277→  const [imageDimensions, setImageDimensions] = useState<{ width: number; height: number } | null>(() => {
   278→    return extractDimensionsFromMedia(media);
   279→  });
   280→
   281→  React.useLayoutEffect(() => {
   282→    const dims = extractDimensionsFromMedia(media);
   283→    if (dims) setImageDimensions(dims);
   284→  }, [media?.id, extractDimensionsFromMedia]);
   285→
   286→  // ========================================
   287→  // UPSCALE HOOK
   288→  // ========================================
   289→
   290→  const upscaleHook = useUpscale({ media, selectedProjectId, isVideo: false, shotId });
   291→  const {
   292→    effectiveImageUrl,
   293→    sourceUrlForTasks,
   294→    isUpscaling,
   295→    upscaleSuccess,
   296→    showingUpscaled,
   297→    isPendingUpscale,
   298→    hasUpscaledVersion,
   299→    handleUpscale,
   300→    handleToggleUpscaled,
   301→    setActiveVariant: setUpscaleActiveVariant,
   302→  } = upscaleHook;
   303→
   304→  // ========================================
   305→  // EDIT SETTINGS PERSISTENCE
   306→  // ========================================
   307→
   308→  const editSettingsPersistence = useEditSettingsPersistence({
   309→    generationId: actualGenerationId,
   310→    projectId: selectedProjectId,
   311→    enabled: true,
   312→  });
   313→  const {
   314→    loraMode,
   315→    setLoraMode,
   316→    customLoraUrl,
   317→    setCustomLoraUrl,
   318→    prompt: persistedPrompt,
   319→    setPrompt: setPersistedPrompt,
   320→    numGenerations: persistedNumGenerations,
   321→    setNumGenerations: setPersistedNumGenerations,
   322→    img2imgStrength: persistedImg2imgStrength,
   323→    setImg2imgStrength: setPersistedImg2imgStrength,
   324→    img2imgEnablePromptExpansion: persistedImg2imgEnablePromptExpansion,
   325→    setImg2imgEnablePromptExpansion: setPersistedImg2imgEnablePromptExpansion,
   326→    img2imgPrompt: persistedImg2imgPrompt,
   327→    setImg2imgPrompt: setPersistedImg2imgPrompt,
   328→    img2imgPromptHasBeenSet: persistedImg2imgPromptHasBeenSet,
   329→    editModeLoRAs,
   330→    setEditModeLoRAs,
   331→    createAsGeneration,
   332→    setCreateAsGeneration,
   333→    advancedSettings,
   334→    setAdvancedSettings,
   335→    qwenEditModel,
   336→    setQwenEditModel,
   337→    editMode: persistedEditMode,
   338→    setEditMode: setPersistedEditMode,
   339→    panelMode: persistedPanelMode,
   340→    setPanelMode: setPersistedPanelMode,
   341→    isReady: isEditSettingsReady,
   342→    hasPersistedSettings,
   343→  } = editSettingsPersistence;
   344→
   345→  // ========================================
   346→  // SHARED LIGHTBOX STATE
   347→  // ========================================
   348→
   349→  // Navigation handlers (for slot-based navigation in segment mode)
   350→  const handleSlotNavNext = useCallback(() => {
   351→    if (onNext) onNext();
   352→  }, [onNext]);
   353→
   354→  const handleSlotNavPrev = useCallback(() => {
   355→    if (onPrevious) onPrevious();
   356→  }, [onPrevious]);
   357→
   358→  // STATE SYNC PATTERN NOTE:
   359→  // useSharedLightboxState needs isInpaintMode/isMagicEditMode for layout calculations,
   360→  // but these come from hooks called AFTER useSharedLightboxState.
   361→  // We use local state as a proxy, updated via useEffect after the hooks run.
   362→  // This causes a one-frame delay but is acceptable for swipe/layout calculations.
   363→  // TODO (Phase 5): Consider restructuring to avoid this sync pattern.
   364→  const [isInpaintModeLocal, setIsInpaintModeLocal] = useState(false);
   365→  const [isMagicEditModeLocal, setIsMagicEditModeLocal] = useState(false);
   366→
   367→  const sharedState = useSharedLightboxState({
   368→    media,
   369→    isVideo: false,
   370→    selectedProjectId,
   371→    isMobile,
   372→    isFormOnlyMode: false,
   373→    onClose,
   374→    readOnly,
   375→    variantFetchGenerationId,
   376→    initialVariantId,
   377→    showNavigation,
   378→    hasNext,
   379→    hasPrevious,
   380→    handleSlotNavNext,
   381→    handleSlotNavPrev,
   382→    shotId,
   383→    selectedShotId,
   384→    allShots,
   385→    onShotChange,
   386→    onAddToShot,
   387→    onAddToShotWithoutPosition,
   388→    onNavigateToShot,
   389→    onShowTick,
   390→    onShowSecondaryTick,
   391→    onOptimisticPositioned,
   392→    onOptimisticUnpositioned,
   393→    optimisticPositionedIds,
   394→    optimisticUnpositionedIds,
   395→    positionedInSelectedShot,
   396→    associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot,
   397→    starred,
   398→    onOpenExternalGeneration,
   399→    showTaskDetails,
   400→    isSpecialEditMode: isMagicEditModeLocal,
   401→    isInpaintMode: isInpaintModeLocal,
   402→    isMagicEditMode: isMagicEditModeLocal,
   403→    isCloudMode,
   404→    showDownload,
   405→    isDownloading,
   406→    setIsDownloading,
   407→    onDelete,
   408→    isDeleting,
   409→    isUpscaling,
   410→    isPendingUpscale,
   411→    hasUpscaledVersion,
   412→    showingUpscaled,
   413→    handleUpscale,
   414→    handleToggleUpscaled,
   415→    handleEnterMagicEditMode: () => {}, // Will be replaced after hook
   416→    effectiveImageUrl,
   417→    imageDimensions: imageDimensions || { width: 1024, height: 1024 },
   418→    projectAspectRatio,
   419→    swipeDisabled: isMagicEditModeLocal || readOnly,
   420→  });
   421→
   422→  // Extract shared state
   423→  const {
   424→    variants,
   425→    intendedActiveVariantIdRef,
   426→    navigation,
   427→    star,
   428→    references,
   429→    lineage,
   430→    shots,
   431→    sourceGeneration,
   432→    makeMainVariant,
   433→    effectiveMedia,
   434→    layout,
   435→    buttonGroupProps,
   436→  } = sharedState;
   437→
   438→  // Keep upscale hook in sync with active variant so it enhances the viewed image
   439→  useEffect(() => {
   440→    setUpscaleActiveVariant(variants.activeVariant?.location, variants.activeVariant?.id);
   441→  }, [variants.activeVariant?.location, variants.activeVariant?.id, setUpscaleActiveVariant]);
   442→
   443→  // ========================================
   444→  // LORA MANAGEMENT
   445→  // ========================================
   446→
   447→  const { data: availableLoras } = usePublicLoras();
   448→
   449→  const editLoraManager = useLoraManager(availableLoras, {
   450→    projectId: selectedProjectId || undefined,
   451→    persistenceScope: 'none',
   452→    enableProjectPersistence: false,
   453→    disableAutoLoad: true,
   454→  });
   455→
   456→  const effectiveEditModeLoRAs = useMemo(() => {
   457→    if (editLoraManager.selectedLoras.length > 0) {
   458→      return editLoraManager.selectedLoras.map(lora => ({
   459→        url: lora.path,
   460→        strength: lora.strength,
   461→      }));
   462→    }
   463→    return editModeLoRAs;
   464→  }, [editLoraManager.selectedLoras, editModeLoRAs]);
   465→
   466→  // ========================================
   467→  // INPAINTING HOOK
   468→  // ========================================
   469→
   470→  const inpaintingHook = useInpainting({
   471→    media,
   472→    selectedProjectId,
   473→    shotId,
   474→    toolTypeOverride,
   475→    isVideo: false,
   476→    displayCanvasRef,
   477→    maskCanvasRef,
   478→    imageContainerRef,
   479→    imageDimensions,
   480→    handleExitInpaintMode: () => {},
   481→    loras: effectiveEditModeLoRAs,
   482→    activeVariantId: variants.activeVariant?.id,
   483→    activeVariantLocation: variants.activeVariant?.location,
   484→    createAsGeneration,
   485→    advancedSettings,
   486→    qwenEditModel,
   487→    imageUrl: variants.activeVariant?.location || effectiveImageUrl,
   488→    thumbnailUrl: variants.activeVariant?.thumbnail_url || media.thumbUrl,
   489→    initialEditMode: persistedEditMode,
   490→  });
   491→
   492→  const {
   493→    isInpaintMode,
   494→    brushStrokes,
   495→    isEraseMode,
   496→    inpaintPrompt,
   497→    inpaintNumGenerations,
   498→    brushSize,
   499→    isGeneratingInpaint,
   500→    inpaintGenerateSuccess,
   501→    isAnnotateMode,
   502→    editMode,
   503→    annotationMode,
   504→    selectedShapeId,
   505→    showTextModeHint,
   506→    setIsInpaintMode,
   507→    setIsEraseMode,
   508→    setInpaintPrompt,
   509→    setInpaintNumGenerations,
   510→    setBrushSize,
   511→    setIsAnnotateMode,
   512→    setEditMode,
   513→    setAnnotationMode,
   514→    handleKonvaPointerDown,
   515→    handleKonvaPointerMove,
   516→    handleKonvaPointerUp,
   517→    handleShapeClick,
   518→    handleUndo,
   519→    handleClearMask,
   520→    handleEnterInpaintMode,
   521→    handleGenerateInpaint,
   522→    handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit,
   523→    handleDeleteSelected,
   524→    handleToggleFreeForm,
   525→    getDeleteButtonPosition,
   526→    strokeOverlayRef,
   527→    isImageLoaded: isInpaintImageLoaded,
   528→    imageLoadError: inpaintImageLoadError,
   529→    isDrawing,
   530→    currentStroke,
   531→  } = inpaintingHook;
   532→
   533→  // ========================================
   534→  // EDIT SETTINGS SYNC
   535→  // ========================================
   536→
   537→  useEditSettingsSync({
   538→    actualGenerationId,
   539→    isEditSettingsReady,
   540→    hasPersistedSettings,
   541→    persistedEditMode,
   542→    persistedNumGenerations,
   543→    persistedPrompt,
   544→    editMode,
   545→    inpaintNumGenerations,
   546→    inpaintPrompt,
   547→    setEditMode,
   548→    setInpaintNumGenerations,
   549→    setInpaintPrompt,
   550→    setPersistedEditMode,
   551→    setPersistedNumGenerations,
   552→    setPersistedPrompt,
   553→  });
   554→
   555→  const handleExitInpaintMode = () => {
   556→    setIsInpaintMode(false);
   557→  };
   558→
   559→  // ========================================
   560→  // MAGIC EDIT MODE HOOK
   561→  // ========================================
   562→
   563→  const magicEditHook = useMagicEditMode({
   564→    media,
   565→    selectedProjectId,
   566→    autoEnterInpaint,
   567→    isVideo: false,
   568→    isInpaintMode,
   569→    setIsInpaintMode,
   570→    handleEnterInpaintMode,
   571→    handleGenerateInpaint,
   572→    brushStrokes,
   573→    inpaintPrompt,
   574→    setInpaintPrompt,
   575→    inpaintNumGenerations,
   576→    setInpaintNumGenerations,
   577→    editModeLoRAs: effectiveEditModeLoRAs,
   578→    sourceUrlForTasks,
   579→    imageDimensions,
   580→    toolTypeOverride,
   581→    isInSceneBoostEnabled: false,
   582→    setIsInSceneBoostEnabled: () => {},
   583→    activeVariantId: variants.activeVariant?.id,
   584→    activeVariantLocation: variants.activeVariant?.location,
   585→    createAsGeneration,
   586→    advancedSettings,
   587→    qwenEditModel,
   588→    enabled: true,
   589→  });
   590→
   591→  const {
   592→    isMagicEditMode,
   593→    setIsMagicEditMode,
   594→    magicEditPrompt,
   595→    setMagicEditPrompt,
   596→    magicEditNumImages,
   597→    setMagicEditNumImages,
   598→    isCreatingMagicEditTasks,
   599→    magicEditTasksCreated,
   600→    inpaintPanelPosition,
   601→    setInpaintPanelPosition,
   602→    handleEnterMagicEditMode,
   603→    handleExitMagicEditMode,
   604→    handleUnifiedGenerate,
   605→    isSpecialEditMode,
   606→  } = magicEditHook;
   607→
   608→  // Sync edit mode state to shared state hook (see STATE SYNC PATTERN NOTE above)
   609→  // This runs after useMagicEditMode provides the real values
   610→  useEffect(() => {
   611→    setIsInpaintModeLocal(isInpaintMode);
   612→    setIsMagicEditModeLocal(isMagicEditMode);
   613→  }, [isInpaintMode, isMagicEditMode]);
   614→
   615→  // ========================================
   616→  // REPOSITION MODE HOOK
   617→  // ========================================
   618→
   619→  const repositionHook = useRepositionMode({
   620→    media,
   621→    selectedProjectId,
   622→    imageDimensions,
   623→    imageContainerRef,
   624→    loras: effectiveEditModeLoRAs,
   625→    inpaintPrompt,
   626→    inpaintNumGenerations,
   627→    handleExitInpaintMode: handleExitMagicEditMode,
   628→    toolTypeOverride,
   629→    shotId,
   630→    onVariantCreated: variants.setActiveVariantId,
   631→    refetchVariants: variants.refetch,
   632→    createAsGeneration,
   633→    advancedSettings,
   634→    activeVariantLocation: variants.activeVariant?.location,
   635→    activeVariantId: variants.activeVariant?.id,
   636→    activeVariantParams: variants.activeVariant?.params as Record<string, unknown> | null,
   637→    qwenEditModel,
   638→  });
   639→
   640→  const {
   641→    transform: repositionTransform,
   642→    hasTransformChanges,
   643→    isGeneratingReposition,
   644→    repositionGenerateSuccess,
   645→    isSavingAsVariant,
   646→    saveAsVariantSuccess,
   647→    setTranslateX,
   648→    setTranslateY,
   649→    setScale,
   650→    setRotation,
   651→    toggleFlipH,
   652→    toggleFlipV,
   653→    resetTransform,
   654→    handleGenerateReposition,
   655→    handleSaveAsVariant,
   656→    getTransformStyle,
   657→    isDragging: isRepositionDragging,
   658→    dragHandlers: repositionDragHandlers,
   659→  } = repositionHook;
   660→
   661→  // ========================================
   662→  // IMG2IMG MODE HOOK
   663→  // ========================================
   664→
   665→  const img2imgHook = useImg2ImgMode({
   666→    media,
   667→    selectedProjectId,
   668→    isVideo: false,
   669→    sourceUrlForTasks,
   670→    toolTypeOverride,
   671→    createAsGeneration,
   672→    availableLoras,
   673→    img2imgStrength: persistedImg2imgStrength,
   674→    setImg2imgStrength: setPersistedImg2imgStrength,
   675→    enablePromptExpansion: persistedImg2imgEnablePromptExpansion,
   676→    setEnablePromptExpansion: setPersistedImg2imgEnablePromptExpansion,
   677→    img2imgPrompt: persistedImg2imgPrompt,
   678→    setImg2imgPrompt: setPersistedImg2imgPrompt,
   679→    img2imgPromptHasBeenSet: persistedImg2imgPromptHasBeenSet,
   680→    numGenerations: persistedNumGenerations,
   681→    activeVariantLocation: variants.activeVariant?.location,
   682→    activeVariantId: variants.activeVariant?.id,
   683→    shotId,
   684→  });
   685→
   686→  const {
   687→    img2imgPrompt,
   688→    img2imgStrength,

   969→  const handleDownload = async () => {
   970→    let urlToDownload: string | undefined;
   971→    const intendedVariantId = intendedActiveVariantIdRef.current;
   972→
   973→    if (intendedVariantId && variants.list.length > 0) {
   974→      const intendedVariant = variants.list.find((v) => v.id === intendedVariantId);
   975→      if (intendedVariant?.location) {
   976→        urlToDownload = intendedVariant.location;
   977→      }
   978→    }
   979→
   980→    if (!urlToDownload) {
   981→      urlToDownload = effectiveMedia.mediaUrl;
   982→    }
   983→
   984→    if (!urlToDownload) return;
   985→
   986→    setIsDownloading(true);
   987→    try {
   988→      const segmentOverrides = readSegmentOverrides(media.metadata as Record<string, unknown> | null);
   989→      const prompt = media.params?.prompt ||
   990→                     (media.metadata as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.enhanced_prompt as string | undefined ||
   991→                     segmentOverrides.prompt ||
   992→                     (media.metadata as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.prompt as string | undefined;
   993→      await downloadMedia(urlToDownload, media.id, false, media.contentType, prompt);
   994→    } finally {
   995→      setIsDownloading(false);
   996→    }
   997→  };

  1010→      onClose();
  1011→      onNavigateToShot(minimalShot);
  1012→    }

1→/**
     2→ * VideoLightbox
     3→ *
     4→ * Specialized lightbox for video media. Handles all video-specific functionality:
     5→ * - Video trimming
     6→ * - Video replace mode
     7→ * - Video regenerate mode
     8→ * - Video enhance mode
     9→ * - Segment slot mode (timeline navigation)
    10→ *
    11→ * Uses useSharedLightboxState for shared functionality (variants, navigation, etc.)
    12→ *
    13→ * This is part of the split architecture where MediaLightbox dispatches to
    14→ * ImageLightbox or VideoLightbox based on media type.
    15→ */
    16→
    17→import React, { useState, useRef, useMemo, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';

   123→export const VideoLightbox: React.FC<VideoLightboxProps> = (props) => {

   172→
   173→  // ========================================

   177→  const isSegmentSlotMode = !!segmentSlotMode;

   191→  const hasPrevious = isSegmentSlotMode

   194→
   195→  const handleSlotNavNext = useCallback(() => {

   202→

   245→

   253→
   254→  // ========================================
   255→  // VIDEO EDIT SUB-MODE STATE

   257→
   258→  const [videoEditSubMode, setVideoEditSubModeLocal] = useState<'trim' | 'replace' | 'regenerate' | 'enhance' | null>(() => {
   259→    if (initialVideoTrimMode) return 'trim';
   260→    // Check localStorage for persisted mode
   261→    try {
   262→      const [REDACTED] ? `lightbox-edit-last-used-${selectedProjectId}` : null;
   263→      const stored = projectKey ? localStorage.getItem(projectKey) : localStorage.getItem('lightbox-edit-last-used-global');
   264→      if (stored) {

   269→      }
   270→    } catch (e) {

   273→    return null;
   274→  });
   275→
   276→  // ========================================
   277→  // EDIT SETTINGS PERSISTENCE

   280→  const editSettingsPersistence = useEditSettingsPersistence({

   285→

   292→    setPanelMode: setPersistedPanelMode,
   293→  } = editSettingsPersistence;
   294→
   295→  // Wrapper for setVideoEditSubMode that also persists
   296→  const setVideoEditSubMode = useCallback((mode: 'trim' | 'replace' | 'regenerate' | null) => {
   297→    setVideoEditSubModeLocal(mode);

   299→      setPersistedVideoEditSubMode(mode);

   301→  }, [setPersistedVideoEditSubMode]);
   302→
   303→  // ========================================
   304→  // IMAGE DIMENSIONS (for video thumbnails)
   305→  // ========================================
   306→
   307→  const resolutionToDimensions = (resolution: string): { width: number; height: number } | null => {
   308→    if (!resolution || typeof resolution !== 'string' || !resolution.includes('x')) return null;
   309→    const [w, h] = resolution.split('x').map(Number);
   310→    if (!isNaN(w) && !isNaN(h) && w > 0 && h > 0) {
   311→      return { width: w, height: h };
   312→    }
   313→    return null;
   314→  };
   315→
   316→  const aspectRatioToDimensions = (aspectRatio: string): { width: number; height: number } | null => {
   317→    if (!aspectRatio) return null;
   318→    const directResolution = ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION[aspectRatio];
   319→    if (directResolution) return resolutionToDimensions(directResolution);
   320→    const ratio = parseRatio(aspectRatio);
   321→    if (!isNaN(ratio)) {
   322→      const closestAspectRatio = findClosestAspectRatio(ratio);
   323→      const closestResolution = ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION[closestAspectRatio];
   324→      if (closestResolution) return resolutionToDimensions(closestResolution);
   325→    }
   326→    return null;
   327→  };
   328→
   329→  const extractDimensionsFromMedia = useCallback((mediaObj: typeof media): { width: number; height: number } | null => {
   330→    if (!mediaObj) return null;
   331→    const params = mediaObj.params as GenerationParams | undefined;
   332→    const metadata = mediaObj.metadata as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   333→
   334→    // Check metadata for width/height (these may exist as extended metadata fields)
   335→    if (metadata?.width && metadata?.height && typeof metadata.width === 'number' && typeof metadata.height === 'number') {
   336→      return { width: metadata.width, height: metadata.height };
   337→    }
   338→
   339→    const resolutionSources = [
   340→      params?.resolution,
   341→      (params as Record<string, unknown>)?.originalParams,
   342→      metadata?.resolution,
   343→    ];
   344→    for (const res of resolutionSources) {
   345→      if (typeof res === 'string') {
   346→        const dims = resolutionToDimensions(res);
   347→        if (dims) return dims;
   348→      }
   349→    }
   350→
   351→    const aspectRatioSources = [
   352→      params?.aspect_ratio,
   353→      metadata?.aspect_ratio,
   354→    ];
   355→    for (const ar of aspectRatioSources) {
   356→      if (ar && typeof ar === 'string') {
   357→        const dims = aspectRatioToDimensions(ar);
   358→        if (dims) return dims;
   359→      }
   360→    }
   361→
   362→    return null;
   363→  }, []);
   364→
   365→  const [imageDimensions, setImageDimensions] = useState<{ width: number; height: number } | null>(() => {
   366→    return media ? extractDimensionsFromMedia(media) : null;
   367→  });
   368→
   369→  React.useLayoutEffect(() => {

   371→    const dims = extractDimensionsFromMedia(media);
   372→    if (dims) setImageDimensions(dims);

   561→  useEffect(() => {
