```
    96→    onMarkAllViewed,
    97→    handlePromoteToGeneration: onPromoteToGeneration,
    98→    isPromoting,
    99→    handleDeleteVariant: onDeleteVariant,
   100→    onLoadVariantSettings,
   101→    onLoadVariantImages,
   102→    currentSegmentImages,
   103→  } = useLightboxVariantsSafe();
   104→
   105→  const hasVariants = variants && variants.length >= 1;
   106→  const [idCopied, setIdCopied] = useState(false);
   107→
   108→  // Get task ID for copy functionality - use prop as fallback for videos
   109→  const taskId = taskDetailsData?.taskId || taskIdProp;
   110→
   111→  // Responsive styles
   112→  const variantsMaxHeight = isMobile ? 'max-h-[120px]' : 'max-h-[200px]';
   113→
   114→  // Render the Info/Edit toggle for images
   115→  const renderImageToggle = () => {
   116→    if (!showImageEditTools || readOnly || isVideo) return null;
   117→
   118→    return (
   119→      <SegmentedControl
   120→        value={isInpaintMode ? 'edit' : 'info'}
   121→        onValueChange={(value) => {
   122→          if (value === 'info' && isInpaintMode) {
   123→            handleExitInpaintMode();
   124→          } else if (value === 'edit' && !isInpaintMode) {
   125→            handleEnterInpaintMode();
   126→          }
   127→        }}
   128→      >
   129→        <SegmentedControlItem value="info">Info</SegmentedControlItem>
   130→        <SegmentedControlItem value="edit">Edit</SegmentedControlItem>
   131→      </SegmentedControl>
   132→    );
   133→  };
   134→
   135→  // Render the Info/Edit toggle for videos
   136→  const renderVideoToggle = () => {
   137→    if (!isVideo || readOnly) return null;
   138→
   139→    return (
   140→      <SegmentedControl
   141→        value={isInVideoEditMode ? 'edit' : 'info'}
   142→        onValueChange={(value) => {
   143→          if (value === 'info' && isInVideoEditMode) {
   144→            handleExitVideoEditMode();
   145→          } else if (value === 'edit' && !isInVideoEditMode) {
   146→            handleEnterVideoEditMode();
   147→          }
   148→        }}
   149→      >
   150→        <SegmentedControlItem value="info">Info</SegmentedControlItem>
   151→        <SegmentedControlItem value="edit">Edit</SegmentedControlItem>
   152→      </SegmentedControl>
   153→    );
   154→  };
   155→
   156→  // Handle ID copy with simple approach that works on all devices
   157→  // Set state immediately (synchronously) for instant feedback, don't wait for clipboard promise
   158→  const handleCopyId = () => {
   159→    if (!taskId) return;
   160→    setIdCopied(true);
   161→    setTimeout(() => setIdCopied(false), 2000);
   162→    // Copy to clipboard async - don't block on it
   163→    navigator.clipboard.writeText(taskId).catch(() => {
   164→      // Silently fail - we already showed feedback
   165→    });
   166→  };
   167→
   168→  // Render the header - consistent single row layout for both mobile and desktop
   169→  const renderHeader = () => (
   170→    <div className={cn(
   171→      "flex-shrink-0 border-b border-border bg-background",
   172→      isMobile ? "sticky top-0 z-[80] px-3 py-2" : "p-4"
   173→    )}>
   174→      {/* Single row: ID + variants on left, toggle + close on right */}
   175→      <div className={cn(
   176→        "flex items-center justify-between",
   177→        isMobile ? "gap-2" : "gap-3"
   178→      )}>
   179→        {/* Left side - copy id + variants link */}
   180→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   181→          {taskId && (
   182→            <button
   183→              onClick={handleCopyId}
   184→              className={cn(
   185→                "px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded transition-colors touch-manipulation",
   186→                idCopied
   187→                  ? "text-green-400 bg-green-400/10"
   188→                  : "text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-700"
   189→              )}
   190→            >
   191→              {idCopied ? 'copied' : 'id'}
   192→            </button>
   193→          )}
   194→          {hasVariants && (
   195→            <button
   196→              onClick={() => variantsSectionRef?.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' })}
   197→              className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded bg-muted/50 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted transition-colors touch-manipulation"
   198→            >
   199→              <span>{variants.length} variants</span>
   200→              <svg className="w-2.5 h-2.5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
   201→                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M19 14l-7 7m0 0l-7-7m7 7V3" />
   202→              </svg>
   203→            </button>
   204→          )}
   205→          {pendingTaskCount > 0 ? (
   206→            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded bg-primary/10 text-primary">
   207→              <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" />
   208→              <span>{pendingTaskCount} pending</span>
   209→            </div>
   210→          ) : variants && variants.length > 1 && unviewedVariantCount > 0 ? (
   211→            <VariantBadge
   212→              variant="inline"
   213→              derivedCount={variants.length}
   214→              unviewedVariantCount={unviewedVariantCount}
   215→              hasUnviewedVariants={true}
   216→              tooltipSide="bottom"
   217→              onMarkAllViewed={onMarkAllViewed}
   218→              onClick={() => variantsSectionRef?.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' })}
   219→            />
   220→          ) : null}
   221→        </div>
   222→
   223→        {/* Right side - toggles and close button */}
   224→        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
   225→          {renderImageToggle()}
   226→          {renderVideoToggle()}
   227→          <Button
   228→            variant="ghost"
   229→            size="sm"
   230→            onClick={onClose}
   231→            className={cn("p-0 hover:bg-muted", isMobile ? "h-7 w-7" : "h-8 w-8")}
   232→          >
   233→            <X className={cn(isMobile ? "h-3.5 w-3.5" : "h-4 w-4")} />
   234→          </Button>
   235→        </div>
   236→      </div>
   237→    </div>
   238→  );
   239→
   240→  // Render task details wrapper
   241→  const renderTaskDetails = () => (
   242→    <TaskDetailsPanelWrapper
   243→      taskDetailsData={taskDetailsData}
   244→      derivedItems={derivedItems}
   245→      derivedGenerations={derivedGenerations}
   246→      paginatedDerived={paginatedDerived}
   247→      derivedPage={derivedPage}
   248→      derivedTotalPages={derivedTotalPages}
   249→      onSetDerivedPage={onSetDerivedPage}
   250→      onNavigateToGeneration={onNavigateToGeneration}
   251→      onVariantSelect={onVariantSelect}
   252→      currentMediaId={currentMediaId}
   253→      currentShotId={currentShotId}
   254→      replaceImages={replaceImages}
   255→      onReplaceImagesChange={onReplaceImagesChange}
   256→      onClose={onClose}
   257→      variant={variant}
   258→      activeVariant={activeVariant}
   259→      primaryVariant={primaryVariant}
   260→      onSwitchToPrimary={onSwitchToPrimary}
   261→    />
   262→  );
   263→
   264→  // Render variants section - matches EditPanelLayout styling
   265→  const renderVariants = () => {
   266→    if (!hasVariants) return null;
   267→
   268→    // Match EditPanelLayout: border-t, consistent padding
   269→    const variantPadding = isMobile ? 'pt-2 mt-2 px-3 pb-2' : 'pt-4 mt-4 p-6';
   270→
   271→    return (
   272→      <div
   273→        ref={variantsSectionRef}
   274→        className={cn("border-t border-border", variantPadding)}
   275→      >
   276→        <VariantSelector
   277→          variants={variants}
   278→          activeVariantId={activeVariant?.id || null}
   279→          onVariantSelect={onVariantSelect}
   280→          onMakePrimary={onMakePrimary}
   281→          isLoading={isLoadingVariants}
   282→          onPromoteToGeneration={onPromoteToGeneration}
   283→          isPromoting={isPromoting}
   284→          onDeleteVariant={onDeleteVariant}
   285→          onLoadVariantSettings={onLoadVariantSettings}
   286→          onLoadVariantImages={onLoadVariantImages}
   287→          currentSegmentImages={currentSegmentImages}
   288→          readOnly={readOnly}
   289→        />
   290→      </div>
   291→    );
   292→  };
   293→
   294→  // Both desktop and mobile: variants inside scroll area (matches EditPanelLayout)
   295→  return (
   296→    <div className={cn("w-full flex flex-col", !isMobile && "h-full")}>
   297→      {renderHeader()}
   298→
   299→      {/* Scrollable content area - contains both task details and variants */}
   300→      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto min-h-0 overscroll-none">
   301→        {renderTaskDetails()}
   302→        {renderVariants()}
   303→      </div>
   304→    </div>
   305→  );
   306→};
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * VariantSelector Component
     3→ *
     4→ * Displays a grid of clickable variant thumbnails to switch between variants.
     5→ * Shows which variant is primary and which is currently active.
     6→ * Shows variant relationships (what it's based on / what's based on it).
     7→ * Allows filtering by relationship and making the current variant primary.
     8→ */
     9→

    21→// Lazy load LineageGifModal since it's only opened on demand
    22→const LazyLineageGifModal = React.lazy(() =>
    23→  import('@/shared/components/LineageGifModal').then(module => ({
    24→    default: module.LineageGifModal
    25→  }))
    26→);
    27→import { getLineageDepth } from '@/shared/hooks/useLineageChain';
    28→import { getSourceTaskId } from '@/shared/lib/taskIdHelpers';
    29→import { useToggleVariantStar } from '@/shared/hooks/useToggleVariantStar';
    30→import type { GenerationVariant } from '@/shared/hooks/useVariants';
    31→import type { RelationshipFilter, CurrentSegmentImagesData } from './utils';
    32→import { VariantGrid } from './components/VariantGrid';
    33→import { MobileInfoModal } from './components/MobileInfoModal';
    34→
    35→/**
    36→ * Props for VariantSelector component
    37→ */
    38→interface VariantSelectorProps {
    39→  /** List of variants */
    40→  variants: GenerationVariant[];
    41→  /** Currently active variant ID */
    42→  activeVariantId: string | null;
    43→  /** Handler for variant selection */
    44→  onVariantSelect: (variantId: string) => void;
    45→  /** Handler to make a variant primary */
    46→  onMakePrimary?: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;

    49→  /** Handler to promote variant to a standalone generation */

    51→  /** Whether a promotion is currently in progress */

    53→  /** Handler to load a variant's settings into the regenerate form */

    55→  /** Handler to delete a variant (not available for primary variant) */
    56→  onDeleteVariant?: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
    57→  /** Read-only mode - hides action buttons (Make Primary, Promote, Delete) */
    58→  readOnly?: boolean;
    59→  /** Handler to load a variant's source images onto the timeline */
    60→  onLoadVariantImages?: (variant: GenerationVariant) => void;
    61→  /** Current segment images data for comparison */
    62→  currentSegmentImages?: CurrentSegmentImagesData;
    63→}
    64→
    65→export const VariantSelector: React.FC<VariantSelectorProps> = ({
    66→  variants,
    67→  activeVariantId,
    68→  onVariantSelect,
    69→  onMakePrimary,
    70→  isLoading = false,
    71→  onPromoteToGeneration,
    72→  isPromoting = false,
    73→  onLoadVariantSettings,
    74→  onDeleteVariant,
    75→  readOnly = false,
    76→  onLoadVariantImages,
    77→  currentSegmentImages,
    78→}) => {
    79→  const [isMakingPrimary, setIsMakingPrimary] = useState(false);
    80→  const [localIsPromoting, setLocalIsPromoting] = useState(false);
    81→  const [promoteSuccess, setPromoteSuccess] = useState(false);
    82→  const [relationshipFilter, setRelationshipFilter] = useState<RelationshipFilter>('all');
    83→  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(0);
    84→  const [loadedSettingsVariantId, setLoadedSettingsVariantId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    85→  const [copiedVariantId, setCopiedVariantId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    86→  const [loadedImagesVariantId, setLoadedImagesVariantId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    87→  const deleteOperation = useAsyncOperationMap();
    88→  const [lineageGifVariantId, setLineageGifVariantId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    89→  const [mobileInfoVariantId, setMobileInfoVariantId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    90→  const [variantLineageDepth, setVariantLineageDepth] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
    91→  const isMobile = useIsMobile();

    95→  const starredCount = useMemo(() => variants.filter(v => v.starred).length, [variants]);
    96→
    97→  const checkedLineageIdsRef = React.useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
    98→  const prefetchTaskData = usePrefetchTaskData();
    99→  const prefetchTaskById = usePrefetchTaskById();
   100→
   101→  // Check lineage depth lazily on hover
   102→  const checkLineageDepthOnHover = useCallback(async (variantId: string) => {
   103→    if (checkedLineageIdsRef.current.has(variantId)) return;
   104→    checkedLineageIdsRef.current.add(variantId);
   105→    try {
   106→      const depth = await getLineageDepth(variantId);
   107→      setVariantLineageDepth(prev => ({ ...prev, [variantId]: depth }));
   108→    } catch {
   109→      setVariantLineageDepth(prev => ({ ...prev, [variantId]: 0 }));
   110→    }
   111→  }, []);
   112→
   113→  const handleVariantMouseEnter = useCallback((variant: GenerationVariant) => {
   114→    if (isMobile) return;
   115→    checkLineageDepthOnHover(variant.id);
   116→
   117→    const variantParams = variant.params;
   118→    const validSourceTaskId = getSourceTaskId(variantParams);
   119→
   120→    if (validSourceTaskId) {
   121→      console.log('[VariantPrefetch] Prefetching source task:', validSourceTaskId.substring(0, 8), 'for variant:', variant.id.substring(0, 8));
   122→      prefetchTaskById(validSourceTaskId);
   123→    } else {
   124→      console.log('[VariantPrefetch] No source_task_id, prefetching via generation:', variant.generation_id.substring(0, 8));
   125→      prefetchTaskData(variant.generation_id);
   126→    }
   127→  }, [isMobile, prefetchTaskData, prefetchTaskById, checkLineageDepthOnHover]);
   128→
   129→  // Calculate variant relationships
   130→  const { parentVariants, childVariants, relationshipMap } = useMemo(() => {
   131→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] Computing relationships:');
   132→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] variantsCount:', variants.length);
   133→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] activeVariantId:', activeVariantId);
   134→
   135→    const parents = new Set<string>();
   136→    const children = new Set<string>();
   137→    const relMap: Record<string, { isParent: boolean; isChild: boolean }> = {};
   138→
   139→    variants.forEach(variant => {
   140→      relMap[variant.id] = { isParent: false, isChild: false };
   141→    });
   142→
   143→    const activeVar = variants.find(variant => variant.id === activeVariantId);
   144→    if (!activeVar) {
   145→      console.log('[VariantRelationship] No active variant found');
   146→      return { parentVariants: parents, childVariants: children, relationshipMap: relMap };
   147→    }
   148→
   149→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] Active variant:');
   150→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] - id:', activeVar.id);
   151→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] - variant_type:', activeVar.variant_type);
   152→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] - params:', JSON.stringify(activeVar.params));
   153→
   154→    const activeSourceVariantId = activeVar.params?.source_variant_id as string | undefined;
   155→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] Active variant source_variant_id:', activeSourceVariantId);
   156→
   157→    if (activeSourceVariantId) {
   158→      const parentVariant = variants.find(variant => variant.id === activeSourceVariantId);
   159→      if (parentVariant) {
   160→        console.log('[VariantRelationship] Found parent variant:', parentVariant.id);
   161→        parents.add(parentVariant.id);
   162→        relMap[parentVariant.id].isParent = true;
   163→      } else {
   164→        console.log('[VariantRelationship] Parent variant not in variants list');
   165→      }
   166→    }
   167→
   168→    variants.forEach(variant => {
   169→      const sourceId = variant.params?.source_variant_id as string | undefined;
   170→      if (sourceId) {
   171→        console.log('[VariantRelationship] Variant', variant.id.substring(0, 8), 'has source_variant_id:', sourceId);
   172→      }
   173→      if (sourceId === activeVariantId) {
   174→        console.log('[VariantRelationship] Found child variant:', variant.id);
   175→        children.add(variant.id);
   176→        relMap[variant.id].isChild = true;
   177→      }
   178→    });
   179→
   180→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] Result:');
   181→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] - parentCount:', parents.size);
   182→    console.log('[VariantRelationship] - childCount:', children.size);
   183→
   184→    return { parentVariants: parents, childVariants: children, relationshipMap: relMap };
   185→  }, [variants, activeVariantId]);
   186→
   187→  // Sort variants with primary first
   188→  const sortedVariants = useMemo(() => {
   189→    return [...variants].sort((a, b) => {
   190→      if (a.is_primary && !b.is_primary) return -1;
   191→      if (!a.is_primary && b.is_primary) return 1;
   192→      return 0;
   193→    });
   194→  }, [variants]);
   195→
   196→  // Filter variants based on relationship filter
   197→  const filteredVariants = useMemo(() => {
   198→    if (relationshipFilter === 'all') return sortedVariants;
   199→    if (relationshipFilter === 'parents') {
   200→      return sortedVariants.filter(variant => parentVariants.has(variant.id) || variant.id === activeVariantId);
   201→    }
   202→    if (relationshipFilter === 'children') {
   203→      return sortedVariants.filter(variant => childVariants.has(variant.id) || variant.id === activeVariantId);
   204→    }
   205→    if (relationshipFilter === 'starred') {
   206→      return sortedVariants.filter(variant => variant.starred || variant.id === activeVariantId);
   207→    }
   208→    return sortedVariants;
   209→  }, [sortedVariants, relationshipFilter, parentVariants, childVariants, activeVariantId]);
   210→
   211→  // Reset page when filter changes
   212→  React.useEffect(() => {
   213→    setCurrentPage(0);
   214→  }, [relationshipFilter]);
   215→
   216→  const hasRelationships = parentVariants.size > 0 || childVariants.size > 0;
   217→  const showFilterRow = hasRelationships || starredCount > 0;
   218→
   219→  // Don't show if no variants at all
   220→  if (!isLoading && variants.length === 0) {
   221→    return null;
   222→  }
   223→
   224→  const activeVariant = variants.find(variant => variant.id === activeVariantId);
   225→  const isViewingNonPrimary = activeVariant && !activeVariant.is_primary;
   226→
   227→  const handleMakePrimary = async () => {
   228→    if (!activeVariantId || !onMakePrimary) return;
   229→    setIsMakingPrimary(true);
   230→    try {
   231→      await onMakePrimary(activeVariantId);
   232→    } finally {
   233→      setIsMakingPrimary(false);
   234→    }
   235→  };
   236→
   237→  const handlePromoteToGeneration = async () => {
   238→    if (!activeVariantId || !onPromoteToGeneration) return;
```

## Summary

I found the issue with the VariantSelector visibility. There are **different visibility conditions** between InfoPanel.tsx and EditPanelLayout.tsx, and within the VariantSelector component itself.

### Key Findings:

#### 1. **InfoPanel.tsx (lines 265-292)** - Renders VariantSelector
```tsx

};

// At line 105:
const hasVariants = variants && variants.length >= 1;
```
**Condition:** Shows VariantSelector if `variants.length >= 1`

---

#### 2. **EditPanelLayout.tsx (lines 229-248)** - Also renders VariantSelector
```tsx
{hasVariants && (
  <div ref={variantsSectionRef} className={...}>
    <VariantSelector {...props} />
  </div>
)}

// At line 86:
const hasVariants = variants && variants.length >= 1 && onVariantSelect;
```

---

#### 3. **VariantSelector/index.tsx (lines 219-222)** - Internal visibility check
```tsx

if (!isLoading && variants.length === 0) {
  return null;
}
```
**Condition:** Returns null if no variants and not loading

---

### The Inconsistency Found:

**InfoPanel.tsx** at line 210 also shows a VariantBadge with a condition:
```tsx
} else if (variants && variants.length > 1 && unviewedVariantCount > 0) ? (
  <VariantBadge ... />
) : null}
```

This badge shows only when `variants.length > 1`, but the VariantSelector below renders when `variants.length >= 1`.

---

### Root Cause Analysis:

The **VariantSelector component itself** (line 220 in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/VariantSelector/index.tsx`) has this logic:

```tsx
if (!isLoading && variants.length === 0) {
  return null;
}
```

This should theoretically hide the component when there's only 1 variant for both images and videos. However:

1. **Images** - The VariantSelector will still render (and not return null) because `variants.length === 1` is not `=== 0`
2. **Videos** - Same behavior

The issue is that **VariantSelector doesn't have explicit logic to hide itself when `variants.length === 1`**. The component only returns null when `variants.length === 0`.

---

### File Locations:

- **VariantSelector renders in InfoPanel:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/components/InfoPanel.tsx` (lines 276-289)
- **VariantSelector renders in EditPanelLayout:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/components/EditPanelLayout.tsx` (lines 234-246)
- **VariantSelector main component:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/VariantSelector/index.tsx` (lines 220-222)

Both lightbox files pass the same `variants` and `onVariantSelect` props, so the visibility should be consistent. The fix would need to either:
1. Add a check in VariantSelector to hide when `variants.length === 1`
2. Add the condition in the rendering parents (InfoPanel and EditPanelLayout) to match each other
3. Investigate why videos might have different variant counts being passed
